### [136] [OrderFlowDiT: Transport-Regularized Diffusion Transformer for Probabilistic Limit-Order Supply Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22727)
*Jung Hun Phee,Tucker Balch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OrderFlowDiT——一个条件扩散 Transformer 模型，用于在给定当前订单簿与近期订单流时，联合预测未来五分钟限价单在价格上的非归一化买卖量场，并引入非平衡最优传输（UOT）辅助损失与全字段评估框架；相比仅用 MSE 训练，全字段传输成本降低 45.8%、传输能量分数降低 26.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以联合刻画价格位置、总量与预测不确定性：逐坐标扩散损失不显式考虑价格位移；将场归一化为单位质量会丢失总量信息；仅在某些汇总统计量上吻合也无法保证联合场分布的真实性。因此需要一个能同时保留价格几何与量级信息、并对场分布与不确定性统一评估的生成建模方法。

Method: 构建条件 Diffusion Transformer（OrderFlowDiT），对未归一化的买/卖 volume-at-price 场进行生成建模，并采用非平衡最优传输（UOT）辅助损失训练；同时设计全字段评估框架，将价格位置与总量敏感的传输成本、proper scoring rules 及校准诊断相结合。

Result: 在 30 只美股、1950 个验证条件上的主对比实验中，加入 UOT 使全字段传输成本相对纯 MSE 训练下降 45.8%，传输能量分数下降 26.1%，且两项指标在每只股票上均有改善。

Conclusion: 在生成建模中显式嵌入价格几何与总量信息具有明显收益，且应将场精度与预测不确定性联合评估；UOT 辅助损失在全部测试标的上表现稳健一致。

Abstract: We study the conditional distribution of limit-order volume submitted over the next five minutes, given the current order book and recent order flow. The task requires capturing price placement, total volume, and predictive uncertainty jointly. Coordinate-wise diffusion losses do not explicitly account for price displacement, while normalizing fields to unit mass removes information about total volume. Agreement on selected summary statistics also does not establish fidelity to the joint field distribution. We introduce OrderFlowDiT, a conditional Diffusion Transformer for unnormalized bid and ask volume-at-price fields, trained with an unbalanced optimal-transport (UOT) auxiliary loss. We also develop a full-field evaluation framework that combines a transport cost sensitive to price placement and total volume with proper scoring rules and calibration diagnostics. In the primary comparison on 1950 validation conditions across 30 U.S. equities, adding UOT reduces full-field transport cost by 45.8% and the transport energy score by 26.1% relative to training with mean squared error alone. Both measures improve for every stock. These findings support incorporating price geometry and volume into generative modeling, with field accuracy and predictive uncertainty evaluated together.

</details>


### [137] [Native Geometry vs. Linear Recoverability: How Alignment Changes Platonic Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22749)
*Angus Hoy,Austin Lutterbach,Jasper Chong,Aman Kumar,Michael J. Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用Legacy Survey与HSC匹配的天文嵌入，在五个模型族中比较"原生余弦几何"与"线性可恢复性"两种"相似"度量，发现Ridge对齐后模型规模与跨数据集邻域对应关系更强，但学到的映射远非旋转或均匀缩放，且无收敛到共享原生度量几何的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Platonic Representation Hypothesis（PRH）预测大模型会学到越来越相似的世界表征，但"相似"含义模糊：既可指未经学习对齐的原生余弦几何（余弦相似度诱导的最近邻结构），也可指线性可恢复性（监督线性映射揭示的对应关系）。本文旨在区分这两种"相似"，检验模型规模究竟在哪一种意义上提升表征相似性。

Method: 采用Legacy Survey与Hyper Suprime-Cam（HSC）匹配嵌入，覆盖五个模型族；对比原生余弦几何诱导的跨数据集邻域对应与Ridge监督线性对齐后的对应强度，并将两表征降至相同维度以排除维度效应；检验学到的映射是否为旋转或均匀缩放；并比较SAE、BSF稀疏特征与稠密嵌入在同等对齐下的表现。

Result: Ridge对齐相对原生余弦几何，增强了模型规模与跨数据集邻域对应之间的关系；该效应在全部五个模型族中出现，且在降至相同维度后依然存在，说明并非宽嵌入给对齐提供更多维度所致。学到的映射远离旋转或均匀缩放；SAE与BSF特征相对于稠密嵌入加同样对齐并无优势。

Conclusion: 模型规模更能提升线性可恢复性，而非原生余弦邻域对应；本文未能证明模型收敛到共享的原生度量几何，"相似"须明确区分度量的具体含义。

Abstract: Do larger models learn increasingly similar representations of the same world? The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) predicts that they should, but similar can mean native cosine geometry---nearest-neighbour structure induced by cosine similarity before any learned alignment---or linear recoverability: correspondence revealed by a supervised linear map. Using matched Legacy Survey and Hyper Suprime-Cam (HSC) embeddings across five model families, we find that Ridge alignment strengthens the relationship between model size and cross-dataset neighbourhood correspondence relative to native cosine geometry. The effect appears in all five families and persists when both representations are reduced to dimension, so it is not simply that wider embeddings give alignment more dimensions to work with. The learned maps remain far from rotations or uniform scaling, and Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) and Block-Sparse Featuriser (BSF) features give no advantage over the same alignment applied to dense embeddings. Model scale thus improves linear recoverability more clearly than native cosine-neighbourhood correspondence, without establishing convergence toward a shared native metric geometry.

</details>


### [138] [Refusal Is Not Robustness: Measuring Harmful Reachability in Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22754)
*Parv Kapoor,Teresa Fortes,Claudia Mamede,Rui Abreu,Jonathan Aldrich,Eunsuk Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出有界续写分析（BCA），将自回归生成建模为令牌前缀上的随机过程，用精确概率模型检验估计到达有害续写的有限时域概率，弥补ASR只评估采样输出的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越狱式LLM安全评估通常以攻击成功率（ASR）为指标，但ASR仅评估采样输出，无法反映生成过程本身：一次采样得到的拒答可能与大量潜在有害生成路径并存，隐藏风险被掩盖。

Method: BCA将自回归生成建模为令牌前缀上的随机过程，构建有界续写树，用语义危害评估器标注终止续写，并借助精确概率模型检验估计有限时域内到达有害续写的概率。

Result: 在4个指令微调LLM和100个JailbreakBench行为上，BCA能有效量化隐藏的有害行为，并可区分ASR相同但隐藏风险不同的模型，为红队工作定位高风险区域提供有用信号。

Conclusion: BCA作为ASR的补充指标，度量采样安全回答之下仍可访问的有害行为，有助于模型区分与红队资源分配。

Abstract: LLM safety evaluations via jailbreaking typically index on attack success rate (ASR): whether a generated response violates safeguards. However, ASR only evaluates sampled outputs and provides little information on the underlying generation process. Autoregressive language models define distributions over possible outputs, and a sampled refusal can coexist with substantial probability mass on harmful generation paths. We study this hidden structure through bounded continuation analysis (BCA), which models autoregressive generation as a stochastic process over token prefixes and computes the finite-horizon probability that generation reaches a harmful continuation. BCA constructs a bounded continuation tree, labels terminal continuations with a semantic harm evaluator, and estimates harmful reachability using exact probabilistic model checking. BCA complements ASR by measuring how much harmful behavior remains accessible beneath a sampled safe response. This makes it useful for distinguishing models that look identical under measured ASR values, and allocating red-team effort toward regions of the generation process with greater hidden risk. Across four instruction-tuned LLMs and 100 JailbreakBench behaviors, we show that BCA effectively quantifies hidden harmful behavior and provides a useful signal to guide red teaming.

</details>


### [139] [Acquisition Function-Guided Generation for Bayesian Optimization over Discrete Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22763)
*Tommaso Ancilli,Thomas Wedenig,Henry Moss,Robert Peharz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AFGG：将预训练生成模型与数据空间代理模型结合，通过扩散桥将采集函数倾斜分布嵌入以便采样，实现采集函数引导生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散空间贝叶斯优化面临无梯度和有效解稀疏的问题；潜空间BO虽规避此问题，却难以直接融入以原始离散空间表达的领域知识。

Method: 在数据空间直接定义代理模型以保留领域核等归纳偏置；不从离散设计空间最大化采集函数，而是从被采集函数倾斜的生成模型分布中采样候选；将该倾斜分布嵌入扩散桥以获得平滑的后验平均采集函数，从而恢复可用于采样的梯度。以类别扩散模型和自回归归一化流两种实例化实现。

Result: 在广泛的分子基准上，扩散模型变体与领先BO方法持平；流模型变体在多数情况下优于最先进方法，其余情况亦具强竞争力。

Conclusion: 数据空间代理加采集函数倾斜生成是离散BO的有效替代方案，避免潜空间中介，兼顾领域知识注入与采样可微性。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) over discrete spaces is challenging due to missing gradients and the sparsity of valid solutions. Latent-space BO sidesteps both issues by fitting a surrogate in the latent space of a generative model, which however substantially complicates the direct incorporation of domain knowledge that is naturally expressed in the original discrete space. In this work, we instead combine a pretrained generative model with a surrogate defined directly in data space, where inductive biases such as domain-specific kernels can be readily incorporated. Rather than maximizing the acquisition function over the discrete design space, our method samples candidates from the generative model under a distribution tilted by the acquisition function. To recover useful gradients for sampling, we embed this tilted distribution into a diffusion bridge that yields a smooth, posterior-averaged acquisition function. This leads to \emph{acquisition function guided generation} (AFGG), which we instantiate with a categorical diffusion model and an autoregressive normalizing flow. Across a broad suite of molecular benchmarks, the diffusion model variant performs on par with leading BO methods, while the flow model variant outperforms state-of-the-art methods in most cases and remains highly competitive otherwise. Our implementation is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/afgg.

</details>


### [140] [Reusing Calibration Gains across Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22771)
*Xu Cao,Wenxuan Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究尺度校准的更新能否在同一预训练模型的不同表示间复用：提取分组增益直接安装到接收方权重或原生量化尺度上，无需接收方优化；Qwen3-1.7B FP/GPTQ INT3双向迁移保留76.9–90.5%的文本损失改进，跨安装保留98.6–99.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尺度校准通常只针对单一模型表示学习一次更新，尚不清楚该更新中有多少可被同一预训练模型的另一种表示复用，这一问题的答案既关系到校准成本的摊销，也关系到接收方专用训练剩余价值的大小。

Method: 提出迁移流程：从源表示中提取正向的分组增益，直接安装到接收方权重或原生量化尺度中，接收方侧不做任何优化；在量化接收方上保持整数码、零点与存储布局不变。在Qwen3-1.7B的浮点/GPTQ INT3配对上进行双向迁移，并在未参与项目开发的新WikiText与C4队列上评估，同时用投影内增益值打乱作对照，并以Llama-3.2-3B作控制实验。

Result: 双向迁移在新WikiText与C4队列上保留了接收方专用校准所得文本损失改进的76.9–90.5%；在每个投影内打乱相同增益值会使两个接收方均变差，说明迁移后学习到的分组归属仍然重要。Llama-3.2-3B控制中四个已校准投影的原生张量与初始值完全一致、其余九处投影发生变化；跨安装保留98.6–99.3%的文本校准收益，同时每个接收方对其自身增益存在微小但可分辨的偏好。

Conclusion: 可复用的校准更新承载了大部分自适应能力，而接收方专用训练仍带来可测量的额外收益；分组增益的结构化归属是迁移有效性的关键，该流程为量化模型表示的零优化校准复用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Scale calibration learns an update for one model representation. We study how much of that update can be reused by another representation of the same pretrained model. Our transfer procedure extracts positive group-wise gains and installs them in the recipient's weights or native quantization scales without recipient-side optimization. On a quantized recipient, it preserves integer codes, zero points and storage layout. For a Qwen3-1.7B floating-point/GPTQ INT3 pair, bidirectional transfer retains 76.9-90.5% of the text-loss improvement from recipient-specific calibration on fresh WikiText and C4 cohorts excluded from project development. Shuffling the same gain values within each projection worsens both recipients, showing that their learned group assignment matters after transfer. A Llama-3.2-3B control keeps the initial native tensors at all four calibrated projections identical and changes nine projections elsewhere. Cross-installation retains 98.6-99.3% of the text-calibration benefit, alongside a small, resolved preference for each recipient's own gain. These results quantify both the adaptation carried by a reusable calibration update and the remaining benefit of recipient-specific training.

</details>


### [141] [Next Token or Vector? Learning How Far to Decode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22788)
*Sai Sreenivas Chintha,Chidera Biringa,Rishab Sharma,Anusha Agrawal,Prachi Badarayani,Gemma Zhang,Nigel Boachie Kumankumah,Marco Ayala,Sulaiman Vesal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CADENCE是一种自适应解码方法，通过高斯预测头与学习型路由器联合决定"草拟什么"和"草拟多远"，在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上实现最高1.3倍于标准贪心解码的生成吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归解码每次仅前进一个token，即使后续多个token可以被同时预测也是如此，导致生成效率受限；而固定预测长度的多token草拟策略无法适配上下文的动态变化。

Method: 提出CADENCE（Context-Adaptive DEcoding）：(1) 高斯预测头将未来片段表示为连续隐向量；(2) 共享解码器将选中的隐向量并行转换为token预测；(3) 学习型路由器依据模型自身隐状态与预测的高斯参数选择预测长度，实现生成粒度的上下文自适应，并保留单token生成作为回退；(4) 主干模型对每个候选续写进行验证，仅保留与贪心预测一致的前缀。

Result: 在Alpaca、GSM8K和HumanEval上使用Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct的实验表明：路由器所选位置的草拟接受率高于在所有位置统一使用相应预测长度的做法；CADENCE相较标准贪心解码最高提升1.3倍生成吞吐。

Conclusion: 动态、上下文自适应的预测长度选择能提升投机式多token生成的接受率与整体吞吐，验证了"学什么草拟、草拟多远"联合建模的有效性。

Abstract: Standard autoregressive decoding advances one token at a time, even when several upcoming tokens can be predicted together. We propose CADENCE (Context-Adaptive DEcoding), a generation method that learns both what to draft and how far to draft. Gaussian prediction heads represent future spans as continuous latent vectors, and a shared decoder converts the selected vector into token predictions in parallel. A learned router selects the prediction length from the model's own hidden states and predicted Gaussian parameters, adapting generation granularity to the current context with single-token generation as a fallback. The backbone verifies each proposed continuation and retains the prefix that matches its greedy predictions. Experiments with Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct on Alpaca, GSM8K, and HumanEval show that router-selected positions yield higher draft acceptance than applying the corresponding prediction length everywhere. CADENCE achieves up to 1.3x higher generation throughput than standard greedy decoding.

</details>


### [142] [Ori: Drift-Aware Streaming $k$-PCA for Very Low-Rank LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22794)
*Xinyu Luo,Jincheng Zhou,Mengbo Wang,Zilin Shen,Nadia Atallah Lanman,Petros Drineas,Bruno Ribeiro,Brian Bullins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出低秩优化器 Ori，利用流式 k-PCA（受 Oja++ 启发）在线高频跟踪 LLM 梯度子空间的漂移方向，在严格低秩约束下取得 SOTA 精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以 GaLore 为代表的低秩 LLM 优化器通过梯度子空间投影实现显存高效训练，但现有方法要么按固定间隔重算子空间，要么做事后适配以追赶移动的子空间。作者在实践中发现梯度子空间是以缓慢、稳定的方式随时间漂移，因此希望显式建模这种漂移。

Method: Ori 采用受 Oja++ 启发的流式 k-PCA 算法，每步开销低，从而支持高频、在线的子空间跟踪，实时捕获变化的主梯度方向，并把更新映射回全参数空间。

Result: 在 1B LLaMA 预训练任务上，将 SOTA 低秩优化器与全秩优化器的性能差距最多缩小 69.3%；在 Roberta-Base 微调的 16 个任务场景中有 10 个取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: Ori 将梯度投影类优化器的性能与效率边界进一步推进，缩小了 LLM 低秩训练与全秩训练之间的差距。

Abstract: A prominent family of low-rank LLM optimizers, pioneered by GaLore, performs gradient-subspace projection: at each step, they project the gradient into a low-dimensional subspace and map the update back to the full model. Most existing work either recomputes the gradient subspace at certain intervals, or performs post-hoc adaptation to catch up to the moving subspace. In this work, we explicitly identify in practice the slow, stable manner in which LLM gradient subspaces drift across time. To this end, we propose Ori, a novel low-rank optimizer for training LLMs that captures the shifting principal gradient directions over time to achieve state-of-the-art accuracy under stringent low-rank constraints. Ori employs a streaming $k$-PCA algorithm inspired by Oja++, whose low per-step cost enables high-frequency, on-the-fly subspace tracking. In practice, Ori reduces the performance gap of the state-of-the-art low-rank optimizers to full-rank optimizers by up to 69.3\% on a 1B LLaMA pretraining task, and achieves the best performance on 10 out of 16 task scenarios in Roberta-Base fine-tuning. Together, these contributions allow Ori to further push the performance and efficiency frontiers of gradient-projecting optimizers, narrowing the gap to full-rank training for LLMs. Code can be found at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Ori-Submission-2132.

</details>


### [143] [Partial-Revelation Panel Forecasting: Predictive Information Gain, Allocation, and Coarsening-Consistent Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22831)
*Bin Wen,Dai-Qiao Zhang,Tien-Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究"部分揭示式面板预测"（partial-revelation panel forecasting）：给定上一轮对话n个标注者中k个的投票，预测下一轮面板的完整计数分布，并给出信息论分解与粗化一致的算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分"跨轮预测信息"与"同项标注价值"，刻画随着额外揭示一个投票所带来的人群价值递减（V(k)-V(k+1)），并解决冷启动/raters数迁移下的计数分布预测。

Method: 在嵌套揭示机制下证明V(k)-V(k+1)=I(Y;R_{k+1}|X_k)（外加模型拟合的相邻超额风险差）；用CCAR掩码给出可交换随机标注者解释；定义粗化一致的面板算子，含闭式Dirichlet-multinomial实现、学习的补全、raters计数迁移。

Result: 相对逐层查表，冷启动计数NLL差在IEMOCAP/M3ED/EmoWOZ为-0.422/-0.166/-0.014 nats；留出标注者实验显示第1、2个额外投票使NLL降低0.583/0.221、0.578/0.184、0.180/0.069 nats；预算为1时目标无关分配在IEMOCAP/M3ED回收83–93%的回溯特征类headroom，EmoWOZ无提升。

Conclusion: 跨轮与同项收益不可互换；CCAR是受控干预而非真实到达过程；一致性可审计但不保证预测提升（逐层边缘化可优于共享表）；增益有限且依语料而定，存在若干微小或为零的部分揭示差异。

Abstract: We study partial-revelation panel forecasting: given $k$ of $n$ annotator votes on the previous dialogue turn, predict the full count distribution of the next turn's panel. This cross-turn predictive information differs from same-item annotation value. Under any nested revelation mechanism, the population decrement satisfies $V(k)-V(k+1)=I(Y;R_{k+1}\mid X_k)$; a fitted model also contributes adjacent excess-risk differences. Coarsened-completely-at-random (CCAR) masking supplies the exchangeable random-rater interpretation used in our operators. We define coarsening-consistent panel operators with a closed-form Dirichlet-multinomial realization, learned completion, and rater-count transfer. Against per-level lookup, cold-start count negative log-likelihood differences are -0.422/-0.166/-0.014 nats on IEMOCAP/M3ED/EmoWOZ. A held-out rater experiment measures same-item value: first and second additional votes reduce NLL by 0.583/0.221, 0.578/0.184, and 0.180/0.069 nats, respectively. Cross-turn and same-item gains are not interchangeable, and CCAR masks are controlled interventions rather than observed arrival processes. Consistency is auditable but does not guarantee predictive improvement; per-level marginalization can outperform a shared table. Target-free allocation beats uniform only where row-level signal is predictable: at budget one it recovers 83--93% of retrospective feature class headroom on IEMOCAP/M3ED but does not improve EmoWOZ. Gains are modest and corpus-dependent, with several small or null partial-revelation differences. Code and aggregate result artifacts accompany the preprint.

</details>


### [144] [Benchmarking and Enhancing Agentic Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22846)
*Pingchuan Ma,Rui Ding,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Heuresis 基准，用匿名模拟数据集剥离变量名泄漏与算法选择效应，从而考察 LLM agent 是否真正"读数据"做因果发现，并发现最强模型能在所有六个数据族上超越最优基线算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现基准存在两个混淆因素：（1）变量名带有语义信息，仅给出 Tübingen cause-effect pairs 的变量名，LLM 就能取得 99% 的方向准确率；（2）数据过于干净，使得"换一个更好的算法"即可弥补大部分差距。因此无法判断 agent 是在真正审视数据、定位算法失效之处并通过额外检验加以修正，还是仅在调用算法与利用名称先验。

Method: 构建 Heuresis：匿名化模拟数据集，机制覆盖线性高斯到重尾分布等多个数据族。对每个数据族，将"更优既有算法可获得的增益"与"只有深入观察数据才能获得的增益"分离量化，并逐条追踪 agent 修改的每一条边。测试对象包括传统因果发现算法、一个路由器和两个因果发现基础模型；再用 agent harness 实例化基准，接入三个 LLM（GPT-6.1 Sol、Claude Opus 5.5、Qwen 3.8 Flash）。

Result: 每个数据族都留有 gap，任何算法、路由器与两个因果发现基础模型均无法弥合，故超过最优算法分数只能来自对数据的使用。GPT-6.1 Sol 在全部六个数据族上超过最优基线算法，最难族上邻接 F1 提升 14%；Claude Opus 5.5 在该族提升 7.4%。在两个最难族上，两个 agent 恢复的真实边中有三分之一不出现在任何算法的输出中。

Conclusion: 通过去除变量名先验并控制算法选择效应，Heuresis 提供了可归因的因果发现评测；结果表明强 LLM agent 具备超出"选算法"的数据驱动发现能力。代码与数据将公开以促进后续研究。

Abstract: LLM agents can now run causal discovery algorithms on a dataset and report what comes out. Whether they do more than that, such as noticing where an algorithm went wrong and fixing it with further tests, is hard to tell from current benchmarks. Most of them give the variables meaningful names, and an LLM shown only the names of the Tübingen cause-effect pairs already gets 99% of the directions right. With the names removed, the data are usually clean enough that picking a better algorithm than PC closes most of the gap. We introduce Heuresis, a benchmark of anonymous simulated datasets whose mechanisms range from linear Gaussian to heavy-tailed. For each family of datasets it separates what a better existing algorithm can gain from what only a closer look at the data can gain, and it traces every edge an agent changes. Every family leaves a gap that none of the algorithms, the router and the two causal-discovery foundation models we ran can close, so a score above the best chosen algorithm has to come from using the data. We instantiate the benchmark with an agent harness and three LLMs, GPT-6.1 Sol, Claude Opus 5.5 and Qwen 3.8 Flash. GPT-6.1 Sol surpasses the best chosen algorithm on all six families, by 14% adjacency F1 on the most difficult one, and Claude Opus 5.5 does so by 7.4% there. A third of the true edges the two agents restore on the two hardest families appear in no algorithm's output. The code and data will be publicly released to facilitate future work.

</details>


### [145] [Learning to Be Guided: Shaping Latent Geometry for Classifier-Free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22885)
*Haoming Liu,Junhao Zhu,Shaoyi Zheng,Yuanhe Guo,Jinnuo Liu,Shenji Wan,Hongyi Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究潜空间中的"类别几何"（class geometry）如何影响无分类器引导（CFG）的生成表现，理论证明在总方差固定时将方差从类内转移到类间可提升噪声下类可分性，并提出原型正则化（PR）以实现CFG兼容的表示学习，在ImageNet上将引导gFID从3.31降至2.28。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜扩散模型致力于学习更易被扩散建模的潜空间，但在CFG下生成效果还取决于条件在潜空间中的排列方式（类别几何），这一因素此前被忽视，作者希望探究何种类别几何能与CFG良好配合。

Method: 构建高斯类别模型进行理论分析：证明在总方差固定时，将方差由类内转向类间可使类别在噪声下更可区分；推导带噪后验目标，其逐样本下降方向正比于oracle CFG修正量。据此提出原型正则化（PR）——一种对空间池化后的潜表示施加余弦原型分类器的轻量代理目标。

Result: 在受控训练预算下（VAE 20轮、DiT 80轮），在LDM基线上加入PR使ImageNet引导gFID由3.31降至2.28，优于DINOv3对齐的VA-VAE（2.44），且额外开销可忽略；PR还可推广到聚类伪标签、连续文本嵌入条件与视频潜变量。

Conclusion: 类别几何是引导生成的关键因素，本研究为CFG兼容的表示学习迈出了第一步。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is a standard component of conditional diffusion models, and guided generation performance has become the \textit{de facto} measure of state-of-the-art models. Recent work on latent diffusion models seeks to learn latent spaces that are easier to model with diffusion. However, under CFG, generation additionally depends on how conditions are arranged in the latent space. We study this arrangement under discrete class conditions, termed \emph{class geometry}, and investigate what class geometry allows a latent space to work well with CFG. Under a Gaussian class model, we show that shifting variance from intra- to inter-class at fixed total variance makes classes more distinguishable under noise, and derive a noisy posterior objective whose per-sample descent direction is proportional to the oracle CFG correction. Based on the analysis, we propose Prototype Regularization (PR), a lightweight surrogate for the intractable noisy posterior objective, which applies a cosine prototype classifier to spatially pooled latents. With a controlled training budget (20 epochs for VAE, 80 epochs for DiT), adding PR to an LDM baseline reduces guided gFID on ImageNet from 3.31 to 2.28, surpassing a DINOv3-aligned VA-VAE (2.44) with negligible overhead. We further demonstrate that PR generalizes to pseudo-labels derived from clustering, continuous text-embedding conditions, and video latents. Together, our findings highlight class geometry as a critical factor in guided generation and take a first step toward CFG-compatible representation learning. Our project page is available at: https://maps-research.github.io/cfg-geometry/.

</details>


### [146] [Batched Contextual Reinforcement II: Does RLVR Need Long Rollout Windows?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22887)
*Bangji Yang,Hongbo Ma,Ruihan Guo,Jiajun Fan,Xi Zhu,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出并发约束强化学习（CCR），将三道独立可验证的题目放入同一有限长度训练补全中，在短窗口下保留正确性奖励信号并大幅降低每步训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 长 rollout 窗口成本高，但简单缩短窗口会导致所有单题 rollout 在给出答案前被截断，组内相对准确率优势全部消失，正确性信号丢失。作者希望在不依赖长窗口的前提下保留奖励信号。

Method: 作为 Batched Contextual Reinforcement（BCR）的技术延续，CCR 在一个受上限约束的训练补全中放置三个独立可验证问题：截断前完成的正确答案仍计分，共享预算自然鼓励更短的有用推理，无需显式长度惩罚；并从条件奖励方差与更新方差角度给出理论分析。实验在 4B 与 8B 数学推理模型上进行。

Result: 与 32k 窗口的单题 GRPO 相比，8,000 token 窗口的 CCR 将每步训练 token 数从 975,464 降至 174,957（4B）与从 793,832 降至 165,788（8B），分别节省 82.1% 与 79.1%。8B 训练轨迹显示准确率奖励上升的同时补全长度缩短；单题评测为推理质量保持提供支持性证据。

Conclusion: 保留奖励的训练结构是短窗口 RLVR 降低每步成本的可行途径。

Abstract: Long rollout windows make reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) expensive, yet simply shortening them can remove the correctness signal: when every single-problem rollout truncates before an answer, group-relative accuracy advantages vanish. As a technical continuation of Batched Contextual Reinforcement (BCR), we study how concurrent rewards relax this dependence on long windows. Concurrent Constraint Reinforcement (CCR) places three independently verifiable problems in one capped training completion. Correct answers completed before truncation retain credit, while a shared budget encourages shorter useful reasoning without an explicit length penalty. We connect this mechanism to conditional reward- and update-variance analyses and study 4B and 8B mathematical reasoning models. Against single-problem GRPO with a 32k window, CCR with an 8,000-token window reduces tokens per training step from 975,464 to 174,957 on 4B and from 793,832 to 165,788 on 8B, saving 82.1% and 79.1%, respectively. An 8B training trace shows increasing accuracy reward alongside shorter completions. Single-problem evaluations provide supporting evidence of retained reasoning quality. These results identify reward-preserving training structure as a practical route to lower per-step cost in short-window RLVR.

</details>


### [147] [What Accuracy Hides In Learned Transformer Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22891)
*Jonathan Lee,Narmeen Oozeer,Philip Quirke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文主张超越token准确率，通过程序审查（program inspection）与失败分析来评估和改进Transformer Programs（TP），发现并修复了被采样测试掩盖的失效，并通过针对性补充训练数据提升了序列级准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TP能将学到的计算抽取为可读的Python代码，但token级准确率可能掩盖"完整答案错误"的情况。作者希望探究程序审查所能提供的、超出准确率分数的洞察，并用其指导训练与数据改进。

Method: 在九个算法任务上评估TP：对比token准确率与完整输出序列准确率，检查采样测试漏掉的失效；基于程序审查与失败分析改进训练数据；并分析TP训练流程与受限操作带来的约束。

Result: 多项任务在序列准确率上获得提升。计数任务中，某程序在采样测试上达100%，但在一个符合训练符号与长度的输入上失败——程序计数正确，而所需答案缺失于输出标签；加入更大计数的训练样例后，重训练模型在有限域上通过穷举评估。频率排序任务中，审查揭示了正确计数如何导致错误答案（数据改动前后均如此）。TP训练过程还会在训练后期出现准确率下降；参考构造表明组合受限操作可能需要很多层或大型查找表。

Conclusion: 程序审查提供了准确率之外的诊断价值，可指导数据修复与训练；但TP的训练动态与受限操作也带来固有约束，需在评估与设计中予以考虑。

Abstract: Transformer Programs (TP) (Friedman et al., 2023) constrain a Transformer's architecture so that its learned computation can be extracted as readable Python code. TP expose learned computations, but token accuracy can hide incorrect complete answers. In this work, we examine how program inspection provides insights beyond accuracy scores and helps guide training. We first evaluated TP on nine algorithmic tasks. We compared token accuracy with accuracy on complete output sequences and checked for failures missed by sampled tests. Further, we assessed training data improvements informed by program inspection and failure analysis, finding gains in sequence accuracy on several tasks. We examined a counting task where a program achieved 100% accuracy on sampled test sequences but failed on another input using the symbols and lengths allowed during training. The program counted correctly, but the required answer was missing from its output labels. After adding training examples with larger counts, the retrained models passed exhaustive evaluation over the bounded domain. In the frequency ranking task, inspection showed how correct counts contributed to incorrect answers in programs trained before and after changes to the training data. Finally, we studied constraints imposed by TP training and operations. The TP training process can lead to accuracy losses late in training. Our reference constructions also show how composing the restricted TP operations can use many layers or large lookup tables.

</details>


### [148] [A Feature Selection Layer Approach for Multi-Class Classification on Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22898)
*João Pedro Silveira e Silva,Márcio Dorn,Bruno Iochins Grisci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出特征选择层（Feature Selection Layer）的多种扩展，实现神经网络中标签特定的嵌入式特征加权，无需额外后处理即可识别全局与标签特有关键特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型缺乏可解释性限制其在科学与商业中的应用；现有事后（post hoc）特征加权方法已较成熟，但嵌入式方法难以刻画特征与标签间的细粒度关系，尤其缺少标签特定（label-specific）相关性建模能力。

Method: 在嵌入式特征加权机制——特征选择层基础上提出多种扩展，通过大量实验筛选最优扩展，并与既有特征加权方法进行基准对比，利用训练后网络内部动力学提取特征对特定标签预测的贡献。

Result: 所提方法在识别全局相关特征上与现有方法持平，在捕捉标签特定相关性方面优于现有方法；且仅需训练过程即可获得特征—标签贡献解释，无需额外后处理。

Conclusion: 嵌入式特征加权可有效兼顾全局与标签特定解释性，为神经网络提供了一种低成本、可训练内嵌的可解释性方案。

Abstract: In the age of data-driven decision-making, deep learning models have become powerful tools for uncovering patterns and making predictions that influence many aspects of society. However, their broader adoption in scientific and business applications is often limited by a lack of interpretability. Feature weighting methods, which assess the relevance of individual features, are among the most widely studied approaches for improving interpretability. While post hoc techniques have advanced significantly, especially in modeling nuanced relationships between features and labels in multiclass problems, embedded methods typically fall short in this regard. Embedded techniques integrate feature relevance learning directly into the model training process, but they are often unable to capture label-specific relationships. To address the limitation, we propose multiple extensions to the Feature Selection Layer, an embedded feature weighting mechanism for neural networks. Through extensive experimentation, we identify the most effective extension and benchmark it against established feature weighting methods. Our results show that the proposed method matches existing techniques in identifying globally relevant features and outperforms them in capturing label-specific relevance. By leveraging the internal dynamics of the trained neural network, it reveals how individual features contribute to the prediction of specific labels, requiring no post-processing beyond the training process.

</details>


### [149] [Scalable Levenberg-Marquardt Training Framework for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22925)
*Xianghao Wang,Johan Barthélemy,John Taylor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeMA 提出一种面向多 GPU 平台的分布式 Levenberg-Marquardt 算法，通过 LMA 的精确对偶形式与 Jacobian 分片并行即时计算，将 LMA 的内存开销从参数维度的二次方降为批量大小的二次方，使十亿参数级模型的精确 LMA 训练成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LMA 在小规模网络上收敛快、对结构鲁棒，优于 Adam 等一阶优化器，但其内存与计算开销随 Jacobian 的样本数×参数数维度增长而急剧膨胀，难以用于大规模网络。现有工作或用预处理共轭梯度（PCG）近似求解（需大量网络传播与任务专用预条件子），或将优化限定于特定任务/架构，因此 LMA 仍无法用于通用大规模网络训练。

Method: 提出 LeMA 分布式 LMA 算法，包含两项设计：一是 LMA 的精确对偶形式，消除内存对参数维度的依赖；二是 Jacobian 分片策略，在多 GPU 上并行、即时地计算 Jacobian 分片，避免物化完整 Jacobian。

Result: 内存占用由参数数量的二次方降为批量大小的二次方，使十亿参数模型的精确 LMA 步（无需 PCG 近似）可行。在 8-GPU 节点上对参数量 1,000,022,632 的卷积网络，相较单 GPU 取得 7.97 倍加速，强扩展效率达 99.7%。

Conclusion: LeMA 通过精确对偶形式与 Jacobian 分片并行，克服了 LMA 在大规模深度网络训练中的内存与计算瓶颈，实现了高可扩展性的分布式精确二阶优化，为十亿参数级模型应用 LMA 提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The Levenberg-Marquardt (LMA) algorithm is a widely used nonlinear least-squares solver known for its rapid convergence. For small-scale neural networks, LMA provides better convergence and architectural robustness than first-order optimizers like Adam. However, LMA imposes prohibitive memory and computational costs. These costs are primarily from the Jacobian matrix, whose dimensions scale with both the number of samples and parameters. This bottleneck prevents its use in large-scale neural networks. Recent efforts scale LMA using preconditioned conjugate gradient (PCG) solvers to avoid materializing the Jacobian. However, PCG only approximates the solution, requiring many network propagations and a task-specific preconditioner. Other studies optimize LMA to reduce costs, but they restrict their methods to specific tasks or network architectures. As a result, LMA remains impractical for training general large-scale neural networks. In this paper, we propose LeMA, a distributed LMA algorithm for large-scale neural network training on multi-GPU platforms. Our contributions are twofold: First, an exact dual formulation of LMA removes the memory dependence on the parameter dimension. Second, a Jacobian sharding strategy computes Jacobian shards on the fly and in parallel to avoid materializing the full Jacobian. For large-scale neural networks, LeMA reduces the memory footprint of LMA from quadratic in the number of parameters to quadratic in the batch size. This makes exact LMA steps feasible for billion-parameter models without approximate PCG solves. Furthermore, for a convolutional neural network with 1,000,022,632 parameters on an 8-GPU node, LeMA achieves a 7.97x speedup over a single GPU, corresponding to a 99.7% strong scaling efficiency.

</details>


### [150] [Large Diffusion Models Need More Than EMA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22945)
*Chuyu Hu,Huanran Chen,Shitong Shao,Yinpeng Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑扩散模型训练中"常数学习率+EMA"的默认范式，从理论与实证两方面比较LR衰减、Schedule-Free与EMA，提出有效优化需在方差缩减与持续优化间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模扩散模型普遍采用常数学习率加指数移动平均（EMA），而LLM训练中LR衰减与Schedule-Free优化已显示出更强性能，因此作者希望厘清EMA是否对扩散训练具有独特适配性，还是存在被低估的更强替代方案。

Method: 理论层面在标准非凸假设下分析常数步长轨迹平均的固有优化局限，并给出LR衰减与Schedule-Free的可消失平稳性保证；实证层面在大规模扩散模型上对比常数LR+EMA、LR衰减（含与EMA组合、不同衰减起始时刻）与Schedule-Free，考察固定评估目标、生成质量、参数（检查点）变异性与后期优化进展，并扩展到ReFL与生成器侧DMD。

Result: LR衰减与EMA收益互补，在多数测试起点上优于基线，但增益对衰减起始时机敏感；Schedule-Free无需调时机，在各训练阶段一致优于常数LR+EMA，固定评估目标更低且生成质量更好。三者均显著降低参数变异性：常数LR+EMA变异性相对较高；激进衰减+EMA检查点变异性最低但削弱持续优化；Schedule-Free在显著降低变异性的同时保留更多后期进展，平衡更佳。其收益可迁移至ReFL与DMD。

Conclusion: 扩散大模型的有效优化不能仅依赖参数平均，需在方差缩减与持续优化能力之间取得平衡；Schedule-Free因兼具稳定性与后期优化进展，是比常数LR+EMA更优且更少超参敏感的默认选择。

Abstract: Large-scale diffusion models are commonly trained with a constant learning rate (LR) and exponential moving average (EMA), despite the strong performance of LR decay and Schedule-Free optimization in training large language models. We study whether EMA is uniquely suited to diffusion training, or whether stronger alternatives have been underexplored. Our theory shows that averaging a constant-step trajectory has an inherent optimization limitation, whereas LR decay and Schedule-Free optimization admit vanishing stationarity guarantees under standard nonconvex assumptions. Empirically, LR decay and EMA provide complementary benefits: combining decay with EMA further improves the standard baseline at most tested starting points, although the gain remains sensitive to when decay begins. Schedule-Free avoids this timing requirement and consistently outperforms EMA with a constant LR across training stages, achieving both lower fixed-evaluation objectives and better generation quality. Our analysis further shows that all three strategies substantially reduce parameter variability, yet stronger stabilization is not always better: EMA with a constant LR retains comparatively higher parameter variability, while aggressive decay combined with EMA achieves the lowest checkpoint variability but also weakens continued optimization. Schedule-Free substantially reduces parameter variability while preserving more late-stage progress, providing a better balance between parameter stability and continued optimization. Finally, the benefits of Schedule-Free extend beyond pretraining to ReFL and generator-side DMD, demonstrating its transferability across distinct diffusion optimization settings. Overall, our results suggest that large diffusion models need more than parameter averaging alone: effective optimization requires balancing variance reduction with continued optimization.

</details>


### [151] [On the tradeoff between robustness and geometric accuracy in adversarial training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22955)
*Rachel Morris,Ryan Murray,Kerrek Stinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在非参数二分类中，证明了对抗分类器的几何精度（以到Bayes分类器的Hausdorff距离度量）与鲁棒性（以对抗预算ε编码）之间的最优定量估计：鲁棒分类器至多牺牲 O(√ε) 或 O(ε) 的精度，取决于Bayes分类器的正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作多聚焦于低复杂度假设类上鲁棒性与精度的不兼容性；本文意在说明高复杂度假设类能够同时容许鲁棒分类器与可精确量化的精度。

Method: 以到Bayes分类器的Hausdorff距离刻画几何精度，针对欧氏空间上任意范数定义的对抗扰动，给出精度损失随对抗预算ε变化的最优上界，并同时覆盖无限与有限数据设定。

Result: 证明鲁棒分类器的精度损失至多为 O(√ε) 或 O(ε)，具体阶数取决于底层Bayes分类器的正则性；该估计为最优。

Conclusion: 高复杂度假设类下鲁棒性与精度可兼容，且精度损失可被精确量化；结果对任意欧氏范数对抗、有限与无限数据均成立。

Abstract: For non-parametric binary classification in the presence of an adversary, we prove an optimal quantitative estimate for adversarial classifiers controlling geometric accuracy, as measured by the Hausdorff distance to the Bayes classifier, in terms or their robustness, as encoded by the adversarial budget $\varepsilon$. This rigorously shows that robust classifiers sacrifice at most $O(\sqrt{\varepsilon})$ or $O(\varepsilon)$ in accuracy, depending on the regularity of the underlying Bayes classifier. In contrast to prior work on the incompatibility of robustness and accuracy over low-complexity hypothesis classes, our work shows that high-complexity hypothesis classes allow for robust classifiers with precisely quantified accuracy. Our results apply for adversaries determined by any norm on Euclidean space and in both the infinite and finite data settings.

</details>


### [152] [Towards Hyperparameter Transfer for Differentially Private Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22976)
*Tianze Wang,Zhiqi Bu,Linjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DP-μP 框架，将 μP 的超参数零样本迁移扩展到差分隐私训练，通过信噪比（SNR）条件与谱缩放推导 DP-SGD/DP-Adam/DP-Muon 的学习率缩放规则，实现跨模型宽度的学习率零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: μP 在标准训练中可实现超参数零样本迁移，但在差分隐私（DP）下失效：DP 引入的噪声注入与逐样本梯度裁剪，使高维噪声与低秩梯度的谱性质存在本质差异，破坏了原有参数化假设；而大规模 DP 训练中的缩放律与超参数调优成本高昂。

Method: 从理论上分析梯度裁剪、噪声注入与特征学习三者的相互作用，推导出支配逐层学习率缩放的 SNR 条件；结合所提出的谱缩放（spectral scaling）公式，给出 DP-SGD、DP-Adam 与 DP-Muon 三种优化器的具体缩放规则，形成 DP-μP 框架。

Result: 在 MLP、ViT、GPT-2 等多种架构上，DP-μP 实现学习率在不同模型宽度间的有效零样本迁移，性能与逐配置单独调参的基线持平或更优，同时显著降低计算开销。

Conclusion: DP 约束下 μP 的失效源于噪声与梯度的谱性质差异，需以 SNR 条件重构层间缩放；DP-μP 为隐私保护的大模型训练提供了可迁移、低成本的超参数设定方案。

Abstract: Scaling laws and hyperparameter tuning remain central challenges in training large-scale models, particularly under differential privacy (DP), where optimization is further complicated by noise injection and per-sample gradient clipping. While Maximal Update Parametrization ($μ$P) allows for zero-shot hyperparameter transfer in standard training, we show that it fails under DP constraints due to the distinct spectral properties of high-dimensional noise versus low-rank gradients. In this work, we introduce DP-$μ$P, a theoretically grounded framework that extends $μ$P to the privacy-preserving setting. By analyzing the interaction between gradient clipping, noise injection, and feature learning, we derive a Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR) condition that governs layer-wise learning rate scaling. Combining this with our proposed spectral scaling formulation, we provide specific scaling rules for DP-SGD, DP-Adam, and DP-Muon optimizers. Empirically, we demonstrate that DP-$μ$P enables effective zero-shot transfer of learning rates across model widths on diverse architectures (MLP, ViT, GPT-2), matching or exceeding the performance of individually tuned baselines while significantly reducing computational costs.

</details>


### [153] [TALLOP: Joint Optimization of Local Training and Communication Compression for Time-Constrained Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22996)
*Honoka Anada,Yafei Ou,Shinya Takamaeda-Yamazaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TALLOP，在每轮时间预算约束下联合优化本地训练工作量与通信压缩，以最小化联邦学习达到目标精度所需的墙钟时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构联邦学习中慢客户端拖累全局同步产生掉队者效应；已有按轮时间约束的自适应方法主要关注本地计算，而通信受限环境下通信同样成为瓶颈，需联合优化训练与通信，但固定轮时预算下的训练—通信权衡因模型性能同时依赖训练进度与压缩退化而难以确定。

Method: TALLOP 由两部分预测器组成：LENS 由早期小批量损失轨迹预测不同计算预算下可达的训练损失；CLEN 用压缩误差模型估计通信压缩带来的性能退化；两者结合后在给定轮时间约束下选择使压缩后损失最小的训练—通信资源分配。

Result: 在异构联邦学习环境中，TALLOP 的 time-to-accuracy 总体与仅优化本地训练的基线相当或更优；但相对仅训练自适应的增益依赖于具体设置。

Conclusion: 在每轮时间约束下显式建模训练损失与压缩退化并进行联合分配可有效缓解掉队者效应；未来需进一步刻画增益成立的条件与适用场景。

Abstract: Minimizing the wall-clock time required to reach a target accuracy is crucial in federated learning (FL), where many applications require frequent model updates as data distributions evolve over time. Nevertheless, in heterogeneous environments, slow clients with limited computational and communication capabilities can delay global synchronization, forcing faster clients to wait and increasing the overall training time. To mitigate this straggler effect, recent studies have introduced per-round time constraints and adapted client workloads within the available time. However, these approaches primarily focus on local computation, whereas communication can become a major bottleneck in communication-limited environments, motivating the joint optimization of training workload and communication compression. Although previous studies have explored such joint optimization, determining an effective training--communication balance under a fixed per-round time budget remains challenging, as the resulting model performance depends on both training progress and compression-induced degradation. We therefore propose TALLOP, which jointly optimizes the local training workload and communication compression under per-round time constraints. TALLOP predicts the training loss achievable under different computation budgets from the early-stage mini-batch loss trajectory (LENS), and estimates the degradation caused by communication compression using a compression-error model (CLEN). TALLOP combines these predictions to select the training-communication allocation that minimizes the post-compression loss under the given round time constraint. Experiments in heterogeneous FL environments show that TALLOP generally achieves competitive or improved time-to-accuracy compared with approaches that optimize only local training or neither, although the benefit over training-only adaptation depends on the setting.

</details>


### [154] [A Label Noise Robust Algorithm for Graph Data using Neighborhood Label Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22998)
*Pintu Kumar,Nandyala Hemachandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究GCN在标签噪声下的鲁棒性问题，从理论上证明标签噪声会平滑节点的邻域标签分布、提升其熵并削弱类间可分性，据此提出端到端的留一熵节点筛选框架LENS，通过衡量移除某节点后邻域熵的下降来识别噪声节点，并结合重标注机制保留有用特征信息，在节点分类精度上达到SOTA且速度比现有方法快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GCN在图结构数据上表现优异，但其成功高度依赖干净标签，而真实场景中该假设往往不成立。由于GCN的可学习性与节点的邻域结构紧密相关，理解标签噪声如何改变这一结构至关重要，现有噪声鲁棒方法在效果与效率上仍有不足。

Method: 1）理论分析：数学推导出标签噪声会平滑节点的邻域标签分布，使其熵升高、类间可分性显著下降；2）LENS框架：以"留一"思想度量移除某节点后邻域熵的减少量，据此识别噪声节点；3）重标注机制：对被判定为噪声的节点进行标签恢复而非直接丢弃，以保留有价值的特征信息；4）整体为端到端设计，计算开销极低。

Result: 大量实验表明，LENS在节点分类精度上达到当前最优（state-of-the-art），同时运行速度比现有噪声鲁棒方法快约一个数量级。

Conclusion: 标签噪声通过平滑邻域标签分布、提高熵来损害GCN的类间可分性；基于留一邻域熵下降的节点筛选配合重标注机制，可在极低端到端开销下高效识别并利用噪声节点，实现精度与效率的双重优势。

Abstract: Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs) have demonstrated exceptional performance on graph-structured data. However, their success heavily relies on the availability of clean labels, an assumption that often fails in real-world scenarios. The learnability of GCNs is intrinsically linked to the neighborhood structure of nodes, making it critical to understand how this structure changes in the presence of label noise. In this work, we investigate how the neighborhood label distribution of a node changes under label noise. We mathematically derive that label noise smooths this distribution, which inherently increases its entropy and severely decreases interclass distinguishability. To address this challenge, we propose Leave-one-out Entropy Node Screening (LENS), an end-to-end framework that identifies noisy nodes by measuring the reduction in neighborhood entropy when a specific node is removed. LENS further incorporates a relabeling mechanism to recover and preserve valuable feature information instead of entirely discarding the identified noisy nodes. Crucially, the end-to-end computational overhead of LENS is remarkably low. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our algorithm achieves state-of-the-art node classification accuracy while executing an order of magnitude faster than existing noise-robust approaches.

</details>


### [155] [A Minimal ReLU Classifier on Non-Linearly Separable Data: Convergence and Implicit Bias at and beyond the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23007)
*Quan Nguyen,Robin Yadav,Param Mody,Mark Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 分析二神经元ReLU网络在1D非线性可分数据上用logistic损失训练时的大学习率梯度下降（EoS）行为：在包含所有完美分类器的"活跃锥"内问题退化为逐段logistic回归，给出有限时间损失界、方向收敛到显式max-margin向量、正交分量收敛到非强凸势的唯一极小点，并证明catapult现象与自适应学习率调度达到比O(1/T)更快的O(1/(T log T))速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大学习率GD能优化神经网络但违反经典光滑性条件，而现有理论多局限于线性模型或线性可分数据，缺少对非线性模型、非线性可分数据的可解析刻画。

Method: 构造最小化设定：两神经元ReLU网络+logistic损失+1D非线性可分数据；识别参数空间中的活跃锥（含所有完美分类器），锥内问题等价于有效数据集随训练变化的逐段logistic回归；在此基础上分析常数学习率的有限时间损失界、参数方向与正交分量的极限行为，并设计使学习率趋于无穷且不离开活跃锥的自适应调度，另用MNIST验证并推广到更一般网络的局部收敛。

Result: 证明常数学习率下的有限时间损失界；GD方向收敛到显式max-margin向量，正交分量收敛到非强凸势的唯一极小点；建立catapult型结果——单次损失尖峰后即达指数小损失；所提自适应调度达到O(1/(T log T))的损失速率，优于标准O(1/T)；理论在更一般网络上给出局部收敛，并在MNIST上验证。

Conclusion: 该二神经元ReLU+1D非线性可分设定构成了一个可解析的神经网络模型，可用于研究超越线性预测器与可分数据的大步长与自适应步长下的EoS动力学。

Abstract: Gradient descent (GD) with large learning rates can successfully optimize neural networks even though classical smoothness-based learning rate conditions are violated. Yet theory for this regime largely covers linear models or linearly separable data. We analyze a minimal setting outside both regimes. Specifically, a two-neuron ReLU network trained with logistic loss on 1D non-linearly separable data. The objective is nonconvex and nonsmooth, and GD exhibits edge-of-stability (EoS) behavior. We identify an active cone in parameter space that contains every perfect classifier, where the problem becomes piecewise logistic regression with a varying effective dataset. Within this cone, we prove a finite-time loss bound for constant learning rates and show that GD converges in direction to an explicit max-margin vector, while the orthogonal component converges to the unique minimizer of a non-strongly convex potential. We further establish catapult results where exponentially small loss is reached after a single loss spike. Furthermore, we propose an adaptive schedule that lets learning rates grow to infinity without leaving the active cone. We prove that this schedule achieves a loss rate of $O(1/(T\log{T})$, which is faster than the standard $O(1/T)$ rate. Our theoretical results extend to local convergence on more general network and are validated on MNIST dataset. Overall, we show that this setup provides a tractable neural-network model for studying large and adaptive step sizes beyond linear predictors and separable data.

</details>


### [156] ["Deep lifts": Neural networks can generalize even with the dumbest optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23009)
*Sunil Madhow,Yu-Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究均匀随机插值参数（在宽W深D的ReLU网络中随机选取）的泛化性质，证明即使选择规则"无视"数据，它仍能以 ~O(wdWD/n) 的速率自适应泛化，而非受全假设类VC维控制，机制是ReLU网络的归纳偏置：小网络"见证"了指数级体积的等价插值参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典学习理论用VC维刻画插值解的泛化上界，无法解释过参数化网络为何能在"随机选择插值参数"这种看似无偏的规则下仍良好泛化。作者希望从参数空间的体积/几何视角解释这一现象，并说明其自适应速率是否依赖于数据真正可由更小网络解释的结构。

Method: 设定数据集可由更小的 w×d ReLU 网络解释；在权重有界 [-M,M] 的宽W深D网络中，从所有插值参数中均匀随机抽取 θ̂。通过名为"deep lifting"的证明技术，显式构造一大类与w×d见证网络近等价的W×D参数；再将可达体积与w×d见证网络的稳定性性质相联系，从而界定插值约束下"差泛化参数"所占体积，并推广到标签受扰动的场景。

Result: 均匀随机插值器以 ~O(wdWD/n) 的速率泛化，远优于由全W×D类VC维给出的界；该界由小网络规模（w,d）而非大网络（W,D）主导。基于deep lifting的构造把近似论结果转化为非参数类的泛化保证，并显示均匀随机插值器的泛化能力在标签污染下依然保持。

Conclusion: 过参数化网络中"随机选一个插值解"并非糟糕选择：ReLU网络存在强归纳偏置，使小网络见证所诱导的高体积区域淹没差泛化区域，从而产生自适应于数据内在复杂度的泛化界。该工作将近似论与泛化理论桥接，并为随机插值器的鲁棒性（抗标签污染）提供理论支持。

Abstract: Given a labeled dataset $\{(x_i, y_i)\}_{i = 1}^n$, consider drawing a parameter vector $\hatθ$ uniformly at random from those that interpolate the data within a width-$W$ depth-$D$ ReLU architecture with continuous weights bounded within $[-M, M]$. We study the generalization properties of $\hatθ$ in the case where the dataset can, in fact, be explained by a smaller $w$-by-$d$ ReLU network. Despite the obliviousness of the selection rule for $\hatθ$, it adaptively generalizes at the rate $\tilde O\left(\frac{wdWD}{n}\right)$, rather than being governed by the VC dimension of the full $W$-by-$D$ hypothesis class. The reason is due to a particular inductive bias in ReLU networks: the $w$-by-$d$ parameter *witnesses* an exponential volume of $W$-by-$D$ interpolating parameters, which dwarfs the volume of poorly generalizing parameters (conditioned on interpolation). We explicitly construct this set of near-equivalent $W$-by-$D$ parameters by a proof technique called *deep lifting*, and then tie the achievable volume to stability properties of the $w$-by-$d$ witness. Examples show that these techniques allow us to convert approximation-theoretic results into generalization guarantees for nonparametric classes. We further show that generalization of uniform-random interpolators can survive label corruptions.

</details>


### [157] [Towards Looped Models Done Right, Part I: Topology, Input Injection, Recurrent-State Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23036)
*Benhao Huang,Chufan Shi,Junlin Chen,Shicheng Wen,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric Xing,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统隔离了循环语言模型中的循环放置、输入注入与循环状态组织三个设计轴，发现"前奏-循环块-尾声"夹层结构（Huginn式）最优，且优势可迁移至MoE模型，并以75%更少常驻参数超越112层前馈MoE基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环语言模型（looped LM）在逻辑深度上复用物理Transformer块，但已有架构将循环位置、输入注入方式、循环状态组织等因素纠缠在一起，无法判断各因素的独立贡献，也缺乏向MoE架构迁移的验证。

Method: 从零训练、在参数量/逻辑深度/token预算匹配条件下对三个设计轴进行受控消融；随后在常驻参数与激活参数匹配的MoE模型上检验设计原则的可迁移性；并与Ouro架构及112层前馈MoE基线在同等训练与推理FLOPs下对比。

Result: prelude-recurrent block-coda夹层结构在上下文抽取与推理密集型任务上稳定提升；输入注入主要改善知识检索与上下文利用；随机初始化与分离的H/L循环状态收益混合或为负。730M参数/336B token规模下完整Huginn在全部10个基准上优于Ouro；8BA0.8B（展开为32BA3B）/500B token规模下Huginn在知识、推理、编程基准上几乎全面优于Ouro，且专家路由更均衡；同FLOPs下Huginn式模型常驻参数比112层前馈MoE少75%，在DROP、GSM8K上更优，MATH500持平，其余基准具竞争力。

Conclusion: 循环LM的性能主要由循环结构布局（前奏-循环块-尾声夹层）决定，而非输入注入或状态分离等细节；该设计原则可跨稠密与MoE架构迁移，并能以显著更少的常驻参数实现可比甚至更优的性能，支持Huginn式设计作为循环架构的优选范式。

Abstract: Looped language models reuse physical Transformer blocks across logical depth, but established architectures entangle recurrence placement, input injection, and recurrent state organization. We isolate these axes in models trained from scratch at matched parameter scale, logical depth, and token budgets, and then test whether the resulting design principles transfer to resident- and active-parameter-matched Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. The prelude-recurrent block-coda sandwich architecture consistently improves performance on context extraction and reasoning-intensive tasks. Input injection primarily enhances knowledge retrieval and context utilization, whereas random initialization and separate H/L recurrent states provide mixed or negative returns. Taken together, these results favor the Huginn-style design: at the 730M-parameter and 336B-token scale, the complete Huginn architecture outperforms Ouro across all ten benchmarks. The advantages of Huginn over Ouro transfer to MoE models. At the 8BA0.8B (32BA3B when unrolled) scale trained on 500B tokens, Huginn outperforms Ouro on nearly all benchmarks spanning knowledge, reasoning, and coding, while also exhibiting more balanced expert routing. Under matched training and inference FLOPs, the Huginn-style model uses 75% fewer resident parameters than a 112-layer feedforward MoE baseline. Despite its smaller physical parameters, it outperforms the feedforward baseline on DROP and GSM8K, matches it on MATH500, and remains competitive on the other benchmarks.

</details>


### [158] [GR-OPD: Group-Relative Sequence Credit for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23042)
*Yushu Wang,Renkai Yang,Jingcao Xu,Zili Geng,Haihong Tang,Yaohui Jin,Hao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Group-Relative On-Policy Distillation（\method{}），将反馈计算与信用分配解耦：把 token 级反馈聚合为校准后的序列级信用，理论上证明其对应序列级 reverse-KL 负梯度的无偏估计，并在多组 Qwen3 师生设置与数学推理基准上稳定优于原生 OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见的采样 token 更新只做局部信用分配，忽略了早期动作对后续反馈的影响；同时分布偏移会让后续反馈可靠性下降，原生 OPD 在思考模式不匹配时会训练崩溃。

Method: 将 token 反馈聚合为序列级信用，解耦反馈计算与信用分配；理论上证明在理想 on-policy 假设下，把原始求和反馈广播到每个响应 token 是序列级 reverse-KL 目标负梯度的无偏估计；实践中对可能因分布偏移而不可靠的后续反馈做折扣，并用同 prompt 的 rollout 组校准教师指导，保留相对响应偏好同时保留受控比例的组合均值（collective teacher pressure）。

Result: 在 5 组 Qwen3 师生设置与 6 个数学推理基准上，macro Avg@K 全面优于原生 OPD，其中两个思考模式匹配的设置分别提升 10.9 与 11.5 个百分点；在思考模式不匹配时训练保持稳定，而原生 OPD 崩溃。消融表明适量 collective teacher pressure 有益，过量或反馈视野不受限会使训练不稳定。

Conclusion: 把序列级信用分配的视角引入 on-policy 蒸馏可兼顾理论无偏性与实践稳定性，关键在于对反馈视野做折扣并施加受控的组合教师压力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides token-level teacher feedback, yet common sampled-token updates assign credit locally and omit the effects of earlier actions on subsequent feedback. We introduce \method{} (Group-Relative On-Policy Distillation), which decouples feedback computation from credit assignment by aggregating token feedback into calibrated sequence-level credit. We establish a theoretical basis for this approach by showing that, under ideal on-policy assumptions, broadcasting raw summed feedback to every response token yields an unbiased estimate of the negative gradient of the sequence-level reverse-KL objective. To support effective learning in practice, \method{} discounts later feedback that may become less reliable under distribution shift and uses same-prompt rollout groups to calibrate teacher guidance. It preserves relative response preferences while retaining a controlled fraction of the group mean, which we term collective teacher pressure. Across five Qwen3 teacher--student settings and six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, \method{} outperforms vanilla OPD in macro Avg@K in every setting, including gains of 10.9 and 11.5 percentage points in two settings with matched thinking patterns. It also maintains stable training under mismatched thinking patterns, where vanilla OPD collapses. Ablations show that retaining an appropriate amount of collective teacher pressure can improve learning, while excessive collective teacher pressure or an unrestricted feedback horizon can destabilize training.

</details>


### [159] [Path to Prior-Data Fitted Relational Models via Learned Latent Multi-table Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23073)
*Mohammad Sadeq Abolhasani,Viswanath Ganapathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将 PFN 式合成预训练范式迁移到基于消息传递的图关系基础模型 Griffin，发现"先模式引导、后模式无关"的合成课程学习最能提升冻结式上下文预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFN 表明在大规模合成任务上预训练可使模型泛化到未见数据集，但该范式主要局限于基于 Transformer 的表格/关系模型（如 RDB-PFN），对基于消息传递的图关系模型尚未探索；同时需验证对 Transformer 关系学习器有效的课程学习是否同样利于消息传递表示。

Method: 在大量合成关系数据库集合上预训练图关系基础模型 Griffin；不进行下游微调，而将预训练编码器作为通用关系表示学习器，其潜在表示送入冻结的上下文预测器（ICL），目标任务的任何组件均不训练。实验在 16 个真实关系分类任务上、5 个随机种子下，与原始特征基线和未训练编码器基线对比。

Result: 由"模式引导数据→模式无关数据"构成的纯合成课程取得最强的冻结 ICL 性能，优于仅用真实数据预训练以及所有无序的单阶段语料混合；当该课程接在真实数据预训练之后时，得到整体最佳编码器。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明 PFN 式合成预训练可迁移至基于图的关系基础模型，且课程顺序（模式引导→模式无关）对消息传递表示具有显著作用。

Abstract: Prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) show that pretraining on large populations of synthetic tasks can enable generalization to unseen datasets with little or no downstream fine-tuning. This paradigm is effective for transformer-based tabular and relational foundation models such as RDB-PFN, but remains largely unexplored for graph-based relational models built on message passing. We pretrain the graph-based relational foundation model Griffin on large collections of synthetic relational databases and ask whether curriculum learning, which benefits transformer-based relational learners, also improves message-passing representations. Rather than fine-tune on downstream tasks, we use the pretrained encoder as a general-purpose relational representation learner and pass its latent representations to a frozen in-context predictor (\ticl{}); no component is trained on the target task. On 16 real-world relational classification tasks, evaluated over five seeds against raw-feature and untrained-encoder baselines, a purely synthetic curriculum that begins with schema-guided data and continues with schema-agnostic data gives the strongest frozen in-context performance\rev{, outperforming real-data-only pretraining and every unordered single-stage mixture of the corpora; when appended after real-data pretraining, it yields the best encoders overall}. These results provide initial evidence that PFN-style synthetic pretraining transfers to graph-based relational foundation models.

</details>


### [160] [Where Latents Belong and How They Flow: Joint Identification of Structural Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23074)
*Xintong Li,Kun Zhang,Yujia Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出"潜在因子的结构角色"概念，刻画潜变量在何处被表达、是否接收过去影响、是否传递影响至未来，并在共同置换下实现三种角色的联合可辨识，进而提出RoleTrace框架在共享潜在坐标系中追踪结构角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力系统中的潜在因子在不同观测与时间上扮演不同角色（如会议中的想法仅由部分参与者表达、继承先前讨论信息并塑造后续发言），但现有工作未形式化刻画这种"角色"；核心难点是对应性问题：三种结构可能在不同置换下被识别，而结构角色只有在对齐到同一潜在坐标时才有定义。

Method: 将结构角色形式化为三要素：表达位置（where）、是否受过去影响（receives influence）、是否影响未来（transmits influence）。理论上，在适当的结构条件与变异性条件下证明三种角色在每一时间点于共同置换下联合可辨识。据此设计RoleTrace框架，在共享潜在坐标系中追踪结构角色。

Result: 合成数据实验验证了结构恢复的有效性；在多方会议数据上的实验考察了真实序列交互中所识别出的角色。

Conclusion: 结构角色可在共同置换下联合可辨识，RoleTrace能够将不同结构对齐到统一潜在坐标并有效追踪角色，兼具理论保证与实证支持。

Abstract: Latent factors in dynamical systems can play different roles across observations and time. For example, an idea in a group meeting may be expressed by only some participants, inherit information from earlier discussions, and shape what is said next. We formalize this notion as the structural role of a latent factor, describing where it is expressed, whether it receives influence from the past, and whether it transmits influence to the future. The main challenge is correspondence, since the three structures may be identified under different permutations, while a structural role is defined only when they are aligned to the same latent coordinate. We show that, under suitable structural and variability conditions, all three roles are jointly identifiable under a common permutation at each time point. Guided by this theory, we develop RoleTrace, a framework that traces structural roles in a shared latent coordinate system. Synthetic experiments validate structural recovery, while experiments on multiparty meetings examine the identified roles in real sequential interactions.

</details>


### [161] [Towards a GraphNARX model for structural dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23103)
*George Tsialiamanis,Keith Worden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出图基非线性自回归外生模型GraphNARX，用图结构刻画结构几何并以单元交互建模动力行为，可实现系统辨识、跨尺寸泛化与虚拟传感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NARX模型难以利用结构几何信息，且通常局限于固定维度与自由度数量的特定结构，无法泛化到不同规模的结构，也难以在数据缺失节点上进行推断。

Method: 构建GraphNARX图神经网络式NARX模型：节点属性编码历史位移与节点力，输出为下一时刻位移；图表述使模型可适配不同维度与自由度的结构；图学习在节点局部邻域进行，从而可学习推断无数据节点的位移（虚拟传感）。

Result: 在系统辨识与虚拟传感两个框架下评估，均取得良好精度，并表现出对不同规模结构的泛化能力。

Conclusion: GraphNARX通过图结构融合几何信息与局部图学习，统一实现系统辨识与虚拟传感，并具备跨尺寸泛化性，为结构动力学建模提供了可扩展的建模范式。

Abstract: The current paper introduces the GraphNARX model, a graph-based nonlinear autoregressive exogenous model for structural dynamics. The model is built to account for the geometry of structures and to model dynamic behaviour as the interaction between elements of the structure. Similar to other types of NARX models, GraphNARX is used for system identification. Nodal attributes of the graph encode past displacements and nodal forces, and the output of the model is the next-timestep displacement. The graph formulation of the model allows it to generalise to structures of different dimensions and degrees of freedom. Moreover, because graph-learning is performed locally between the nodes, the GraphNARX model can also learn to infer displacements of nodes for which data are not available, i.e.\ to perform virtual sensing. The proposed model's performance is evaluated in a system-identification framework and a virtual-sensing framework, and achieves good accuracy and exhibits generalisation capabilities to structures of different sizes.

</details>


### [162] [Domain-specific language alignment of multimodal embeddings towards explainable predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23110)
*Gabrielė Tijūnaitytė,Thijs L. van der Plas,Mohamed-Bachir Belaid,Rob Knapen,Maciej J. Soja,Ioannis N. Athanasiadis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个通用框架，可将任意能与可解释辅助数据关联的嵌入空间与语言对齐：用标注函数将辅助数据转为部分描述，以软对比损失按属性相似度加权配对，并用基于排序的概念检索任务评估对齐；应用于地球多模态嵌入（AlphaEarth），在S2BMS与SatBird任务上超越SOTA，并实现可解释的物种–生境关联分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态数据融合得到的潜在表示虽提升下游任务性能但缺乏可解释性，限制了在需要可解释决策的领域的应用。视觉–语言对齐可使图像嵌入通过语言获得可解释性，但依赖大量图文对数据；而多模态嵌入通常缺乏配对的描述数据，且相关特征无法直接从嵌入本身观察和标注。

Method: 提出语言对齐任意嵌入空间的框架，适用于可与可解释辅助数据关联的嵌入：(1) 描述函数（captioning functions）将辅助数据转换为部分描述（partial captions）；(2) 软对比损失（soft contrastive loss）依据每段描述所刻画属性的相似度对样本对加权；(3) 基于排序的概念检索（rank-based concept retrieval）任务，以辅助数据而非描述文本为基准评估对齐效果。将该框架应用于地球多模态嵌入，在两个物种分布建模任务 S2BMS 与 SatBird 上验证。

Result: AlphaEarth 嵌入在 6/7 项指标上优于此前SOTA模型；该框架在嵌入空间中实现领域特定概念的语言对齐，在两个数据集上的平均概念检索性能均优于先前的对地观测视觉–语言模型；对齐后的概念可恢复物种–生境关联（ρ=0.44），并能将生境描述与物种预测相联系。

Conclusion: 抽象的多模态嵌入空间可以通过可解释辅助数据进行语言对齐，从而以领域专家的语言对其进行审视与 interrogate，提升下游预测性能的同时赋予模型决策可解释性；该框架具有通用性，可推广至其他具有可解释辅助数据的嵌入空间。

Abstract: Multimodal data are commonly fused into latent representations that improve downstream task performance but are not interpretable, limiting their application in domains that require explainable model decisions. Vision-language alignment has made image embeddings interpretable through language, but requires vast image-caption training data. However, captioned data are typically not available for multimodal embeddings, as relevant features cannot directly be observed and annotated from embeddings alone. Here, we propose a framework to language-align any embedding space that can be linked to interpretable auxiliary data. Captioning functions convert auxiliary data into partial captions; a soft contrastive loss weights pairs by the similarity of their properties that each caption describes; and a rank-based concept retrieval task evaluates alignment against the auxiliary data rather than the captions. We apply this framework to multimodal Earth embeddings to facilitate explainable predictions for two species distribution modelling tasks: S2BMS and SatBird. AlphaEarth embeddings beat previous state-of-the-art models on 6/7 metrics, while our framework language-aligns domain-specific concepts of the embedding space, outperforming previous Earth observation vision-language models on average concept retrieval for both datasets. Finally, aligned concepts recover species-habitat associations ($ρ$=0.44) and relate habitat descriptions to species predictions, showing that abstract embeddings can be interrogated in the language of domain experts.

</details>


### [163] [LED: Improving Masked Diffusion Language Models by Lookahead Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23122)
*Yuyan Zhou,Zeyuan Yang,Yuxuan Song,Zhicheng Cai,Hongli Yu,Weiying Ma,Hao Zhou,James Kwok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Lookahead Editing（LED），在掩码扩散解码中引入可重写提议状态，以较低成本兼得 MDM 易优化性与迭代修订的表达力；LED-7B/8B 在八个基准上显著超越标准 MDM 微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型分两支：MDM 采用基于掩码的转移调度，易优化但计算表达力低；UDM 支持迭代状态修订、表达力更强但训练困难。作者希望在不放弃 MDM 简单转移调度的前提下获得迭代修订的表达能力。

Method: 理论分析 MDM 与 UDM 在可优化性与表达力上的权衡；据此提出 LED：在掩码扩散解码中附加一个可重写的提议状态，每个解码步在该状态上执行多次 lookahead 更新以迭代精修未来预测，再将 token 提交到掩码序列，用额外计算换取表达力，同时保留掩码式转移调度。基于 Qwen2.5-7B 与 Qwen3-8B 微调得到 LED-7B、LED-8B。

Result: 在八个基准上，LED SFT 相比标准 MDM SFT 平均提升 +11.4 与 +7.7 分；仅用 SFT 的 LED-8B 超过经持续预训练、监督微调与强化学习训练的 LLaDA2.1-16B Q-mode。

Conclusion: 可重写提议状态是一种有效机制，能够在保持 MDM 易优化转移调度的同时显著提升表达力与生成质量，验证了理论权衡分析并展现出优于更大规模强基线模型的效率优势。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive language models, enabling non-autoregressive text generation through parallel decoding. Existing dLLMs can be broadly categorized into two families: masked diffusion models (MDMs) and uniform diffusion models (UDMs) In this work, we theoretically show that MDMs are easier to optimize due to their mask-based transition schedule but have lower computational expressivity, whereas UDMs enable iterative state revision and greater expressivity but are substantially harder to train. This trade-off raises a natural question: can we obtain the expressivity of iterative revision without giving up the simple, easy-to-optimize transition schedule of MDMs? Motivated by this analysis, we propose Lookahead Editing (\ours{}), which augments masked diffusion decoding with a rewritable proposal state. At each decoding step, the model performs several lookahead updates to iteratively refine future predictions in this proposal state before committing tokens to the masked sequence. This design introduces a rewritable proposal that uses additional compute to improve expressivity while keeping the simple mask-based transition schedule of MDMs. We fine-tune Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen3-8B to obtain LED-\texttt{7B} and LED-\texttt{8B}, respectively. Across eight benchmarks, LED SFT yields average gains of $+11.4$ and $+7.7$ points over standard MDM SFT. Moreover, using SFT alone, LED-\texttt{8B} outperforms LLaDA2.1-16B Q-mode, which is trained with continued pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [164] [MetaCurate: Evolving Curation Filters for High-Quality Agentic Coding Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23134)
*Donghoon Kim,Seunghyuk Oh,Kevin Galim,Minseo Kim,Minjae Lee,Younghyun Kim,Wooseok Seo,Hyung Il Koo,Wonjun Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaCurate 是一个无需训练的框架，利用成对 LLM 判断来进化可执行的轨迹筛选过滤器，自动发现轨迹筛选策略，在下游 SFT 实验中取得最高 SWE-Verified 均分，且搜索成本仅为训练循环基线的 1/3.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对智能体轨迹做监督微调是提升编程智能体的有效手段，但挑选有用训练样本不能仅凭"成功/失败"。成功的 patch 可能伴随低效推理或多余工具调用，失败的运行也可能包含有用的中间步骤。现有流水线依赖人工编写的规则与定性评估来过滤轨迹，既需要大量专家经验，通过反复训练-评估验证改进又代价高昂。

Method: 提出 MetaCurate：一个无需训练的轨迹筛选框架，通过成对 LLM 判断来进化可执行的筛选过滤器。过滤器排序与判断器偏好之间的不一致被用作定向反馈，用于联合修订其规则、评分标准（rubric）与聚合策略。最终过滤器可对轨迹独立打分，因此成对比较只在搜索阶段需要。从空白过滤器起步，自主发现筛选策略。

Result: 在未筛选轨迹池与 Nemotron v3 SWE 数据集上进行受控 SFT 实验，以随机选择和人工撰写过滤为基线。MetaCurate 在对比方法中获得最高的平均 SWE-Verified 分数，优于人工规则过滤，也优于搜索成本高 3.5 倍的训练循环基线。

Conclusion: 轨迹筛选策略可以无需人工撰写标准而被自动发现，并在下游性能上具有竞争力，同时显著降低搜索成本，为智能体轨迹的 SFT 数据选择提供了一种高效、可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning on agent trajectories is an effective way to improve coding agents, but selecting useful training examples requires more than identifying successful runs. A resolved patch may follow inefficient reasoning or unnecessary tool use, while a failed run may still contain useful intermediate steps. Training pipelines therefore rely on rules and qualitative assessments to filter trajectories, yet designing these filters typically requires substantial human expertise, and validating improvements through repeated training and evaluation is expensive. We introduce MetaCurate, a training-free framework that evolves executable trajectory curation filters using pairwise LLM judgments. Disagreements between the filter's rankings and the judge's preferences provide targeted feedback for jointly revising its rules, rubric, and aggregation policy. The resulting filter scores trajectories independently, so pairwise comparisons are required only during search. Starting from a blank filter, MetaCurate autonomously discovers a curation strategy that achieves competitive downstream performance without task-specific handwritten criteria. We validate downstream utility through controlled SFT experiments on our uncurated trajectory pool and the Nemotron v3 SWE dataset, with random selection and hand-authored filtering as baselines. In our experiments, MetaCurate achieves the highest mean SWE-Verified score among the compared methods, including hand-authored rule-based filtering and a training-loop baseline whose search costs 3.5x as much.

</details>


### [165] [Local proxies for memory-efficient training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23145)
*Wu S. Zihan,Brian A. Yuan,Guillaume Bellec*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出在Transformer中通过为每个block训练局部代理网络（以蒸馏方式预测全局输出）来实现精确且内存高效的局部学习，称为代理传播，在视觉Transformer与LLM微调中取得优于其他局部算法的精度/内存权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准端到端反向传播的内存瓶颈在于需保存全部激活直到损失梯度回传完成；局部学习可避免此问题，但通常以精度下降为代价。作者希望为Transformer实现既精确又省内存的局部学习。

Method: 对每个Transformer block训练一个局部代理网络，通过蒸馏预测全局网络输出；梯度可独立地在局部代理中回传，从而较好地估计真实反向传播梯度。理论上，该方法对深层线性网络是精确的；在非线性残差模型中，采用合适的残差代理可界定梯度误差。通过数值实验验证误差界，并证明优化蒸馏损失可改善梯度估计。

Result: 在CIFAR-100上的视觉Transformer评测中，代理传播相比其他局部算法取得更好的精度/内存权衡；在有限内存预算下微调大语言模型时，该方法比内存高效的零阶学习算法训练更快、性能更高，且内存成本相当。

Conclusion: 基于局部代理网络与蒸馏的代理传播为Transformer提供了精确、内存高效的局部学习方案，兼具理论保证与实证优势，适用于视觉任务与大规模语言模型微调。

Abstract: Local learning algorithms can reshape resource requirements of artificial intelligence. The memory bottleneck of standard end-to-end backpropagation lies in the need to store all network activations until the loss gradient has been propagated back through. Local learning avoids this, but typically at a cost in accuracy. We study how to achieve accurate and memory efficient local learning in transformers. For each transformer block, we train a local proxy network to predict the global network output via distillation. Gradients can be independently propagated through this local proxy while estimating well the backpropagation gradients. In theory, this approach is exact for deep linear networks; in non-linear residual models, the gradient error can be bounded when using the appropriate residual proxy. We verify this numerically and demonstrate that optimizing the distillation loss improves gradient estimation. Evaluating the performance of proxy propagation on CIFAR-100 in vision transformers, we find that it achieves a better accuracy/memory trade-off compared to other local algorithms. Finally, we show that the method is further relevant for fine-tuning large language models on a small memory budget. Our method trains faster and achieves higher performance than memory-efficient zeroth-order learning algorithms with comparable memory cost.

</details>


### [166] [Grounded Credit Assignment in Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Turn Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23148)
*Qirui Liu,Hao Chen,Yi Wen,Weijie Shi,Yao Zhao,Xianhe Chen,Hao Zhao,Xing Tang,Weihong Luo,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出GRPO式结果奖励对搜索智能体的所有token赋予相同优势，导致"准确率上升但依据性下降"；提出Grounded Credit Assignment（GCA），用可验证子结果分别校正搜索与回答的信用分配，在不增加额外模型/rollout/训练阶段的情况下提升准确率与证据支持率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索智能体的一次rollout同时涉及两种能力：找到证据与使用证据，而GRPO等结果奖励方法给所有token相同的advantage，无法区分二者的贡献。作者发现Search-R1在七个QA基准上准确率均提升，但在Bamboogle上正确答案中"有证据支持"的比例从84.0%跌至42.9%，即准确率提升缺乏grounding。其原因在于：只有当一个rollout的准确率增益相对其他rollout足够大时，检索到含答案证据的rollout才在总体上被偏好；而"凭记忆作答"或"误用证据"反而会缩小甚至逆转这一grounding差距，使信用分配失真。

Method: 提出Grounded Credit Assignment（GCA），利用可验证的子结果（证据检索、答案正确性）对搜索信用与回答信用分别进行修正：对错误答案的惩罚重于对"已找到证据的搜索"的惩罚，对绕过可检索证据而得到的正确答案给予更低信用。该修正对"找到证据的搜索"永不为负，因此不会随grounding差距缩小而失效；修正量被限制在每个token advantage的10%以内，从而不会压过原始结果信号。方法无需额外模型、额外rollout或额外训练阶段。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-3B上，GCA相对Search-R1平均EM提升5.7分；在Qwen3-30B-A3B上提升6.3分；同时将正确答案的证据支持比例从76.9%提升至93.4%。

Conclusion: 结果级奖励的均匀信用分配会造成"准确率与grounding脱钩"，GCA通过子结果分解的细粒度信用分配，在零额外开销下同时提升任务准确率与答案依据性，为搜索智能体RL训练提供了简洁有效的信用分配修正方案。

Abstract: Outcome-reward reinforcement learning such as GRPO gives every token of a search-agent rollout the same advantage, although the rollout exercises two capabilities: finding evidence and using it. We show that accuracy improves without grounding: Search-R1 raises accuracy on all seven QA benchmarks, yet on Bamboogle the evidence-supported share of correct answers falls from 84.0% to 42.9%. We attribute this to GRPO's credit assignment: rollouts that retrieve answer-bearing evidence are favoured in aggregate only in proportion to their accuracy gain over the rest, a grounding gap that answering from memory or misusing evidence shrinks or reverses. To address this, we propose Grounded Credit Assignment (GCA), which uses verifiable sub-outcomes (evidence retrieval and answer correctness) to correct search and answer credit separately: a wrong answer is blamed more than the search that found the evidence, and a correct answer that bypasses retrievable evidence is credited less. Never negative on evidence-finding search, the correction does not shrink with the grounding gap; capped at 10% of each token's advantage, it never overrides the outcome signal. Without extra models, rollouts, or training stages, GCA improves average EM over Search-R1 by 5.7 (Qwen2.5-3B) and 6.3 points (Qwen3-30B-A3B) and raises this share from 76.9% to 93.4%.

</details>


### [167] [Nearly Linear Private Learning of Littlestone Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23160)
*Shay Moran,Chao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明 Littlestone 维数为 d 的二分类类存在 O(d log d) 的近似差分隐私 PAC 学习样本复杂度上界，接近线性，显著改进了此前的 Õ(d^5) 和 Õ(d^6)。核心是提出一种结构化指数机制，以及为一般 Littlestone 类构造满足稳定性条件的秩函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二分类的近似差分隐私 PAC 学习中，有限 Littlestone 维刻画了可学习性，但该维数如何控制私有样本复杂度仍是核心问题。已有上界从双指数（Bun–Livni–Moran）改进到 Õ(d^6)、Õ(d^5)，仍与下界存在较大差距；同时在子空间指示类等自然概念类上，已有算法的维度依赖为二次，未达线性。

Method: 算法上，提出针对带秩函数的数据依赖候选集的结构化指数机制（structural exponential mechanism），该秩需满足适当的稳定性条件，从而给出私有 PAC 学习器；对子空间指示类，秩具有简单几何解释，即余维数，由此得到任意域上维数至多 d 的子空间的私有 PAC 学习器，对 d 线性依赖且与环境维数无关。组合上，借鉴模型论思想为一般 Littlestone 类构造合适的秩，并建立机制所需的计数界。

Result: 一般 Littlestone 维数 d 的类获得 O(d log d) 的近似私有 PAC 样本复杂度上界，接近线性；对子空间类得到对 d 严格线性、与环境维数无关的样本复杂度，改进了 Kaplan 等人（STOC 2025）的二次依赖。

Conclusion: 通过结构化指数机制与模型论式秩构造的结合，将差分隐私 PAC 学习的样本复杂度上界推进至近线性 O(d log d)，并在子空间类上达到最优阶的线性依赖，统一并强化了此前的算法与组合结果。

Abstract: Differentially private learning aims to learn from data while protecting individual training examples. For binary classification, finite Littlestone dimension characterizes approximate private PAC learnability (Alon et al., JACM 2022). Understanding how this dimension controls private sample complexity remains a central question. For classes of Littlestone dimension $d$, the first general upper bound was doubly exponential in $d$ (Bun, Livni, and Moran, FOCS 2020). Subsequent work improved this to $\widetilde O(d^6)$ (Ghazi et al., STOC 2021) and then to $\widetilde O(d^5)$ (Lyu, STOC 2026). We prove a nearly linear upper bound of $O(d\log d)$.
  Our main algorithmic contribution is a structural exponential mechanism for data-dependent candidate sets equipped with a rank satisfying suitable stability conditions. For natural Littlestone classes such as subspace indicators, this rank has a simple geometric interpretation: it is the codimension. This yields a private PAC learner for subspaces of dimension at most $d$ over any field, with tight linear dependence on $d$ and no dependence on the ambient dimension, improving the quadratic dependence of Kaplan et al. (STOC 2025). Our second contribution is combinatorial: drawing on model-theoretic ideas, we construct a suitable rank for general Littlestone classes and establish the counting bounds required by the mechanism.

</details>


### [168] [Structural Affinity: An Algorithm for Coherent Cage-Based Shape Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23173)
*Aviraj Newatia,Shengan Yang,Hantao Zhong,Rika Antonova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Structural Affinity显式正则化方法：用均值坐标由粗外层笼构建仿射矩阵，将内层控制笼绑定到外层笼以耦合手柄位移，从而在变形时以线性开销获得更平滑、低畸变、少自交的笼形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于笼的变形（CBD）广泛用于动画、交互建模、协同设计、神经变形迁移与气动外形优化，但现有正则化多为软约束，与任务目标竞争，未改变变形参数化本身，因而仍会出现笼自交与网格畸变。

Method: 在两个笼结构下预计算仿射矩阵：以均值值坐标（mean-value coordinates）将内层控制笼绑定至更粗的外层笼，该矩阵定义单个手柄位移向相邻手柄的传播量，从而在参数化层面直接耦合手柄运动；变形阶段仅增加线性开销。

Result: 在机器人控制—形态协同设计、源到目标神经变形迁移、机翼气动外形优化三类任务上，产生更规则的形变并显著降低对手工结构损失项的依赖；三项诊断指标（余切拉普拉斯、笼畸变、表面粗糙度）均有一致提升，笼自交大幅减少甚至消除。

Conclusion: Structural Affinity以显式参数化正则替代软性损失引导，可在多种CBD下游任务中稳定改善形变质量且计算代价低，为CBD优化提供通用、轻量的结构性约束方案。

Abstract: Cage-based deformation (CBD) parametrises 3D geometry through a small set of control handles, effectively enabling a variety of tasks from animation and interactive modelling to tool-based co-design, neural deformation transfer, and aerodynamic shape optimization. Many optimization approaches relying on CBD employ regularisation strategies to reduce the distortion of the deformed mesh by encouraging smooth, regular cage deformations. These techniques offer soft guidance to an optimization algorithm towards desirable deformations, but compete with the task objective rather than changing the deformation parameterization itself. In this work we introduce Structural Affinity for CBD, an explicit regularisation method which enables smooth deformations through control cages by exploiting a two-cage structure to couple cage-handle motion directly. Structural Affinity precomputes an affinity matrix using mean-value coordinates, binding the inner control-cage to a coarser outer-cage. This matrix defines how much a cage handle displacement propagates to neighbouring handles which substantially decreases and often eliminates cage self-intersections, reduces mesh distortion, and adds linear overhead at deformation time. Across robotic control--morphology co-design, source-to-target neural deformation transfer, and aerodynamic wing shape optimization, Structural Affinity produces more regular deformations and substantially reduces reliance on hand-designed structural loss terms. We report consistent gains on three complementary diagnostics: the cotangent Laplacian, a proxy for surface smoothness; cage distortion, capturing whether control handles move coherently; and surface roughness, which measures undesirable oscillation in wing geometry.

</details>


### [169] [Tree-Aware Posterior Distillation for Long-Horizon Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23181)
*Heming Zou,Qi Wang,Yixiu Mao,Yun Qu,Yuyi Guo,Zeyuan Liu,Chenglin Li,Weijie Liu,Saiyong Yang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TreeOPSD：无critic的树感知后验自蒸馏框架，用树分支回传奖励构造有界闭式教师分布，解决长程智能体RL的信用分配稀疏与策略陈旧问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程交互智能体RL存在两个问题：(1) 仅用终端奖励导致回合级信用分配稀疏、高方差；(2) 在固定rollout批次上做序列小批量更新会随当前策略偏离行为策略而产生策略陈旧。已有方法依赖价值网络、过程奖励模型、步骤级标注或更强外部教师，增加训练复杂度；重要性采样与裁剪虽缓解漂移，但未给出有限目标，无法阻止序列更新过冲。

Method: 提出TreeOPSD，将每个交互回合视为树节点，从共享前缀分支出备选后续，递归将终端奖励回传为隐式回合级信用；再把每个信用映射为由相对冻结行为策略的似然比参数化的闭式、有界教师分布；最小化该教师与学生策略的散度，在行为策略处切向恢复策略梯度方向，同时在跨序列小批量更新中有界地趋向有限目标，无需价值网络。

Result: 在多个竞争性基准和模型规模上提升智能体能力，长程任务尤为明显；在Deep Research设定下，Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507的平均准确率较GRPO提升4.97个百分点。

Conclusion: TreeOPSD以树结构自蒸馏实现无critic、无步骤标注、无外部教师的稠密信用分配，并为序列小批量更新提供有界目标，在长程智能体RL中优于GRPO等基线。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become a scalable post-training paradigm for large language models. However, training agents over long-horizon interactions presents two challenges: using only terminal rewards makes turn-level credit assignment sparse and high-variance, while sequential mini-batch updates on a fixed rollout batch introduce policy staleness as the current policy drifts from the behavior policy. Existing approaches obtain denser credit via learned critics, process reward models, or on-policy distillation, but they rely on a value network, step-level annotations, or a stronger external teacher that increase training complexity. Meanwhile, importance sampling and clipping mitigate policy drift, but they do not specify a finite target that stops sequential updates from overshooting. In this work, we introduce TreeOPSD, a critic-free, tree-aware posterior self-distillation framework for long-horizon agentic reinforcement learning. Treating each interaction turn as a tree node, TreeOPSD branches alternative continuations from shared prefixes and recursively backs up terminal rewards into implicit turn-level credit. It then maps each credit to a closed-form, bounded teacher distribution parameterized by the likelihood ratio relative to the frozen behavior policy. Minimizing the divergence between this teacher and the student policy recovers the policy-gradient direction as the tangent at the behavior policy while bounding updates toward a finite target across sequential mini-batches. Empirically, TreeOPSD improves agentic capabilities across several competitive benchmarks and model scales, especially for long-horizon tasks, e.g., raising the average accuracy of Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507 by 4.97 points over GRPO on the Deep Research setting.

</details>


### [170] [A Stronger Teacher Within: Strengthening MoE Models via On-Policy Bidirectional Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23185)
*Haoyu Chen,Jun Bai,Yang Liu,Jianfei Zhang,Wenge Rong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MoEvolve，一种双向蒸馏框架，通过"先恢复、后提升"策略利用 MoE 激活预算扩展产生的更强教师来增强原低激活模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 对 MoE LLM 的监督仅停留在结果层面的稀疏奖励；on-policy 蒸馏虽提供 token 级稠密反馈，但需更强的兼容教师，而 on-policy 自蒸馏依赖人工设计的特权信息。作者观察到 MoE 架构本身可提供解决途径：提高每 token 的激活预算可释放潜在专家容量，形成模型内部的更强教师。

Method: MoEvolve 采用双向蒸馏：第一步先恢复因激活扩展（high-k）而性能退化的高激活模型，并利用其额外激活容量进一步强化；第二步反转蒸馏方向，将增强后的能力迁移回原始 low-k 配置，从而保持推理期激活预算不变。该方法针对朴素扩展导致新共激活专家未协同优化的问题。
