Result: 在多种 MoE 模型与基准上，MoEvolve 相较初始模型最高提升 22.2 个领域平均分，并比同等预算的 RLVR 基线最高领先 6.5 分；最终 low-k 模型常超越其激活扩展教师。

Conclusion: MoE 自身可通过激活扩展提供内部教师，双向蒸馏可将扩展容量带来的能力有效回灌至原始激活预算配置，实现无需外部教师、保持推理成本的高效提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has substantially improved Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs, but its outcome-level rewards provide only sparse supervision. On-Policy Distillation offers dense token-level feedback, yet requires a stronger compatible teacher, while on-policy self-distillation relies on hand-designed privileged information. We observe that the MoE architecture itself offers a way to resolve this dilemma, as increasing the activation budget per token exposes latent expert capacity and yields a potentially stronger teacher within the model. However, naive expansion degrades performance because newly co-activated experts are not optimized to work together. We therefore propose MoEvolve, a bidirectional distillation framework following a recovery-then-improvement strategy: we first restore the activation-expanded high-$k$ model and further strengthen it by exploiting its additional active capacity, then reverse the direction of distillation to transfer the enhanced capabilities back into the original low-$k$ configuration, retaining its inference-time activation budget. Across diverse MoE models and benchmarks, MoEvolve improves the initial models by up to $22.2$ domain-average points and outperforms same-budget RLVR baselines by up to $6.5$ points. Notably, the final low-$k$ models often surpass their activation-expanded teachers.

</details>


### [171] [LangNCO: Compiling Natural-Language Constraints into Inference-Time Interventions for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23190)
*Hironori Ohigashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LangNCO框架，用冻结的LLM将自然语言需求编译为经模式校验的DSL规则，由确定性Rule Executor转为无扩张硬动作掩码，并用Critical-Offset Decoding（按规则引用的关键节点重分配预算做多强度偏置rollout）保证完整可行解可达，从而在不重训练、不改动预训练NCO策略的前提下实现部署后适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经组合优化（NCO）能在训练分布内快速给出高质量解，但部署后运行需求会变化且常以自然语言表达；重新训练或修改策略代价高，因此需要一种模块化、可检视、无需重训的推理时适应机制。局部掩码只能避免即时违规，无法保证仍存在完整可行续解，这是核心挑战。

Method: 1) 预训练LLM作为可替换语义前端，将每条自然语言要求翻译为schema校验的领域特定语言（DSL）规则集；2) 确定性Rule Executor将规则实现为非扩张（nonexpansive）硬动作掩码；3) Critical-Offset Decoding提取规则涉及的关键节点，把固定rollout预算的一部分重新分配给多强度偏置rollout，以恢复可行续解的可达性；4) 采用受控的任务特定语言，将求解器适应性与开放式语言鲁棒性解耦；在CVRP策略上评测道路封闭、时间窗、车辆指派、访问顺序、两两组合及12类附加约束。

Result: 四个主数据集上可行性达100%，平均相对参考解的间隙为1.63–3.26%；对两两约束使用分别生成的规则进行组合时，可行性为97.33–100%；12类附加约束上可行性为98.67%。

Conclusion: 结果表明可通过可检视的语言导出规则，实现对冻结NCO求解器的模块化部署后适应，无需重训练；该方法把语义理解与求解策略解耦，兼具模块化与可解释性。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) rapidly produces high-quality solutions for problem structures encountered during training, but operational requirements may change after deployment and be communicated in natural language. We propose LangNCO, a framework that compiles instance-specific requirements into structured inference-time interventions for a frozen NCO solver, without changing the pretrained policy's architecture, learned input representation, or parameters. A pretrained LLM serves as a replaceable semantic front end, translating each requirement into a schema-validated domain-specific language (DSL) rule set. A deterministic Rule Executor applies the rules as nonexpansive hard action masks. Because local masking prevents immediate violations but does not ensure that a complete feasible continuation remains reachable, Critical-Offset Decoding extracts rule-referenced critical nodes and reallocates part of a fixed rollout budget to multi-strength biased rollouts. Using controlled task-specific language to isolate solver adaptation from open-ended language robustness, we evaluate a CVRP policy on road closures, time windows, vehicle assignments, visit-order requirements, all pairwise combinations, and twelve additional constraint types. LangNCO achieves 100% feasibility on the four primary datasets with mean reference gaps of 1.63--3.26%, 97.33--100% feasibility when separately generated rules are composed for pairwise constraints, and 98.67% feasibility across the additional constraints. These results demonstrate modular post-training adaptation of a frozen NCO solver through inspectable language-derived rules, without retraining.

</details>


### [172] [Non-Parametric Optimization for Scalable Learning in Stochastic Decision Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23204)
*Mohsen Amidzadeh,Lauri Viitasaari,Mario Di Francesco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文针对时变随机优化（TV-SO）缺少非参数方法的空白，提出基于Malliavin微积分的随机变分框架，导出非参数最优性条件，并设计可扩展、对参数化维度不敏感的深度学习算法Stochastic Path Follower（SPF），在分布漂移下的最小二乘恢复与逻辑回归任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机优化在不确定性决策中居核心地位，其中时变随机优化因自适应控制与机器学习应用而尤为重要。已有工作为时变确定性优化提出了非参数方法，但尚未推广到随机情形，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建基于Malliavin微积分的随机变分框架，据此推导具有随机决策的SO问题的非参数最优性条件；并基于该框架设计深度学习算法Stochastic Path Follower（SPF），其计算复杂度对参数化维度不敏感，具备可扩展性。

Result: 在分布漂移场景下的最小二乘恢复与逻辑回归两个任务上进行实验，结果显示所提方法在性能与可扩展性两方面均优于当前最优的基于学习与基于梯度的方法。

Conclusion: Malliavin微积分可为时变随机优化提供统一的变分与非参数化理论工具，所导出的SPF算法在分布漂移问题上兼具性能与可扩展性优势，可作为TV-SO的非参数求解范式。

Abstract: Stochastic optimization (SO) plays a central role in decision-making under uncertainty. Among SO problems, time-varying stochastic optimization (TV-SO) is particularly important due to its applications in adaptive control and machine learning. Non-parametric approaches have been proposed for time-varying deterministic optimization; however, they have not been developed for their stochastic counterparts. This work addresses that gap by developing a stochastic variational framework based on Malliavin calculus. This framework yields non-parametric optimality conditions for SO problems with stochastic decisions and supports the design of a scalable deep-learning algorithm that is insensitive to the parameterization dimension. This algorithm, called the Stochastic Path Follower (SPF), is applied to two important problems under distribution drift, namely least-squares recovery and logistic regression. Experimental results show that the proposed approach outperforms state-of-the-art learning-based and gradient-based methods in both performance and scalability.

</details>


### [173] [Process Reward Modeling with Reliability- and Conservatism-Aware Bayesian Belief](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23223)
*Zhongjian Qiao,Zhixiang Zhou,Jiafei Lyu,Peisong Wang,Qiaosheng Zhang,Siyang Gao,Shuang Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BayesianPRM，将过程奖励建模为随机变量并在离散化的奖励假设空间上维护贝叶斯信念，通过可靠性感知信念更新与保守性感知信念校准分别应对 PRM 不确定性与策略不确定性，期望值即过程奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PRM 在 LLM 推理的测试时扩展中居核心地位，但其过程奖励可能不准。作者识别出两类不确定性：一是监督数据不足与 PRM 训练不完善带来的 PRM 不确定性；二是收集监督数据的策略与测试时扩展所用策略不匹配导致的策略不确定性，进而引起系统性奖励高估。既有工作未能联合考虑二者。

Method: 在奖励假设空间内将过程奖励视为随机变量并维护贝叶斯信念；因连续假设空间上的精确贝叶斯推断不可解，用奖励集成（reward ensemble）离散化假设空间，并引入两种互补的信念更新：(1) 可靠性感知信念更新，衡量各集成成员对监督数据的解释程度，用由 ELBO 优化的摊销信念网络更新信念，针对 PRM 不确定性；(2) 保守性感知信念校准，将信念向低奖励重新加权以抑制高估，针对策略不确定性。最终以该信念的期望确定过程奖励。

Result: 在多模态推理基准上，BayesianPRM 稳定给出更准确的过程奖励估计，同时提升测试时扩展性能。

Conclusion: 联合建模 PRM 不确定性与策略不确定性的贝叶斯过程奖励框架可有效缓解奖励不可靠与高估问题，兼具奖励准确性与测试时扩展收益。

Abstract: Process Reward Models (PRMs) play a central role in test-time scaling for large language model (LLM) reasoning. However, PRMs may provide inaccurate process rewards for test-time scaling, which we identify as arising from two sources of uncertainty: (1) \textit{PRM uncertainty} that arises from insufficient supervision data and imperfect PRM training; and (2) \textit{policy uncertainty} that results from the mismatch between the policy to collect supervision data and the policy for test-time scaling, leading to systematic reward overestimation. Previous studies fail to jointly consider both uncertainties. To bridge this gap, we propose BayesianPRM, which treats the process reward as a random variable within a reward hypothesis space and maintains a Bayesian belief over it. Since exact Bayesian inference over the continuous hypothesis space is generally intractable, we discretize the reward hypothesis space with a reward ensemble and introduce two complementary belief updates, each targeting one source of uncertainty: (1) to address PRM uncertainty, we introduce \textit{reliability-aware belief updating} that measures how well each ensemble member explains the supervision data and updates the belief with an amortized belief network optimized by the evidence lower bound (ELBO); and (2) to tackle policy uncertainty, we propose \textit{conservatism-aware belief calibration} which further reweights the belief toward lower rewards to suppress overestimation. These two updates yield a reliability- and conservatism-aware Bayesian belief, whose expectation determines the process reward. Experiments on multimodal reasoning benchmarks indicate that BayesianPRM consistently yields more accurate process reward estimates while enhancing test-time scaling performance.

</details>


### [174] [Response-Informed Value Estimation for Cost-Aware Multi-Turn LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23239)
*Haotian Yan,Jiaqi Liu,Bingyi Jing,Tianjun Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RIVET：利用"调用后响应证据"训练、但部署阶段仍保持严格"调用前"决策的多轮LLM路由价值估计方法，在MINT上以更低成本取得更高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮交互中路由需在每轮响应产生前选模型，而任务成功与否只在交互结束时才揭晓，导致中间每一步选择缺乏自身标签，信用分配困难；但日志轨迹中保留了每次调用实际产生的响应，蕴含"该轮需要什么、所选模型完成得如何"的信息，这部分信息被仅基于最终结果的方法所浪费。

Method: 提出RIVET（Response-Informed Value Estimation for Turn-Level Routing）。训练阶段同时训练两个共享状态模型表示的价值估计器：(1) 可部署的 Routing Value Estimator，仅用调用前信息为候选模型打分，保证部署时严格前调用；(2) 仅训练期使用的 Response-Informed Value Estimator，额外观测已实现响应。以响应为条件把响应引起的回报波动由残差噪声转为可观测协变量，并从理论上证明该做法降低了共享表示的主导渐近估计误差。

Result: 在MINT基准上，RIVET在留出任务上的求解数超过最强多轮基线，同时总调用成本降低74.7%。

Conclusion: 训练期引入响应信息、部署期保持前调用约束的双估计器设计，能有效缓解多轮路由的信用分配与估计误差问题，实现成功率与成本的更优权衡。

Abstract: Multi-turn LLM routing selects a model at every turn before its response is available, yet task success is revealed only when the interaction ends, leaving each intermediate choice without a label of its own. Logged trajectories, however, retain the response each call produced, which exposes what each turn required and how well the chosen model served it, evidence that outcomes alone hide. We introduce RIVET (Response-Informed Value Estimation for Turn-Level Routing), which exploits this post-call evidence during training while keeping routing strictly pre-call at deployment. RIVET jointly trains two value estimators over a shared state-model representation: a deployable Routing Value Estimator that scores candidate models from pre-call information, and a training-only Response-Informed Value Estimator that additionally observes the realized response. Conditioning on the response turns response-induced return variation from residual noise into an observed covariate, and we show that this reduces the leading asymptotic estimation error of the shared representation. On the MINT benchmark, RIVET solves more held-out tasks than the strongest multi-turn baseline while using 74.7% less total call cost.

</details>


### [175] [Anisotropic Representation Dynamics in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23242)
*Nguyen Do,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"混合门控的各向异性表示动力学"理论，将自注意力分解为值传输、查询门控与键门控，揭示注意力权重变化可使本应收缩的表示差异转为扩张，并给出谱判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表示差异在穿过Transformer层时可能收缩或放大，影响信息保真度与扰动敏感性；但何时注意力权重的变化会把"收缩"翻转为"扩张"仍不清楚。

Method: 将自注意力拆分为value transport、query gating、key gating三部分；分析被关注表示的几何结构如何约束门控，以及机制间交互如何逆转value transport引起的收缩；形式化为"门控诱发的机制切换"，推导充要谱判据以认证切换存在性并直接确定切换方向；进一步刻画最大相对切换强度、可被克服的收缩严重程度，以及全方向切换子空间的最大维数。

Result: 在四种Transformer架构上通过解析与有限前向比较验证预测，揭示存在独立与联合的query–key门控机制及非平凡切换子空间。

Conclusion: 工作从机制与谱角度阐明注意力路由如何改变局部表示动力学，为理解Transformer内部信息保持与扰动敏感性提供理论工具。

Abstract: Small differences between internal representations can shrink or grow as they pass through a Transformer layer, affecting how information is preserved and how sensitive the model is to perturbations. Yet it remains unclear when changes in attention weights can turn a representation difference that would otherwise shrink into one that grows. We develop a \textbf{Mixture-Gated Theory of Anisotropic Representation Dynamics} that separates self-attention into value transport, query gating, and key gating. Our analysis reveals how the geometry of attended representations constrains gating and how interactions between these mechanisms can reverse contraction induced by value transport. We formalize this phenomenon as gating-induced regime switching, where a representation difference contracts under fixed attention routing but expands under full attention. We derive a necessary and sufficient spectral criterion that certifies the existence or absence of switching and identifies switching directions without exhaustive search. Beyond existence, our theory characterizes maximum relative switching strength, certifies the severity of contraction that gating can overcome, and determines the largest dimension of a subspace in which every nonzero direction switches. Experiments across four Transformer architectures validate these predictions through analytical and finite-forward comparisons, revealing distinct and joint query--key gating mechanisms and nontrivial switching subspaces. Our results provide a mechanistic and spectral understanding of how attention routing can alter local representation dynamics.

</details>


### [176] [FLARE: Physics-Grounded Evaluation of Language-Model Agents for Feeder Restoration with Degraded Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23251)
*BHVSP Subrahmanyam,Neeraj Choudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FLARE——面向配电故障隔离后馈线恢复的物理约束基准，含800个AC可行案例、5种观测设置；评测5个指令微调模型共78,750个回合，平均归一化遗憾0.082–0.148，中位遗憾为0但P95达0.814–1.000，影子回放显示96.4%被拒动作会造成物理违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网故障后恢复供电需在信息不完整且相互冲突的条件下做决策，同时必须满足电气约束；现有缺乏可复现、物理一致的评测基准来检验语言模型在此类恢复任务中的表现与失效模式。

Method: 构建FLARE（Feeder-Level Agent Restoration Evaluation）：一个基于物理约束的故障隔离后馈线恢复基准。包含800个AC可行案例、覆盖5种观测设置，允许智能体主动收集证据、评估开关状态并在固定运行限值下执行恢复动作；对5个指令微调模型进行78,750个回合评测，并引入拓扑、分布式电源与负荷变化场景，配合guard拒动作的影子回放分析。

Result: 平均归一化遗憾为0.082–0.148；所有模型中位遗憾均为0，但P95遗憾达0.814–1.000，揭示显著的长尾失败；影子回放表明15,706个被guard拒绝的动作中有96.4%本会导致物理违规。

Conclusion: FLARE提供了基于语言模型的恢复辅助决策的可复现评测手段，说明外部安全校验层不可或缺：guard可能过度保守但极少放行真实违规，需在保留安全约束的同时缓解长尾失败与信息不完整带来的风险。

Abstract: Restoring service after distribution-grid faults requires decisions from incomplete and conflicting information while respecting electrical constraints. Addressing this need, we introduce FLARE (Feeder-Level Agent Restoration Evaluation), a physics-grounded benchmark for post-isolation feeder restoration. FLARE con- tains 800 AC-feasible cases across five observation settings and allows agents to gather evidence, evaluate switches, and perform restoration actions under fixed operational limits. We evaluate five instruction-tuned models over 78,750 episodes, including topology, distributed-generation, and loading shifts. Mean normalized regret ranges from 0.082 to 0.148. Although every model has zero median regret, P95 regret ranges from 0.814 to 1.000, revealing an important failure tail. Shadow replay shows that 96.4% of 15,706 guard-rejected actions would have caused a physical violation. FLARE enables reproducible evaluation of language-model based restoration support while emphasizing the need for external safety checks.

</details>


### [177] [Sample, Mix, or Verify? Allocating Test-Time Compute under Imperfect Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23255)
*Srinivasan Iyengar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPLIT：基于小型标注试点数据，用精确公式与经验贝叶斯在推理时为采样/模型选择/验证预算做最优分配，七项基准上接近事后最优（差0.02–0.8个百分点），显著优于所有固定规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时扩展（多采样+验证器择优）是提升准确率的常见手段，但其成功依赖"存在正确答案"与"验证器选中正确答案"两点。前者随样本数可预测增长，后者不可，因为真实验证器（单元测试、多数投票、LLM评审、奖励模型）会接受错答、拒绝正答且错误相关。固定预算下需决定采样数、来源模型与验证开销，现有做法多为经验规则。

Method: 提出SPLIT：从少量标注试点中规划预算。核心是一个精确公式，刻画在accept/reject与top-score两种选择机制下提交正确答案的概率，建模了任务难度差异、跨模型共享盲点及重复验证调用的相关性。用经验贝叶斯拟合该公式，并据此对未见任务规划预算。

Result: 在数学、科学QA、代码与软件工程智能体共7个基准、26个模型、175个智能体系统、6类真实验证器（均来自公开发布）上，SPLIT与事后最优分配的差距仅0.02–0.8个百分点，而所有测试的固定规则在某些设置下至少损失2.5个百分点。遗憾仅取决于SPLIT对配置的排序质量，在计算、价格与延迟成本下以及在线场景中均近最优。公式还解释了何时增加LLM评审会降低精度；作为远超试点的预测器，在验证器饱和限制成功时优于幂律拟合，并报告了其失效情形。

Conclusion: SPLIT将推理时计算分配从经验规则转为基于试点数据的可计算规划，理论上有精确公式支撑，实证上跨多领域、多模型、多验证器接近事后最优，并可推广到成本敏感与在线设置，同时刻画了LLM评审数量与精度的非单调关系及方法失效边界。

Abstract: A common way to make language models and agents more accurate is to spend more compute at inference time: generate many candidate solutions and submit the one a verifier prefers. Success then requires that some candidate is correct and that the verifier picks it. The first grows predictably with the number of samples; the second does not, because real verifiers (unit tests, majority votes, LLM judges, reward models) accept wrong answers, reject right ones, and make correlated mistakes. With a fixed budget, a practitioner must decide how many samples to draw, which models to draw them from, and how much to spend on verification, and today these choices are made by rules of thumb. We introduce SPLIT, which makes them from a small labelled pilot. It rests on an exact formula for the probability of submitting a correct answer under accept/reject and top-score selection that accounts for varying task difficulty, blind spots shared across models, and correlated repeated verifier calls. SPLIT fits the formula by empirical Bayes and uses it to plan budgets on unseen tasks. Across seven benchmarks spanning math, science QA, code and software-engineering agents, 26 models, 175 agent systems and six kinds of real verifiers, all from public releases, SPLIT lands within 0.02-0.8 percentage points of the best allocation in hindsight, while every fixed rule we test loses at least 2.5 points in some setting. Regret depends only on how well SPLIT ranks configurations, and it stays near-optimal under compute, price and latency costs and online. The formula also explains when asking more LLM judges lowers precision. As a forecaster far beyond the pilot, SPLIT beats power-law fitting when the verifier caps success, and we report where it loses.

</details>


### [178] [ADT: All-Atom Diffusion Tokenizer for 3D Molecular Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23265)
*Xuantuo Huang,Encheng Su,Aoran Wang,Pan Tan,Shengyong Xu,Yizhou Wang,Chen Tang,Shixiang Tang,Mingxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出全原子扩散分词器（ADT），通过解耦分词粒度与重建分辨率，用紧凑离散结构token实现全原子分子重建与自回归生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散结构分词器存在"紧凑表示"与"全原子重建"之间的权衡：原子级token保留原子细节但序列过长，残基级蛋白质token紧凑却通常丢失侧链几何信息。

Method: ADT将token粒度与重建分辨率解耦：小分子每个原子一个结构token，蛋白质每个残基一个token且联合表示主链与侧链几何；为缓解量化带来的几何失真，利用扩散模型的生成能力，从离散token恢复细粒度原子坐标，超越确定性解码。

Result: 在小分子与蛋白质上的大量实验表明，ADT能从紧凑离散表示实现强重建，同时支持高质量的小分子与全原子蛋白质结构的自回归生成。

Conclusion: 紧凑结构token可作为全原子分子重建与生成的有效离散接口。

Abstract: Discrete structural tokenization provides a promising route to autoregressive 3D molecular generation. Yet, existing tokenizers face a trade-off between compact representation and all-atom reconstruction: atom-level tokens preserve atomic details but produce long sequences, while compact residue-level protein tokens typically omit side-chain geometry. To overcome this limitation, we propose All-atom Diffusion Tokenizer (ADT), which decouples token granularity from reconstruction resolution. ADT uses one structural token per atom for small molecules and one token per protein residue that jointly represents backbone and side-chain geometry. To mitigate geometric distortion introduced by quantization, we leverage the generative capacity of diffusion models to recover fine-grained atomic geometry beyond deterministic decoding from discrete tokens. Extensive experiments across small molecules and proteins demonstrate that ADT achieves strong reconstruction from compact discrete representations while enabling high-quality autoregressive generation of both small molecules and all-atom protein structures. These results establish compact structural tokens as an effective discrete interface for all-atom molecular reconstruction and generation.

</details>


### [179] [Beyond Marginal Influence: Interaction-Aware Memory Selection for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23266)
*BHVSP Subrahmanyam,Neeraj Choudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究“逐决策记忆充分性”：选取小子集以保留全记忆行为，提出 JointCoreBench 基准与 JointCore 组删除基线，显著降低评估次数与活跃记忆占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆选择依赖记录相关性或单条影响力排序，在证据冗余或联合必要条件下无法识别充分子集，且难以区分搜索误差与模型误差。

Method: 构建 JointCoreBench（9 类交互族、6 个工作流域、自然语言决策、精确充分集 oracle）；提出 JointCore——按相关性成本排序的组删除基线，先检验记录块再检验单条记录；与成本感知的单条删除方法在 4 个 12B–70B 模型家族、每模型 432 任务上对比，并用物理删除、UNKNOWN 掩码、PENDING 哨兵、反事实值与替代标签做界面依赖测试。

Result: 在 6,912 个精确条件下，成本排序 JointCore 100.0% 恢复最小成本充分集，子集评估次数由 30.00 降至 11.13（62.9%），活跃记忆节省 87.8%；跨模型未加权均值 10.28 次查询（对照 16.00），最小成本恢复率 50.6%（对照 43.1%）；确定性主界面下两法均保持全记忆行为，其他界面下出现行为依赖。

Conclusion: JointCore 是高效的基准基线，但该研究未确立可直接部署的记忆压缩方案。

Abstract: LLM-agent memory selection often uses retrieval rankings based on record relevance or individual influence. Such rankings cannot identify sufficient subsets under redundant or jointly necessary evidence. We study per-decision memory sufficiency: selecting a small subset that preserves full-memory behavior. To separate search error from model error, we introduce JointCoreBench, a benchmark with nine interaction families, six workflow domains, natural-language decisions, and exact sufficient-set oracles, and JointCore, a relevance cost-ordered group-deletion baseline that tests blocks before individual records. On 6,912 exact conditions, cost-ordered JointCore recovers a minimum-cost sufficient set in 100.0% of cases, reduces subset evaluations from 30.00 to 11.13 (62.9%), and achieves 87.8% active-memory savings. Across four 12B-70B model families and 432 tasks per model, its unweighted mean is 10.28 versus 16.00 model queries and 50.6% versus 43.1% minimum-cost recovery relative to cost aware singleton deletion. Both methods preserve full-memory behavior under the deterministic primary interface. Tests with physical deletion, UNKNOWN masks, PENDING sentinels, counterfactual values, and alternative labels identify interface dependent behavior. JointCore is an efficient benchmark baseline; the study does not establish deployment-ready memory compression.

</details>


### [180] [DeDiCa: A Scalable Deconfounding Discrete Causal Generative Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23272)
*Kunchangtai Liang,Adrián Javaloy,Antonio Vergari,Isabel Valera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeDiCa 用张量化参数化的因果贝叶斯网络处理离散变量与隐混杂下的因果推断：在观测数据上以最大似然一次训练、对隐混杂精确边缘化，随后可毫秒级精确估计任意（代理）可识别的干预查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散变量加隐混杂场景下的干预查询需求普遍，但现有神经因果生成模型与因果基础模型在推理效率、显存占用、样本扩展性与推断精确性之间存在权衡：前者常需昂贵训练与近似推断，后者在增加观测样本时扩展性有限且推理开销大。

Method: 回顾经典概率建模，提出 DeDiCa 框架：以张量化参数化表示因果贝叶斯网络，使推断可处理；给定因果图，在观测数据上以最大似然训练一次，并对隐混杂变量做精确边缘化；训练后无需重训即可估计任意（代理）可识别的干预查询，并支持内存高效的 CPU 训练与精确干预推断。

Result: 在 ATE 估计上达到与最先进神经因果生成模型相当的水平；支持内存高效的 CPU 训练与毫秒级精确干预推断；相较因果基础模型，随额外观测样本增加扩展更有效，并在显著更低的推理时间与内存下取得有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: 尽管参数化刻意简单，DeDiCa 为含隐混杂的离散场景提供了一种可处理、准确且可扩展的因果推断途径，在精度、效率与扩展性之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: We introduce DeDiCa, a practical framework for efficient causal inference with discrete variables and hidden confounding that revisits classical probabilistic modeling through a tensorized parameterization of causal Bayesian networks, enabling tractable inference. Given a causal graph, DeDiCa is trained once on observational data by maximum likelihood, exactly marginalizing over hidden confounders, and can then estimate any (proxy-)identifiable interventional query. Despite this deliberately simple parameterization, DeDiCa matches state-of-the-art neural causal generative models in ATE estimation, while supporting memory-efficient CPU training and exact interventional inference in milliseconds. Compared with causal foundation models, DeDiCa scales more effectively with additional observational samples and achieves competitive accuracy at substantially lower inference time and memory. DeDiCa provides a tractable, accurate, and scalable approach to causal inference in discrete settings with hidden confounding.

</details>


### [181] [Data-Efficient Classifier Pretraining Through Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23289)
*Qinyu Li,Yee Whye Teh,Chris Holmes,Joel Jaskari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DiffPT：用扩散模型重构式预训练分类骨干，在低数据场景下对医学影像与时间序列任务优于自监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小数据下深度网络易过拟合；AlexNet 之前基于自编码器/去噪自编码器的重构式预训练在训练集有限时有收益，作者希望以现代扩散建模复兴这一思路，用于领域特定的小样本分类器预训练。

Method: DiffPT，一种简单的基于扩散建模的重构式预训练方法，对给定分类骨干网络进行预训练；在时间序列、标准自然图像基准和医学影像数据上实验，并与多种现代与传统无监督/自监督方法对比。

Result: CIFAR-10 与 Tiny ImageNet 低数据场景下对比式预训练更优；DiffPT 在医学影像与时间序列任务的多档低数据设定中显著优于自监督基线。

Conclusion: DiffPT 是一种简单有效的、面向不同模态的数据高效分类器预训练方法，在领域特定小数据上尤其具优势。

Abstract: Machine learning with limited data is susceptible to overfitting, especially with modern deep neural networks. Prior to the introduction of AlexNet, reconstruction-based pretraining with auto-encoder and denoising auto-encoder were found to be beneficial, partially due to the limited size of the training sets at the time. Motivated by these traditional approaches, we propose DiffPT, a simple diffusion modeling approach to pretrain a given classifier backbone for limited domain-specific training data. We experiment on time-series data, standard natural-image benchmarks, and medical imaging datasets, and contrast the diffusion approach to a multitude of modern and traditional un/self-supervised methods. While contrastive pretraining performs better on CIFAR-10 and Tiny ImageNet in low-data regimes, DiffPT is particularly effective on medical imaging and time-series tasks, where it outperforms self-supervised baselines across a range of low-data regimes. Our results suggest that DiffPT is a simple and effective approach for data-efficient classifier pretraining on different modalities.

</details>


### [182] [Untrained CNN and coefficient-space regularization for joint reconstruction in photoacoustic tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23315)
*Alfio Bozi,Anwesa Dey,Souvik Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种正则化数据驱动框架，在有限维小波空间中表示未知场，用未训练CNN生成的固定非线性特征通过Tikhonov正则最小二乘重建初始压力系数，再用学习到的系数空间先验推断声速平方系数，实现光声断层成像中初始压力与空间变化声速的联合重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光声断层成像中初始压力与声速同时未知，联合重建是欠定逆问题；常规监督训练CNN方法计算成本高、泛化与噪声鲁棒性有限，需要更高效稳健的重建方案。

Method: 未知场在有限维小波空间中展开；采用未训练CNN产生的固定随机卷积特征，通过Tikhonov正则化最小二乘重建初始压力系数；随后利用学习的系数空间先验推断声速平方系数；并在冻结的重建映射上测试噪声稳健性与泛化性。

Result: 在留出数据上实现准确重建，对未见模体几何具有强泛化能力；冻结重建映射在训练未覆盖的测量噪声下保持稳定，噪声水平达10%时精度仅轻微下降；相比本工作中的监督训练CNN方法，重建误差显著更小，计算时间约为其六分之一。

Conclusion: 固定随机卷积特征结合正则化系数空间学习，为光声断层成像联合重建提供了一种高效且稳健的方法。

Abstract: The joint reconstruction of the initial pressure and spatially varying sound speed in photoacoustic tomography from boundary measurements is considered. A regularized data-driven framework is proposed in which the unknown fields are represented in finite-dimensional wavelet spaces and fixed nonlinear features generated by an untrained convolutional neural network (CNN) are used to reconstruct the initial-pressure coefficients through Tikhonov-regularized least squares. A learned coefficient-space prior is subsequently used to infer the squared sound-speed coefficients. Numerical experiments demonstrate accurate reconstruction on held-out data and strong generalization to previously unseen phantom geometries. The frozen reconstruction map also remains stable under measurement noise not represented during training, with only minor changes in reconstruction accuracy for noise levels up to $10\%$. In comparison with the supervised trained-CNN approach considered in this work, the untrained representation yields substantially smaller reconstruction errors while requiring approximately one-sixth of the computational time. These results demonstrate that fixed random convolutional features combined with regularized coefficient-space learning provide an efficient and robust approach to joint reconstruction in photoacoustic tomography.

</details>


### [183] [Peak-Memory Schedules You Can Trust: Exact Realization, Certified Optimality, and the Limits of Recomputation for Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23328)
*Ivan Fomin,Ivan Koryabkin,Boris Sheludko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于对已生成程序进行语句级重排的内存优化方法：精确建模峰值内存、以最小割给出的正反向"trap"下界证明最优性，绝大多数推理图已被最优调度；分支网络与选择性重计算仍有收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小设备上的实时推理需要低激活内存且不增加延迟，因此算子顺序（operator order）是最有价值的省内存手段——它在时间上是免费的。但常规做法（把更好的顺序交回编译器重新生成代码）并不可靠。

Method: 实证表明 PyTorch Inductor 重生成程序经常达不到预测峰值（54 个 CPU 配置中 22 个失配、9 个劣于原程序；GPU 50 个中 19 个劣化且数值结果改变）。改为对生成程序做 profile，依据其自身语句及读写效果精确建模内存，再置换这些语句；证明在分配不依赖历史时能逐字节实现所建模的峰值且结果按位一致。给出正向与反向"trap"下界，各可用 O(n^2) 最小割计算。另研究带逐算子上限的选择性重计算，分析其复杂度。

Result: 重排在所有 CPU 运行中按字节达到建模峰值且输出按位一致；GPU 上刻画了缓存分配器破坏该条件的原因。trap 下界在 94% 的留出 wrapper 图和全部 16 个微控制器级模型上于 1 秒内认证最优。分支网络受益：DeepLabV3 上节省 10.0%，编译训练最多节省 7.6%。选择性重计算对每个固定上限均强 NP-hard（即使在树宽为二），在串并联图上精确编码背包问题，在受限树宽上可精确求解；对峰值为单层的公开微型模型无效，但在 ReDimNet 说话人网络上峰值降低 5.2–6.1%，额外 MAC 不到 0.4%，输出按位一致。

Conclusion: 语句级重排能可靠、精确地兑现顺序带来的内存收益，且大多数推理图经 trap 下界已证最优；优化空间主要集中于分支网络与训练。带上限的选择性重计算理论上困难，但在"廉价值晚期复用"的架构（如 ReDimNet）上仍有可观收益。

Abstract: Real-time inference on small devices needs low activation memory without extra latency, which makes operator order the most valuable memory lever: it is free in time. We show that the usual way of deploying a better order, handing it back to the compiler and regenerating code, is unreliable. In PyTorch Inductor the regenerated program misses its predicted peak in 22 of 54 CPU configurations. It is worse than the stock program in 9 of them and in 19 of 50 on a GPU, and on the GPU it also changes numerical results. We instead profile the generated program, model its memory exactly from its own statements and their read/write effects, and permute those statements. We prove that this realizes the modeled peak whenever allocation does not depend on history. It does so to the byte, with bitwise-identical results, in all our CPU runs. On the GPU we characterize why the caching allocator breaks the condition. To know when to stop, we give forward and reverse "trap" lower bounds, each computable with $O(n^2)$ minimum cuts. They certify the found order optimal for 94% of held-out wrapper graphs and all 16 microcontroller-class models we study, in under a second. So most inference graphs are already scheduled optimally. Branching networks are the exception, where reordering is free and pays, e.g. 10.0% on DeepLabV3. In compiled training we save up to 7.6%. Beyond the certified optimum we study selective recomputation with per-operator caps. It is strongly NP-hard for every fixed cap even at treewidth two, encodes knapsack exactly on series-parallel graphs, and is solvable exactly on bounded treewidth. It is useless on public tiny models whose peak is a single layer, but on architectures that hold a cheap value for a late reuse it pays: on the public ReDimNet speaker networks, whose order is already optimal, it lowers the peak by 5.2-6.1% at under 0.4% extra MACs, with bitwise-identical outputs.

</details>


### [184] [HiPredict: Predicting the missing HiRISE RED4 spectral channel with machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23335)
*Ardan Suphi,Ben Moseley,Gareth S Collins,Valentin T Bickel,Vidhya G Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出HiPredict——一个基于U-Net的机器学习框架，利用IR10、BG12、邻近红通道影像及采集元数据重建失效的RED4探测器影像，测试集归一化平均绝对误差仅1.1%，显著优于现有SYN4合成方案（3.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HiRISE的红外-红-蓝绿三通道彩色影像对火星成分变化识别和活动地表过程研究至关重要。2023年焦平面中央RED4探测器间歇性失效，导致影像出现缺口、彩色成像能力减半。现有处理流程用线性缩放IR10得到合成红通道SYN4，但无法刻画通道间的非线性关系，精度与应用性受限。

Method: 构建HiPredict机器学习框架：以同位置IR10与BG12影像、邻近红通道观测及采集元数据为输入，采用U-Net架构学习输入与RED4之间的非线性时空-光谱关系；训练集81,717个样本（对应13,625次观测），在23,348个样本（3,892次失效前观测）上测试。

Result: HiPredict重建RED4的归一化平均绝对误差为1.1%，显著优于SYN4的3.1%；重建产品改善彩色影像恢复效果，更真实地呈现冰霜陨石坑、山脊顶部出露的地层及氯化物沉积等目标特征。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了机器学习在行星任务老化、仪器自然退化背景下缓解数据丢失的潜力，并以开源软件形式发布HiPredict工具。

Abstract: The High Resolution Imaging Science Experiment (HiRISE) onboard the Mars Reconnaissance Orbiter (MRO) acquires imagery of Mars at 25--30 cm/pixel, supporting studies of martian geology, climate history, and landing-site characterization. Its 14-detector focal plane assembly includes several overlapping detectors that enable the acquisition of three-channel colour-infrared images, which are important for identifying compositional variations and studying active surface processes. In 2023, a detector in the center of the focal plane assembly (RED4) began failing intermittently, creating a gap in acquired imagery and reducing the instrument's colour-imaging capability by half. The HiRISE processing pipeline provides a synthetic red (SYN4) channel obtained by linearly scaling the co-located infrared (IR10) channel. However, this approach cannot capture observed non-linear relationships between the colour channels, limiting its accuracy and applicability. Here, we present HiPredict, a machine-learning framework that reconstructs RED4 from co-located IR10 and blue-green (BG12) imagery, neighbouring red-channel observations, and acquisition metadata. The model employs a U-Net architecture to learn non-linear spatiotemporal-spectral relationships between these inputs and is trained on 81,717 training samples, representing 13,625 observations. Evaluated on 23,348 test samples, representing 3,892 pre-failure observations, HiPredict reconstructs RED4 with a Normalised Mean Absolute Error of 1.1%, substantially outperforming SYN4 (3.1%). The reconstructed products improve colour image recovery and more closely represent features of interest such as icy craters, stratigraphy exposed along ridge crests and chloride deposits. We release HiPredict as an open software tool. This work demonstrates the potential of machine learning to mitigate data loss as planetary missions age and naturally degrade.

</details>


### [185] [Minimax-optimal Scaling Laws in Ridge Regression via Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23337)
*Yalda Shabanzadeh,Diyuan Wu,Marco Mondelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究预训练+微调的两步迁移学习能否在缩放律意义下带来实质性性能提升，在高维岭回归原型设定下给出了风险的确定性等价，并识别出达到极小极大最优指数的参数区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型常被复用于下游任务，但迁移学习是否能在缩放律（scaling law）层面带来显著性能提升尚缺乏理论刻画。作者选取高维回归这一原型设定，以严格分析迁移过程的风险与最优缩放指数。

Method: 设定为：(i) 源模型通过岭回归预训练；(ii) 目标模型以源估计的 L2 距离作为正则项进行微调。在模型偏移与协方差偏移下建立该两步流程风险的确定性等价（deterministic equivalent）；再将其特化到共享回归函数与可交换幂律协方差谱的情形，识别出缩放律达到极小极大最优指数的区间，并分析用单遍随机梯度下降（one-pass SGD）执行微调的等价性；辅以合成实验与语言模型实验。

Result: 得到了两步流程风险的确定性等价表达式；在共享回归函数、可交换幂律谱下确定了一个缩放律达到极小极大最优指数的参数区间；该区间内最优源正则化仅依赖源数据，因而对所有目标任务具有普适性；单遍 SGD 微调可达到相同的优率。

Conclusion: 迁移学习在适当参数区间内确实能在缩放律上取得极小极大最优指数，且最优源正则化具有跨目标的普适性，并可由计算高效的 one-pass SGD 实现；理论结论经合成数据与语言模型实验支持。

Abstract: Pretrained models are often reused across downstream tasks, motivating the question of whether this transfer learning procedure can provide a substantial performance improvement, namely, in terms of the scaling law. We address this question in the prototypical setting of high-dimensional regression, where (i) a pretrained \emph{source} model is trained via ridge regression, and (ii) a fine-tuned \emph{target} model is regularized via the $L_2$ distance from the source estimator. First, we establish a deterministic equivalent for the risk of this two-step procedure under both model and covariance shift. Next, by specializing the deterministic equivalent to a shared regression function and commuting power-law covariance spectra, we identify a regime where the resulting scaling law achieves the minimax-optimal exponent. Notably, in this regime, the optimal source regularization depends on source data only, indicating its universality across all targets. Furthermore, we show that the same optimal rate is achieved by performing the fine-tuning step via one-pass stochastic gradient descent. We support our theoretical analysis via experiments on both synthetic settings and language models.

</details>


### [186] [SETAREH Transform: A Sorting-Assisted Haar-Wavelet Sparsification Method for Approximate Inner-Product Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23338)
*Seyed Kamaledin Setarehdan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Haar小波的稀疏化变换SETAREH，用于近似固定高维系数向量与连续输入向量的内积，在对两向量施加相同的置换与Haar变换后截断保留部分系数-输入对，从而降低表示成本并引入可控近似误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注通过剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏和高效架构压缩神经网络模型规模，而重复出现的高维输入向量在存储、内存流量和通信上的开销尚缺少针对性处理，这在分布式与边缘计算场景尤为突出。

Method: SETAREH变换：对固定系数向量与输入向量施加同一序列的置换与Haar小波变换以保持截断前的内积精确性，随后仅保留一部分变换后的系数-输入对，实现可控误差的近似表示。评估包含合成高斯向量基线与APPA-REAL数据集上CNN表观年龄估计的真实任务。

Result: SETAREH可用远少于原始向量维度的变换系数-输入对近似所评估的内积，在合成基线与真实任务上均得到验证。

Conclusion: SETAREH可作为固定权重下重复内积的一种潜在结构化数据表示方法，其实际收益取决于近似精度以及置换元数据的存储/传输成本。

Abstract: Modern deep learning systems increasingly rely on high-dimensional vector operations, in which large input and parameter vectors are repeatedly stored, transferred, and processed. Although substantial research has focused on reducing neural-network model size through pruning, quantization, knowledge distillation, and efficient architectures, the storage, memory-traffic, and communication costs associated with repeatedly presented high-dimensional input vectors remain important practical considerations, particularly in distributed and edge-computing environments. This paper proposes the SETAREH (Sorting-assisted Energy-concentrating Transform for Approximate Representation using Haar wavelets) transform, a Haar-wavelet-based sparsification approach for approximating the inner product between a fixed high-dimensional coefficient vector and successive input vectors. By applying the same sequence of permutations and Haar transforms to both vectors, SETAREH preserves the exact inner product before truncation. It then retains only a subset of the transformed coefficient-input pairs, reducing their representation cost while introducing a controllable approximation error. To evaluate SETAREH, we consider two complementary experimental settings: a synthetic Gaussian-vector baseline and a real apparent-age estimation task using a convolutional neural network on the APPA-REAL dataset. The results show that SETAREH can approximate the evaluated inner products using substantially fewer transformed coefficient-input pairs than the original vector dimension. The findings support its use as a potential structured data-representation method for repeated inner products with fixed weights, while the practical benefit depends on approximation accuracy and the cost of storing or transmitting permutation metadata.

</details>


### [187] [How Finite-Rank Bottlenecks Shape the Low-Rank Adaptation Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23343)
*Long Nguyen-Chi,Quynh Nguyen,Thanh Nguyen-Cung,Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在冻结骨干网络的前提下，指出损失仅通过由冻结激活诱导的有限秩线性映射"看到"各适配权重矩阵，由此用迹范数正则解释LoRA低秩解的理论来源，并给出非凸与泛化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA已成为参数高效微调的标准方法，但"低秩更新为何足够"的理论解释不完整；现有分析多依赖NTK线性化假设，本文希望在无需线性化的条件下给出理论刻画。

Method: 关键观察：骨干冻结后，每个适配权重矩阵仅通过有限秩线性映射作用于损失；迹范数正则迫使优化器在产生相同输出的矩阵中选取低秩解，秩由"瓶颈秩"而非样本量控制。针对多头注意力，利用softmax对分数矩阵行加常数不敏感、值更新仅在注意力加权与冻结输出投影后可观测这两个不变性，得到更紧的秩上界。对带权重衰减的因子化LoRA目标，证明在任意小的通用正半定扰动下，一旦LoRA秩超过由瓶颈秩确定的阈值，每个一阶驻点均秩亏。

Result: 得到全局极小点秩由瓶颈秩控制而非样本量；多头注意力下获得更紧的秩上界；非凸侧证明驻点秩亏现象，并表明扰动目标的全局极小点对原迹范数问题近似最优，同时给出由冻结特征谱结构决定的均匀泛化界。

Conclusion: LoRA的有效性可由冻结激活诱导的有限秩瓶颈与迹范数正则自然解释，无需NTK线性化；该框架统一了全局最优、驻点结构与泛化三个层面的理论保证。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become the standard method for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large pretrained models, yet theoretical explanations for why low-rank updates suffice remain incomplete. Most existing analyses rely on the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) regime, which linearizes the network around the pretrained weights. In this work, we take a different approach that requires no linearization: our key observation is that, once the backbone is frozen, the loss sees each adapted weight matrix only through a finite-rank linear map induced by the frozen activations. Trace-norm regularization then forces the optimizer to pick a low-rank matrix among all matrices that produce the same observed output. This yields global minimizers whose ranks are controlled by the rank of this map, which we call the \emph{bottleneck rank}, rather than the sample size. For multi-head attention, we obtain tighter rank upper bounds by exploiting two additional invariances: row-wise softmax is insensitive to additive row constants in the score matrix, and value updates are observable only after attention weighting and frozen output projection. For the factorized LoRA objective with weight decay, we prove a complementary result on the nonconvex side: after an arbitrarily small generic positive semidefinite perturbation, every first-order stationary point is rank-deficient once the LoRA rank exceeds a threshold determined by the bottleneck rank. Finally, we show that global minimizers of the perturbed objective are near-optimal for the original trace-norm problem, and we provide uniform generalization bounds governed by the spectral structure of the frozen features.

</details>


### [188] [RTSM: Adapting Recursive Models to Multivariate Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23353)
*Georg Tenzing,Anna Tegon,Luca Benini,Thorir Mar Ingolfsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出递归时间序列模型（RTSM），将学习式卷积时间分词器、单状态权重共享细化与池化分类读出结合，在30个UEA数据集上取得72.81%的平均准确率，为17个对比深度模型中最高，比MambaSL高1.60个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列分类需要同时提取时序特征并整合整段序列信息；权重共享的反复细化可在不随深度增加参数的前提下重复处理特征。但将该原理迁移到时间序列上，需同时确定合适的时间表示与潜在状态组织方式，这两点正是本文要研究的设计选择。

Method: RTSM由三部分组成：(1) 可学习的卷积时间分词器（temporal tokenizer）；(2) 单状态、权重共享的递归细化模块；(3) 池化式分类读出层。论文通过对比简单卷积嵌入与所提分词器、单状态与双状态变体进行消融实验。

Result: 在全部30个UEA数据集上，采用基于验证集的配置与检查点选择，RTSM平均准确率达72.81%，在17个评估的深度学习模型中最高，领先MambaSL 1.60个百分点。八个数据集上的消融显示所提分词器优于简单卷积嵌入，单状态模型优于测试的双状态变体；完整分类器平均准确率86.10%，高于单独训练的分词器分类器的84.79%。

Conclusion: 时间编码方式与状态组织是递归架构适配时间序列的关键设计选择；所得分类器在保持有竞争力精度的同时，其共享细化模块的参数量与计算深度无关。

Abstract: Accurate multivariate time-series classification requires extracting temporal features and integrating information across a sequence. Weight-tied refinement offers a way to repeatedly process these features without allocating new parameters at each stage. Applying this principle to time series requires choosing both a temporal representation and a latent-state organization suited to classification. We introduce the Recursive Time-Series Model (RTSM), which combines a learned convolutional temporal tokenizer, single-state weight-tied refinement, and a pooled classification readout. Across all 30 UEA datasets, RTSM achieves 72.81% mean accuracy under validation-based configuration and checkpoint selection, the highest observed mean among the 17 evaluated deep-learning models and 1.60 percentage points above MambaSL. Ablations on eight datasets favor the proposed tokenizer over a simple convolutional embedding and the single-state model over the tested two-state variants. The complete classifier reaches 86.10% mean accuracy versus 84.79% for a separately trained tokenizer-only classifier. These findings identify temporal encoding and state organization as key design choices for adapting recursive architectures to time series. The resulting classifier combines competitive accuracy with a shared refinement module whose parameter count is independent of computational depth.

</details>


### [189] [Forecasting Voltage Time Series under Anomalies Using a WGAN Model With Lipschitz Control and Progressive Kernel Critic Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23394)
*Najmieh Sadat Safarabadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种带渐进Lipschitz惩罚与自适应核的Wasserstein GAN（WGAN-CLP），用于高维电压时间序列的递归预测与流重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归式预测在高维数据上显著增加预测复杂度；序列时序变异性大，导致长期动态难以建模。作者希望改善长期动态建模并解决高维递归预测问题。

Method: 以WASSERSTEIN GAN为基础，引入渐进Lipschitz惩罚（progressive Lipschitz penalty）与自适应核（adaptive kernel）。模型输入数据流的窗口，预测后续取值，进而重建时间序列流，并针对每个新窗口动态调整核。方法上先回顾WGAN-LP公式，再通过自适应核判别器（critic）扩展其框架。

Result: 采用功率比、相关系数（CC）与相对均方根误差（RRMSE）评估。实验表明该模型在准确性与鲁棒性上均优于基准方法，在电压与能源时间序列数据集上预测序列变化表现良好。

Conclusion: WGAN-CLP结合自适应核的框架在电压与能源时间序列的序列变化预测中展现出有前景的性能，可作为高维递归预测的有效方案。

Abstract: In time-series forecasting tasks, the use of recursive forecasting on high-dimensional data substantially increases the complexity of the prediction process. The main challenge is that long-term dynamics becomes harder to model, since the sequences show greater temporal variability. To counteract this limitation and improve the modeling of long-term dynamics, and more importantly, address high-dimensional recursive forecasting, this paper uses a Wasserstein GAN equipped with a progressive Lipschitz penalty and an adaptive kernel. This model is capable of taking a window of the data stream and forecasting the next values, subsequently reconstructing the time-series stream and dynamically adapting its kernel to each new window. The proopsed model is a deep learning framework based on a Wasserstein GAN (WGAN-CLP) with an adaptive kernel to model alterations in streaming voltage time series. To establish the methodological foundation, we review the WGAN-LP formulation another variant model in this class and extend its framework through an adaptive kernel critic model. We then evaluate this extended model using power ratios, correlation coefficient (CC), and relative root mean squared error (RRMSE). The experimental results demonstrate that the proposed model outperforms benchmark methods in both accuracy and robustness. The evaluation shows promising performance of the WGAN-CLP with an adaptive kernel framework in forecasting sequence alterations across the voltage and energy time-series datasets.

</details>


### [190] [Contracting with an Agent under Model Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23402)
*Elif Yılmaz,Jakub Tłuczek,Victor Villin,Christos Dimitrakakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究智能体对环境转移模型存在错误认知情形下的委托-代理契约设计，提出PAMDP框架刻画模型失配下的契约设计并给出诱导理想长期收益的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典委托-代理理论通常假设智能体对环境的信念正确，但实际中智能体可能持有误设的转移模型，从而做出系统性次优决策。委托方需要理解这种模型失配如何影响契约设计，并仍能实现自身长期利益。

Method: 构建委托-代理马尔可夫决策过程（PAMDP）：委托方与持有（可能错误）转移模型的智能体反复交互；委托方掌握真实转移模型，基于智能体的信念与激励约束设计契约，并刻画模型失配下的契约设计性质，发展出一套契约设计框架。

Result: 刻画了模型失配情形下的契约设计特征，并给出框架证明委托方可在智能体对环境理解不准确的情况下诱导出理想的长期收益。

Conclusion: 即使智能体对环境存在不准确认知，委托方仍可通过考虑其信念与激励的契约设计实现自身的长期利益，为误设信念下的委托-代理MDP提供理论与设计框架。

Abstract: In the classical principal-agent setting, the principal delegates some task to the agent, who receives a payment conditioned on the outcome of the task, which in turn benefits the principal. To align their objectives, the principal designs a contract that incentivizes the agent to take actions beneficial to the principal. In practice, however, the agent may act under misspecified beliefs about the underlying environment, leading to systematically suboptimal decisions. In this work, we study this problem through a principal-agent Markov Decision Process (PAMDP), in which the principal repeatedly interacts with an agent whose transition model may differ from the true environment dynamics. The principal has access to the true transition model and designs contracts that account for the agent's belief and incentives. We characterize contract design under model mismatch and develop a framework that enables the principal to induce desirable long-term benefits even though the agent has a potentially inaccurate understanding of the environment.

</details>


### [191] [Information Estimation with Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23404)
*Alberto Foresti,Giulio Franzese,Pietro Michiardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 InfoSEDD，一种基于离散扩散（discrete diffusion）的方法，将信息论估计与生成建模相结合，通过连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）理论计算 KL 散度等互信息类度量，无需"嵌入技巧"，在离散数据上更轻量、可扩展且可复用预训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互信息等信息论度量在刻画随机变量非线性关系上很重要且应用广泛，但在真实离散数据上难以估计。现有方法普遍将离散数据嵌入连续空间再用面向连续分布的神经估计器，需精心设计嵌入模型与估计器架构，且受高维数据问题困扰。

Method: 提出 InfoSEDD：基于离散扩散的生成式框架，借连续时间马尔可夫链理论在离散状态空间上直接建模，并将信息论估计与生成建模桥接，从而可计算 KL 散度；设计轻量、可扩展，并能与预训练模型无缝集成，避免"嵌入技巧"。

Result: 在基因启动子数据的 motif 发现、文本摘要中的语义感知模型选择、Ising 模型熵估计三任务上展示方法通用性；并在真实文本与基因组数据上构建一致性检验，实验表明 InfoSEDD 优于依赖"嵌入技巧"的替代方法。

Conclusion: InfoSEDD 是面向离散数据信息论分析的稳健且可扩展工具。

Abstract: Information-theoretic measures, such as Mutual Information (MI), play a crucial role in understanding non-linear relationships between random variables and are widely used across scientific disciplines. Yet, their use on real-world discrete data remains challenging. Existing methods typically rely on embedding discrete data into a continuous space and apply neural estimators originally designed for continuous distributions. This process requires careful engineering for both the embedding model and estimator architecture, but suffers from issues related to high data dimensionality. In this work, we introduce InfoSEDD, a discrete diffusion-based approach that bridges information-theoretic estimation and generative modeling such that they can be used to compute Kullback-Leibler divergences. Backed by Continuous Time Markov Chains theory principles, the design of InfoSEDD is lightweight and scalable and allows seamless integration with pretrained models. We showcase the versatility of our approach through applications on motif discovery in genetic promoter data, semantic-aware model selection in text summarization, and entropy estimation in Ising models. Finally, we construct consistency tests on real-world textual and genomics data. Our experiments demonstrate that InfoSEDD outperforms alternatives that rely on the "embedding trick". Our results position InfoSEDD as a robust and scalable tool for information-theoretic analysis of discrete data.

</details>


### [192] [CausalEvo: Causal Skill Self-Evolution for Health LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21016)
*Yuchen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CausalEvo，一种“因果技能自演化”方法：在固定模型权重、反馈案例与规则数量的条件下，估计被有意改动的技能集合的相对效应，从而更可控地决定技能保留与替换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 医疗智能体可从任务反馈中积累可复用的文本技能（如计算准则、测量时点、公式单位）。技能保留决策影响重大：有用规则改善后续回答，错误泛化则传播错误，因此需要一种能区分“哪些技能真正有用”的可控保留机制。

Method: 每步由冻结的 LLM 提出候选规则，与在任技能一并进入候选池，保证当前技能集始终是可行选项；对共享背景规则进行成对技能替换，并在相同反馈案例上打分，用最小二乘法将分数差转化为相对技能价值，保留排名靠前者（并列时偏好在任技能）；并给出分离候选支持误差、近似误差与估计误差的决策损失界。

Result: 在 MedCalc-Bench-Verified 六个任务的 120 个留出案例上，CausalEvo 准确率 60.00%，SkillOpt 为 55.83%，无技能为 51.67%；在不同优化预算的训练配置下，训练 token 消耗比 SkillOpt 少 55.6%。增益集中于部分计算，同时在另一些计算上出现退化，配对准确率区间包含零。

Conclusion: CausalEvo 将任务反馈转化为对技能保留与替换的受控决策；但整体增益尚未在统计上显著，且存在任务间的正负抵消。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents for health can accumulate reusable textual skills from task feedback, such as calculation criteria, measurement time points, and formula units. Deciding which skills to retain is consequential: useful rules improve later answers, while faulty generalizations propagate errors. We propose CausalEvo, a method for causal skill self-evolution that estimates the relative effects of deliberately changed skill sets while holding the model weights, feedback cases, and rule count fixed. At each evolution step, a frozen LLM proposes candidate rules, which are pooled with the incumbent skills so that the current skill set remains a feasible choice. Paired skill replacements against shared background rules are scored on the same feedback cases; least squares converts the score differences into relative skill values, and the top-ranked rules are retained, favoring incumbents on ties. A decision-loss bound separates candidate support, approximation, and estimation errors. On six MedCalc-Bench-Verified tasks with 120 held-out cases, CausalEvo achieves 60.00% accuracy, compared with 55.83% for SkillOpt and 51.67% without skills. Across the recorded training configurations, which use different optimization budgets, it consumes 55.6% fewer training tokens than SkillOpt. Gains concentrate in particular calculations alongside regressions on others, and paired accuracy intervals include zero. CausalEvo turns task feedback into controlled decisions about skill retention and replacement. Project repository: https://github.com/txbrook/CausalEvo

</details>


### [193] [Flash RNN-T: Memory-Efficient Joint Network and Transducer Loss Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23409)
*Mahmoud Ashraf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Flash RNN-T，将联合网络与损失函数实现为专用内核，并可选地对完整对齐空间与词表做分块重计算，从而在中等与长序列负载上同时取得吞吐与显存的最优前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN-T是流式语音识别的实用选择，但训练需要占用大量中间张量（显存开销大），限制了可扩展性。

Method: Flash RNN-T：联合网络+损失的一体化实现，结合专用内核，并可选地在完整对齐空间和词表上进行分块（tiled）重计算以降低显存。

Result: 在H100上，非分块Flash在适用负载下吞吐比最快基线高24–39%、显存少26–56%；中等负载下分块Flash显存约为基线的1/9、吞吐仅低16%（每批一次反向）；长负载下在80GB显存中可运行，而采用相同反向调度的所有对比方案均OOM。对比均使用各实现达到峰值吞吐≥95%的最低显存配置。

Conclusion: 分块与非分块Flash共同构成实测的吞吐-显存前沿，验证了一体化内核加可选重计算是提升RNN-T训练效率的有效途径。

Abstract: The recurrent neural network transducer (RNN-T) remains a practical choice for streaming speech recognition, but training requires large intermediate tensors. We present Flash RNN-T, a joint and loss implementation combining specialized kernels with optional tiled recomputation over the full alignment space and vocabulary. Tiled and untiled Flash form the entire measured throughput-memory frontier on medium and long workloads. On H100, untiled Flash achieves 24-39% higher throughput with 26-56% less memory than the fastest baseline on these workloads wherever it fits. Tiling reduces memory further through recomputation. On medium, tiled Flash uses ninefold less memory than that baseline at 16% lower throughput when both use one backward call per batch. On long, it fits where all tested alternatives with that backward schedule run out of memory with 80 GB. These comparisons use each implementation's lowest-memory configuration reaching at least 95% of its peak throughput.

</details>


### [194] [Residual Stochastic Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23421)
*Stephen Marcou,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Sonia Laguna,Moritz Vandenhirtz,Julia E. Vogt,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出残差随机概念瓶颈模型（Res-SCBMs），用多元高斯联合建模概念与残差变量，借助协方差结构与高斯条件化使干预可传播至残差变量，在概念不完备设定下兼顾预测性能与测试时干预有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念模型依赖人类可解释的中间概念，但在真实场景中常面临概念不完备（训练概念无法覆盖全部任务相关信息）问题。引入无约束残差通路虽可弥补不完备性，却形成难以解释、易泄漏的预测路径，且干预通常不改变残差变量，从而削弱测试时概念干预的效果。

Method: Res-SCBMs 通过多元高斯分布对概念变量与残差变量进行联合建模，学习显式刻画已知概念与所学残差变量之间依赖关系的协方差结构；干预时利用高斯条件化，使对概念的干预在统计依赖关系下传播至残差变量，从而在无需概念完备的前提下扩展干预能力。

Result: 在多个概念不完备基准上，Res-SCBMs 相比对比基线提升了预测性能，同时保持有效的测试时概念干预能力。

Conclusion: 通过在概率框架下将残差通路与概念结构耦合，Res-SCBMs 缓解了残差泄漏对干预的削弱问题，为概念不完备场景提供了一种兼顾性能与可干预性的概念模型方案。

Abstract: Concept-based models (CMs) learn human-interpretable intermediate representations that enable users to understand the model's reasoning and steer its predictions through test-time concept interventions. However, in realistic settings, their performance can be limited by concept incompleteness, where the training concepts fail to capture all task-relevant information. Adding an unconstrained residual pathway to the model's predictive process can overcome the consequences of incompleteness. However, this introduces an inscrutable, leak-prone prediction pathway that may weaken concept interventions, as these often leave residual variables unchanged. To address this limitation, we introduce Residual Stochastic Concept Bottleneck Models (Res-SCBMs), a family of concept-based models that jointly model concept and residual variables through a multivariate Gaussian distribution. Res-SCBMs learn a covariance structure that explicitly captures dependencies between the known concepts and learnt residual variables, allowing interventions to propagate to statistically dependent residual variables through Gaussian conditioning. This extends the intervention capabilities of previous CMs without requiring concept completeness during training. Our results show that, across multiple concept-incomplete benchmarks, Res-SCBMs improve predictive performance over competing baselines while retaining effective test-time concept interventions.

</details>


### [195] [Bridging Compressed Communication and Local Steps via Spectral Method in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23434)
*Dmitriy Bystrov,George Chirkov,Daniil Medyakov,Dmitry Bylinkin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将谱优化器Muon引入联邦学习的新方法，首次同时支持本地多步更新与通信压缩，并在较温和假设下（去掉有界异质性条件）给出收敛保证，在图像分类基准上验证了效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临数据异质性与高通信开销两大瓶颈；Muon谱优化器在集中式训练中优于Adam类方法，但现有将其迁移到FL的工作是"朴素集成"，无法兼容高效FL的两项基础技术——通信压缩与本地步（local steps）。因此需要一个原生支持本地更新与压缩的谱FL方法。

Method: 提出一种新的谱（spectral）联邦学习方法，将Muon式谱更新与联邦学习的本地多步更新和通信压缩机制相结合；在温和假设下建立严格的收敛性分析，并去除了传统分析中限制性的有界异质性（bounded heterogeneity）假设。

Result: 理论：在去掉有界异质性假设的较温和条件下获得严格收敛保证。实证：在标准图像分类基准上的实验表明该方法具有较好的效率与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 现有Muon-FL朴素集成缺乏对压缩与本地步的支持，本文所提谱FL方法补齐了这一缺口，兼顾通信效率、异质性鲁棒性与理论收敛性，为谱优化器在高效联邦学习中的落地提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative training of complex models across large-scale networks of edge devices while keeping data localized. However, data heterogeneity and high communication overhead remain fundamental bottlenecks. Recently, the spectral optimizer Muon has demonstrated remarkable advantages over Adam-like methods in centralized training. While recent efforts have attempted to transition Muon to FL, naive integrations into traditional architectures fail short. Crucially, existing approaches do not support the the communication compression and local steps --- the two foundational techniques of efficient FL. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel spectral FL method that utilizes both local updates and compression. We establish rigorous convergence guarantees under mild assumptions, notably eliminating the restrictive bounded heterogeneity condition. Empirical evaluation on standard image classification benchmarks validates the efficiency and robustness of our approach.

</details>


### [196] [RECREG: Recovery-based Shielding for Regular Safety Properties in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23445)
*Alexander W. Goodall,Francesco Belardinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RecReg——基于恢复式屏蔽(recov-based shielding)的安全强化学习框架，可强制满足非马尔可夫的正则安全属性，支持不同先验知识水平与安全保证等级，并在表格设定下给出严格的安全性与收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署RL智能体需满足形式化需求（如安全性、约束合规性），而现有工作缺乏对非马尔可夫安全属性的统一处理与严格理论保证。

Method: RecReg框架：通过恢复式屏蔽机制约束策略，支持非马尔可夫正则安全属性；主要贡献为表格设定下的理论基础（安全性与收敛性证明），并在MASA-Safe-RL库上开展实验，另提出基于SOTA基于模型的RL向深度RL扩展的方案。

Result: 实验表明RecReg实现高效学习，且实证结果与理论一致；理论部分给出相关工作中缺失的安全性与收敛性保证。

Conclusion: RecReg是统一、可扩展的安全RL框架，兼具理论严谨性与实证有效性，并可延伸至深度RL领域。

Abstract: In many real-world scenarios we need to consider formal requirements, such as safety and constraint compliance, to deploy Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents. In this paper we introduce RecReg, a safe RL framework capable of enforcing non-Markovian regular safety properties via recovery-based shielding. RecReg is a unified approach that supports different levels of a priori knowledge and safety guarantees. The paper primarily focuses on building the theoretical foundations for RecReg in the tabular setting, providing rigorous safety and convergence guarantees that are not present in related work. We provide an extensive set of empirical results using the MASA-Safe-RL library, and we outline an extension of RecReg to deep RL domains using SOTA model-based RL. Our empirical results demonstrate that RecReg achieves efficient learning and matches the theory in this paper.

</details>


### [197] [Regularized free deconvolution for spectral repair of resampling distortion in high-dimensional particle filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23448)
*Deming Song,Chi-Kin Lam,Kefeng Li,Hui Yu,Xilin Li,Huagang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究粒子滤波重采样导致集成协方差谱失真的逆问题：从观测重要性权重与经验谱反演重采样前谱，并以无偏方式用于滤波，即仅作为Metropolis调整复兴移动的预条件子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维下重采样虽能缓解权重退化，却会依据可由重要性权重计算的方式扭曲集成协方差谱。作者希望从"权重+经验谱"恢复重采样前的谱结构，且不因此引入滤波偏差。

Method: 先证明前向谱律在"权重＋实际后代计数"条件下可推广到随机重要性权重；随后构造正则化无卷积(free-deconvolution)估计器，证明其在固定分辨率尺度下的一致性并给出显式误差预算，同时用可计算的前向残差检验模型失配；修复谱仅作为MCMC复兴步的预条件子进入滤波器。滤波循环层面，在稳定标量动力学下推导几何可求和的误差递推，并给出"放弃修复"成本的显式律。采用预注册仿真。

Result: 反演将谱误差降低约一个数量级；校正后的预条件子在各向异性情形下达到oracle采样效率的约90%；放弃修复的代价取决于放弃发生的时机而非次数。

Conclusion: 该修复仅恢复特征值律而非特征向量，且只在明确设定的外生权重机制内被认证；谱误差只影响混合效率而不改变不变目标，从而保证滤波无偏。

Abstract: Resampling is the standard remedy for weight degeneracy in particle filters, but in high dimensions it distorts the spectrum of the ensemble covariance in a way that is computable from the importance weights. This paper studies the inverse problem: recovering the pre-resampling spectrum from the observed weights and the empirical spectrum alone, and using it without biasing the filter. We first show that the forward spectral law extends to random importance weights once one conditions on the weights together with the realized offspring counts, rather than on the weights alone. We then construct a regularized free-deconvolution estimator, prove its consistency with an explicit error budget at a fixed resolution scale, and certify model mismatch through a computable forward residual. The repaired spectrum enters the filter only as the preconditioner of a Metropolis-adjusted rejuvenation move, so that spectral error affects mixing efficiency but not the invariant target. For the full filtering cycle, we derive a geometrically summable error recursion under stable scalar dynamics and an explicit law for the cost of abstaining from repair. Pre-registered simulations show that the inversion reduces the spectral error by an order of magnitude, that the corrected preconditioner recovers about 90% of the oracle's sampling efficiency in an anisotropic regime, and that the cost of abstention depends on when abstentions occur rather than on how many. The repair recovers eigenvalue laws, not eigenvectors, and is certified only within an explicitly stated exogenous-weight regime.

</details>


### [198] [Predictable Scaling: Efficient Model Exploration via Scaling-Law Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23453)
*Aishwarya Wesanekar,Sheng-Tao Yang,Jiawei Zhang,Flavio Sales Truzzi,Chen Chen,Nader Beigiparast,Shengyuan Huang,Roshanak Zilouchian Moghaddam,Vitor Cid,Colin Clement,Vinayak Thapliyal,Sebastian Ament,Jihao Andreas Lin,Vincent Huang,Kai Han,Boyi Jiang,Wenlin Chen,Chaorong Chen,Ashwin Kumar,Maximilian Balandat,David Eriksson,Yifei Zhang,Pan Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Predictable Scaling (PS) 框架，通过小规模代理预测全规模推荐模型性能，可在79-88%算力削减下正确排序候选假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估推荐模型的候选改动（假设）需全规模训练，耗费数千 GPU 小时，严重限制了工程人员可探索的假设空间。

Method: PS 利用经实证验证的规律性条件：沿不同资源轴用可组合的缩放函数构建缩小版变体，通过曲线拟合将指标外推至目标规模，并给出标定后的不确定性估计；比较三种表达力递增的预测策略（raw proxy、中间策略、Gaussian Process），其中 GP 提供标定不确定性。

Result: 最简单的 raw proxy 预测误差已处于重复运行方差的经验噪声底限内；表达力最强的 GP 带来约15% 相对 MAE 改善并提供标定不确定性。在两个复杂度递增的排序模型（任务特定模型与基础模型）上，PS 在多种假设类型下均能正确排序可执行假设，节省79-88% 算力。

Conclusion: PS 为推荐模型假设筛选提供了高效、可靠的规模化性能预测手段，显著降低实验成本并扩展可探索的假设空间。

Abstract: Evaluating candidate changes (hypotheses) to recommendation models requires full-scale training runs consuming thousands of GPU-hours, severely limiting the hypothesis space engineers can feasibly explore. We introduce Predictable Scaling (PS), a systematic framework that exploits empirically verified regularity conditions to predict full-scale model performance from small-scale proxies. PS constructs scaled-down variants via composable scaling functions along distinct resource axes, and projects metrics to the target scale via curve fitting with calibrated uncertainty estimates. We compare three prediction strategies of increasing expressiveness: even the simplest (raw proxy) achieves prediction error within the empirical noise floor of repeated-run variance; the most expressive (Gaussian Process) provides an indicative 15% relative MAE improvement and calibrated uncertainty. Evaluation on two ranking models of increasing complexity---a task-specific model and a foundation model---demonstrates that PS correctly ranks actionable hypotheses across diverse hypothesis types at 79-88% compute reductions.

</details>


### [199] [A Topological Signature of Learning under Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23491)
*Zhongtian Sun,Fan Mo,Prayag Tiwari,Kelin Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用持续同调（persistent homology）追踪神经激活在不同距离阈值下的环路结构，发现噪声监督会在表示几何上留下可测量的特征，即使模型与损坏标签的吻合度很低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练准确率只反映与训练标签的一致性，无法刻画表示几何；作者希望探究噪声监督如何改变网络学到的表示，并从几何角度超越准确率指标来刻画标签损坏的影响。

Method: 采用持续同调，在神经激活上跨距离阈值追踪环路结构；实验覆盖图像分类、文本分类与模算术；在固定 CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 训练探针上比较等大小的干净标签与损坏标签子集，并做均匀尺度对照、归一化对照以及五条流水线上的噪声率校准比较。

Result: 在 epoch 10 时干净与损坏子集的持续性无差异；epoch 50 时损坏子集持续性更高，且该对比在均匀尺度控制下依然成立，尽管此时损坏探针样本中仅 7–11% 的预测与噪声标签一致。归一化保留了 CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 上干净与噪声轨迹的交叉现象，而 CIFAR-100/ResNet-18 在五种构造下均无交叉。噪声率校准中，平均激活范数的样本内 R² 均值高于原始持续性（0.936 vs 0.908）。

Conclusion: 噪声监督即使在与损坏标签吻合度很低时也会留下可测的几何特征；将尺度控制下的持续性与标签拟合测量相结合，可为研究表示如何响应标签损坏提供超越训练准确率的手段。

Abstract: How does noisy supervision change the representations learned by a neural network? Training accuracy measures agreement with training labels but provides little information about representation geometry. We use persistent homology to track loop structure across distance thresholds in neural activations. Experiments span image classification, text classification, and modular arithmetic. On a fixed CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 training probe, equally sized clean-label and corrupted-label subsets show no detectable persistence difference at epoch 10, whereas corrupted-label subsets have higher persistence at epoch 50. The contrast survives uniform-scale controls, even though predictions match the assigned noisy labels for only 7-11% of corrupted probe examples at that checkpoint. Normalisation preserves the crossover between clean and noisy training trajectories on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18, whereas CIFAR-100/ResNet-18 shows no crossover under any of the five evaluated constructions. In a separate noise-rate calibration comparison across five pipelines, mean activation norm achieves a higher mean in-sample R2 than raw persistence (0.936 versus 0.908). These findings show that noisy supervision leaves a measurable geometric signature even when agreement with corrupted labels is low. Comparing persistence under scale controls with label-fitting measurements offers a way to study how representations respond to label corruption beyond what training accuracy reveals.

</details>


### [200] [Uncertainty-aware AI-enabled framework for predicting hydrogen dispersion in confined spaces to enhance operator situational awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23515)
*Niclas Flehmig,Mary Ann Lundteigen,Shen Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段AI框架，用于压缩氢气加注泄漏场景：先由早期传感器读数估计未知泄漏率，再预测未来氢气云团扩散，输出带置信区间的浓度预测图，作为操作员的决策支持工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上密闭空间压缩氢气加注过程中，泄漏后有害浓度累积迅速，操作员态势感知不足，缺乏泄漏率与未来浓度分布的早期信息，难以在达到危险浓度前部署预防性安全屏障。

Method: 两阶段框架由两个独立AI模型构成一个AI系统：(1) 基于早期传感器数据反演泄漏率；(2) 预测后续时间步的氢气云团扩散，给出浓度预测图与不确定度界限。系统定位为决策支持/传感器增强工具，设计上包含保障机制以确保其运行于能力边界内，且独立的紧急关断系统仍作为最终安全层。在开源密闭空间氢气释放数据集上训练与测试。

Result: 泄漏率估计平均绝对误差0.052 g/s；氢气浓度预测误差约0.018，接近传感器精度界限±0.01；浓度预测提供良好校准的不确定度估计，可辅助判断每次预测的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该框架可提前提供有害浓度累积信息，潜在支持操作员的预防性干预；定位为增强态势感知的辅助层而非替代安全系统。

Abstract: We present a two-stage AI-enabled framework for enhancing operator situational awareness during compressed hydrogen bunkering in maritime confined spaces. In the event of a leak, the framework first estimates the unknown leakage rate from early sensor readings and then forecasts how the hydrogen cloud will disperse over the next time steps, providing operators with a predicted concentration map together with confidence bounds. The framework is based on two independent AI models building one AI system. The AI system is designed as a decision-support tool. It augments sensor readings, and thus enhances operator situational awareness and supports decision-making. This enables preventive safety barriers to be deployed before hazardous concentrations are reached, while an independent emergency shutdown system remains the ultimate safety layer. The system design incorporates safeguards to ensure the AI system operates within its competence envelope. Trained and tested on an open dataset of confined-space hydrogen releases, the framework achieves a mean absolute error of $0.052$~g/s in leakage-rate estimation and $\approx0.018$ in hydrogen-concentration prediction, approaching the sensor precision bound of $\pm0.01$. The hydrogen-concentration prediction provides well-calibrated uncertainty estimates that allow operators to judge the reliability of each forecast. These results suggest that the framework may provide early information about hazardous concentration build-up, potentially supporting preventive actions by operators.

</details>


### [201] [Out of Support, Not Out of Reach: In-Context Learning with Unsupported Instructions and Composed Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23528)
*Xuhan Tong,Yuchen Zeng,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立一个理论框架，将ICL面临的两种分布偏移（指令不在预训练支持内、目标任务不在预训练支持内）分开刻画，证明示例证据可在一定程度上压过指令偏移并保持指数级识别率，并以同一框架解释通过子任务分解实现长程目标任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL理论多将ICL解释为"识别预训练中已存在的潜在任务"，无法说明当指令或目标任务超出预训练支持时ICL是否仍然有效，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 在温和假设下构建理论框架，区分两类分布偏移：(1) 目标任务已被预训练表示时，分析示例数量增加对任务识别及对提示模板敏感性的影响；(2) 指令不受支持时，在固定的模型诱导参考扩展下分析示例证据与N无关指令偏移的相对作用；(3) 将同一框架作为解释视角，用于可分解为预训练支持子任务的长程目标任务。

Result: 目标任务在预训练支持内时，示例可识别任务，且随示例数量增加降低对预训练支持模板的敏感性；指令不受支持时，示例证据可主导与N无关的指令偏移，同时保持指数级识别率；对长程任务逐步应用受支持任务分析，可得到标准累积误差界，并说明沿中间状态轨迹调用熟悉子任务可避免直接选择不受支持的完整任务。

Conclusion: 示例、指令与任务分解在何种条件下能使ICL超越相应的预训练支持得到系统刻画，为ICL的泛化能力提供了统一的理论解释。

Abstract: In-Context Learning (ICL) enables pretrained LLMs to adapt to downstream tasks from a small set of input-output demonstrations, without parameter updates. Yet existing theory primarily explains ICL as identifying a latent task already represented in pretraining, leaving unclear whether ICL can succeed when either the instruction or the target task falls outside the pretraining support. We develop a theoretical framework under mild assumptions that separates these two forms of distribution shift. For target tasks represented in pretraining, we show that demonstrations can identify the task and reduce sensitivity to pretrain-supported prompt templates as their number increases. For an unsupported instruction, we show that under a fixed model-induced reference extension, demonstration evidence can dominate an $N$-independent instruction shift while preserving the exponential identification rate. We then use the same ICL framework as an interpretive lens for long-horizon target tasks that are not represented in pretraining but admit a plan that decomposes them into pretraining-supported subtasks. Applying the supported-task analysis step by step yields a standard accumulated-error bound and clarifies how invoking familiar subtasks along an intermediate-state trajectory can avoid directly selecting the unsupported complete task. Together, our results characterize when demonstrations, instructions, and task decomposition can support ICL beyond the corresponding pretraining supports.

</details>


### [202] [Beyond Expected Values: Risk-Sensitive Planning with Distributional Monte-Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23565)
*Quynh Trinh,Trung Nguyen,Nam Nguyen,Tuan Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CATSO与PATSO两种分布式MCTS算法，在Q边上维护有限支撑的经验回传分布，用CVaR加乐观奖赏选动作，并证明$O(n^{-1/2})$的根值误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MCTS仅存储标量均值估计，难以刻画尾部风险目标（如条件风险价值CVaR），在均值最优与风险敏感行为不一致时可能产生灾难性结果。

Method: 在Q边上维护有限支撑经验回传律：CATSO用类别原子+Dirichlet Thompson采样，PATSO用自适应粒子表示；动作选择基于Thompson采样Q边律的CVaR加多项式乐观奖赏；标量续值通过子节点Q边CVaR分数的访问加权平均回传。

Result: 针对有限深度嵌套CVaR规划目标，证明n次rollout后根值误差上界为$O(n^{-1/2})$，从而上界风险敏感简单遗憾；CATSO引入固定网格离散化偏差，截断PATSO引入可调Wasserstein压缩偏差；随机规划基准实验显示分布式Q边改善下尾回报并减少灾难性结果。

Conclusion: 分布式Q边结合Thompson采样与乐观奖励可有效扩展MCTS至尾部风险目标，兼具理论收敛保证与实验结果支持。

Abstract: Monte-Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) typically stores only scalar mean estimates, which makes it poorly suited for tail-risk objectives such as conditional value-at-risk (CVaR). We introduce Categorical Thompson Sampling with Optimism (CATSO) and Particle Thompson Sampling with Optimism (PATSO), two distributional MCTS algorithms that maintain finite-support empirical backup laws at Q-edges. CATSO represents Q-edge laws with categorical atoms and Dirichlet Thompson sampling, while PATSO represents them with adaptive particles. Both algorithms select actions using the CVaR of a Thompson-sampled Q-edge law plus a polynomial optimism bonus, and propagate scalar continuation values through visit-weighted averages of child Q-edge CVaR scores. For the finite-depth nested CVaR planning target, we prove an $O(n^{-1/2})$ bound on the root value error after $n$ rollouts, which upper-bounds the risk-sensitive simple regret. CATSO adds a fixed-grid discretization bias, while capped PATSO adds a tunable Wasserstein compression bias. Experiments on stochastic planning benchmarks show that distributional Q-edges improve lower-tail return and reduce catastrophic outcomes when mean-optimal and risk-sensitive behavior differ.

</details>


### [203] [BMuon: Budgeting Muon's Spectral Geometry for Structured Compressibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23571)
*Rida Assalouh,Tom Jacobs,Rebekka Burkholz,Yani Ioannou,Umut Şimşekli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Budget Muon (BMuon)，将 Muon 的谱范数几何修改为跨块共享通道分配预算的归一化最速下降，用可解释的全局通道预算取代稀疏惩罚系数，提升训练后 MLP 结构化剪枝的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 作为强优化器可视为谱范数几何下的最速下降，但其训练的 Transformer 在训练后剪除 MLP 隐藏通道时性能会急剧下降；作者希望在保留 Muon 优化性能的同时获得结构化可压缩性。

Method: BMuon 修改 Muon 的谱几何，使各块隐藏通道竞争共享分配预算；其 oracle 是介于 Muon 几何与结构化稀疏几何之间的一族范数下的精确归一化最速下降。主稀疏控制为可解释的全局通道分配预算而非依赖设置的稀疏惩罚系数。理论分析线性因子分解模型；实践中为精确分配问题推导 minorization-maximization 代理目标。

Result: 在线性因子分解模型中，Muon 存在带冗余隐藏宽度的最小代价精确表示，而在 BMuon oracle 几何且预算足够稀缺时，所有最小代价表示均为宽度最小。实验上，ImageNet-1K 的 ViT-S/16 在渐进结构化 MLP 剪枝下 BMuon 优于 Muon，且学到的分配比权重幅值是更有效的剪枝准则；FineWeb 上训练的 1B LLaMA 风格模型在训练后 MLP 通道剪枝下 BMuon 亦优于 Muon。

Conclusion: 通过将 Muon 的几何与跨块共享通道预算耦合，BMuon 兼顾优化性能与结构化可压缩性，在理论与大规模实证上均优于 Muon 的剪枝鲁棒性。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a strong optimizer for neural network training and admits an interpretation as steepest descent under a spectral-norm geometry. Yet we find that Muon-trained Transformers can degrade sharply when their MLP hidden channels are pruned after training. To reconcile Muon's optimization performance with structured compressibility, we introduce Budget Muon (BMuon), which modifies Muon's spectral geometry so that hidden channels across blocks compete for a shared allocation budget. The resulting oracle is exactly normalized steepest descent under a family of norms that interpolate between Muon's geometry and structured sparse geometries. Our principal sparsity control is an interpretable global channel-allocation budget, rather than a setting-dependent sparsity-penalty coefficient. For linear factorization models, we show that Muon admits minimum-cost exact representations with redundant hidden width, whereas under the BMuon oracle geometry and a sufficiently scarce budget, every minimum-cost representation is width-minimal. For practical training, we derive a minorization-maximization surrogate for the exact allocation problem. On ImageNet-1K with ViT-S/16, BMuon improves over Muon under gradual structured MLP pruning, and its learned allocations provide a more effective pruning criterion than weight magnitude. On a 1B-parameter LLaMA-style model trained on FineWeb, BMuon improves over Muon under post-training MLP channel pruning.

</details>


### [204] [Boundary Detection for High-Dimensional Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23590)
*Abigail Hickok,Valerio Galanti,Osama Al Dalahmah,Andrew J. Blumberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种用于高维点云边界检测的新方法，分别基于局部降维后的分离超平面拟合与局部邻域方向均匀性度量，并附带边界点法向量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边界检测方法难以应对非均匀密度、高环境维度与显著噪声并存的高维点云数据，因此需要更稳健的边界检测与法向量估计方法。

Method: 方法一：在局部邻域上先做局部降维，再拟合分离超平面，适用于能可靠估计本征维度的情形；方法二：度量局部邻域的方向均匀性（directional uniformity）来判定边界。

Result: 两种方法均输出逐点数值化边界分数及边界点处的估计法向量；在合成数据上，基于方向均匀性的方法整体表现最佳。在空间转录组数据中成功检测到肿瘤边界，并应用于大语言模型激活几何分析。

Conclusion: 所提方法在高维、非均匀密度与噪声条件下具有良好鲁棒性，且能同时提供法向量信息；在合成与两个真实案例中验证了有效性。

Abstract: We introduce two new methods for boundary detection in high-dimensional point cloud data, intended to work in situations with non-uniform density, high ambient dimension, and substantial noise. Our first method fits a separating hyperplane on local neighborhoods after doing local dimension reduction---this method works best when one can reliably estimate the intrinsic dimension of the data. Our second method measures the directional uniformity of local neighborhoods, and has the best overall performance on synthetic data. In addition to pointwise numerical boundary scores, our algorithms yield an estimated normal vector at each boundary point. We validate our methodology on synthetic data as well as two real examples: we analyze spatial transcriptomics data, where we show that we can detect the boundary of a tumor, and we also study LLM activation geometry.

</details>


### [205] [When Magnitude Suffices: Reconstructing Time-Domain-Gated Microwave Signals Without Phase on a Thoracic Phantom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23608)
*Maciej Cichoń,Patrycja Gołaś,Maksymilian Bartosik,Angelika Walczak,Agata Bielecka-Dąbrowa,Maciej Ślot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种仅用扫频幅度（SA+跟踪源）重建VNA时域门控幅度响应的方法：通过参考态逐频点校正与全连接网络学习幅度-相位关系，在$S_{11}$上达到与VNA输入相当的0.08 dB²形状误差，在$S_{21}$上为0.82 dB²；证明当幅度携带足够相位信息时标量仪器可替代VNA完成门控测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时域门控需测量复数S参数，传统上依赖矢量网络分析仪(VNA)，成本高、部署受限。作者希望验证：在单一校准配置下，仅凭频谱分析仪(SA)配合跟踪源的纯幅度扫频，是否足以重建VNA给出的门控幅度响应，从而用标量仪器替代矢量仪器。

Method: 1) 选择门中心以保证各测量状态可分；2) 对每台仪器在安装时以参考态做逐频点幅度减除进行一次校正；3) 在两类仪器的扫频数据上训练全连接网络，从SA幅度输入重建门控幅度；4) 测试对象为含肺形腔体的躯干体模，共11种介电填充状态，采用逐一留出交叉验证；5) 与显式相位预测、逐频点线性映射进行对比，并在公开UM-BMID乳腺数据集上验证泛化性。

Result: $S_{11}$上SA输入重建达到0.08 dB²形状误差、状态排序0.81，与VNA输入效果相当；$S_{21}$上为0.82 dB²并保持状态有序分组。显式相位预测显著更差，因门控要求相位精度约0.2 rad RMS。在11种测量配置下，相位对幅度的依赖在反射中强、透射中弱，解释了$S_{11}$与$S_{21}$差异。UM-BMID数据集上整体体模留出时，网络形状误差比逐频点线性映射低约30%。

Conclusion: 当幅度中蕴含的相位信息足够时，标量仪器（SA）在安装校准后无需任何相位测量即可复现门控响应，为低成本替代VNA提供了依据；但该等效性取决于幅度-相位耦合强度，反射测量优于透射测量。

Abstract: Time-domain gating separates the arrivals that form a microwave S-parameter measurement. It requires the measurement of complex values, so this application almost always uses a vector network analyzer (VNA). We show that, on one calibrated measurement configuration, the gated magnitude the VNA would give can be reconstructed from a magnitude-only sweep of a spectrum analyzer with a tracking generator (SA). We select the gate center for separability of the measured states, correct each instrument once by a per-bin subtraction of a reference state, and train a fully connected network on sweeps from both instruments. The test object is a torso phantom with lung-shaped compartments whose filling takes eleven dielectric states, each held out in turn. On $S_{11}$ the reconstruction from SA input reaches a shape error of 0.08 dB$^2$ with a state ordering of 0.81. This matches the reconstruction from VNA input. On $S_{21}$ it reaches 0.82 dB$^2$ and keeps the ordered groups of the states. An explicit phase prediction performs much worse, because gating needs the phase to about 0.2 rad RMS. Across eleven measurement configurations the dependence of phase on magnitude is strong for reflection and weak for transmission. This explains the difference between $S_{11}$ and $S_{21}$. Where the magnitude carries enough of the phase, a scalar instrument gives the gated response without any phase measurement after installation. On the open UM-BMID breast data set, with whole phantoms held out, the shape error of the network is 30 percent lower than that of a per-bin linear map.

</details>


### [206] [How Many, but How Sure? Class Competition as a Confidence Proxy in Multi-Class Density-Based Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23611)
*Villanelle O'Reilly,Georgios Leontidis,Marc Hanheide*
