Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的置信度代理指标（基于最强类别通道间的局部竞争），用"突出度"衡量多类密度估计中密度质量分配的可靠性，并通过推理时衰减存在竞争的密度降低MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多类密度估计器可对拥挤场景计数，但其类别输出无法指示密度质量分配的可靠程度；为此需要一种不依赖重训练或模型内部信息的置信度度量。

Method: 基于最强类别通道之间的局部竞争构造无需训练的置信度代理，仅用预测密度图计算"突出度"（prominence）指标；将残差竞争视为跨类泄漏的诊断信号，在推理阶段衰减存在争议的密度。

Result: 在 Hicks et al. 数据集的 DSACA 预测上，整体 MAE 从 0.920 降至 0.846。

Conclusion: 类别竞争蕴含关于计数可靠性的有用信息，突出度可作为有效的置信度代理，且推理时衰减策略能直接改善计数精度。

Abstract: Multi-class density estimators can count objects in crowded scenes, but their class-wise outputs offer no indication of how reliably density mass has been allocated. We introduce a training-free confidence proxy based on local competition between the strongest class channels. The resulting prominence measure requires only the predicted density maps, with no retraining or access to model internals. We test whether residual competition is diagnostic of cross-class leakage by attenuating contested density at inference time. On DSACA predictions for the Hicks et al. dataset, this reduces overall MAE from 0.920 to 0.846, suggesting that class competition contains useful information about count reliability.

</details>


### [207] [The Sign Code: The Hidden Binary Nature of Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23616)
*Niclas Göring,Shuofeng Zhang,Roi Holtzman,Yoonsoo Nam,Ard A. Louis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"符号码"(sign code)——所有神经元输出符号的集合,并实证与理论地表明:对带保号激活的过参数化视觉分类器,分类决策主要依赖符号码而非幅值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练后网络中每个神经元输出可分解为符号与幅值,但二者对分类决策的相对贡献尚不清楚;作者希望厘清这一点,并据此重新思考网络的有效分析单元(连续权重空间 vs. 离散符号码)。

Method: (1) 仅以二值符号码为输入的k近邻分类器与完整模型对比;(2) 在保持符号不变的前提下将各层幅值替换为随机噪声;(3) 定向翻转20%符号;(4) 设计正则项破坏同类样本符号码重叠;(5) 通过margin分解分析符号项与幅值残差随网络宽度的缩放(符号项按√d增长)。

Result: 仅用符号码的kNN在不同架构、激活函数与数据集上达到与完整模型相当的精度;幅值随机化几乎不影响ResNet性能;而翻转仅20%的符号即便精度降至随机水平;破坏符号码重叠会保持训练精度但导致测试性能崩溃;理论分解显示符号贡献以√d主导幅值残差。

Conclusion: 对该类网络,泛化良好的关键在于同类输入共享相似的符号码;网络的自然分析单元可能不是连续权重空间,而是离散符号码,为压缩、鲁棒性与可解释性研究提供新视角。

Abstract: Every neuron in a trained network outputs a number that can be separated into its sign (positive or negative) and its magnitude (absolute size). We call the set of all neuron signs the \textit{sign code}. This raises a key question: does classification depend more on the sign code or on the magnitudes? For overparameterized vision classifiers with sign-preserving activations, the answer is overwhelmingly the sign code. A $k$-nearest neighbor classifier operating only on this binary sign code achieves accuracy comparable to the full model across different architectures, activation functions, and datasets. Moreover, replacing all magnitudes in every layer with random noise while keeping the original signs barely changes residual network performance. In contrast, flipping just 20\% of the signs reduces accuracy to chance. Models generalize well when same-class inputs share similar sign codes. When this overlap is disrupted by a regularizer, training accuracy remains high, but test performance collapses. A margin decomposition helps explain these results: the sign-only contribution to each logit grows as $\sqrt{d}$ in network width, dominating the magnitude residual. The natural unit of analysis for these networks may not be through their continuous weight space but instead through their discrete sign codes.

</details>


### [208] [ThetreStageAI: Multi-View Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Structured Bengali Theatre Production Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23618)
*Md. Mehedi Hasan Naeem,Mst. Kamrunnahar Ruma,Shakila Sultana,Nafiza Anjum,Nasreen Jannatun Nemu,Md. Sujan Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ThetreStageAI，一个面向孟加拉语戏剧制作规划的多视图检索增强生成框架，通过分离场景、演员走位与灯光视图的检索并生成受模式约束的 JSON 制作方案，同时发布 500 条人工引导、LLM 辅助构建的合成数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 戏剧排演需在同一制作计划中协调对白、演员调度、舞台指示、灯光焦点、提示时序与设备约束，单一混合语料检索难以对齐这些异质要素。

Method: 将语料拆分为场景、演员走位（blocking）与灯光三个视图，用 BGE-M3 嵌入孟加拉语文本并分别索引至 Qdrant 集合；检索结果与制作简报一并输入 Gemini，生成含角色、场景、对白、舞台指示、走位、灯光及可选音效提示的模式约束 JSON；接受前进行结构与语义校验，最多允许一次受限修复重试。

Result: 构建 500 条孟加拉语戏剧短记录（扩展至 1500 篇检索文档）的人机协同合成数据集；诊断显示标识符一致、灯光字段有效，但存在明显对白重复；在匹配设置下对"单一合并检索"与"完整多视图检索"进行配对评测。

Conclusion: 多视图分离检索结合模式约束生成可用于结构化戏剧制作规划，数据集与评测材料公开，但对白重复问题仍需改进。

Abstract: Staging a theatre performance requires coordinating dialogue, actor movement, stage directions, lighting focus, cue timing, and equipment constraints within the same production plan. This work presents ThetreStageAI, a retrieval augmented framework for structured Bengali theatre production planning. Rather than retrieving from one mixed corpus, the system maintains separate scene, actor-blocking, and lighting views. Bengali text is embedded with BGE-M3 and indexed in view-specific Qdrant collections. Retrieved references are combined with a production brief and passed to Gemini, which generates schema-constrained JSON containing characters, scenes, dialogue, stage directions, blocking, lighting, and optional sound cues. Structural and semantic checks are applied before acceptance, with at most one constrained repair attempt. We also construct a human-guided, LLM-assisted synthetic dataset of 500 short Bengali theatre records that expands to 1,500 retrieval documents. Dataset diagnostics confirm consistent identifiers and valid lighting fields while also revealing substantial dialogue repetition. A paired camera-ready evaluation compares single-combined and full multi-view retrieval under matched settings. The source code, dataset, and evaluation materials are publicly available.

</details>


### [209] [GRAFT-EEG: Gauge-Aware Physical Field Transport for Cross-Dataset Motor-Imagery EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23620)
*Ying Zhou,Mengting Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无需目标统计量的跨数据集EEG迁移方法，将导联/参考/电极变化建模为观测算子而非表征漂移，在五个数据集上取得60.87%平衡准确率，较BIOT基线提升8.18个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨数据集EEG解码通常把导联、参考方式与有源传感器变化视为"表征漂移"，但这些变化实际上改变了观测算子。作者研究"目标统计盲"迁移：拟合阶段不使用任何目标信号、标签或经验统计量，电极坐标仅用于实例化固定的预测算子，从而在缺乏目标侧数据时仍能实现物理可解释的迁移。

Method: 1) 去除有源集共参考规范（active-set common-reference gauge）；2) 将每个原生掩码通过模板运动导联场（template motor leadfield）进行传输；3) 主判决由源域拟合的频谱分类器给出，有界状态空间模型（SSM）残差作为次级校正；4) 理论上建立受限可辨识性与算子扰动界。

Result: 五折留一数据集目标（253名被试）：动态方法等目标平衡准确率60.865%，严格清洗5/5 BIOT为52.684%（层次差异+8.182点，95%CI +6.382~+10.196，各目标均为正）；四个EEGPT干净目标上分别为61.520%与59.057%。四项事后同父精确检验中，FULL较坐标基、匹配谱、行打乱、错误解剖对照分别高9.959、9.698、11.241、3.985点（95%CI均不含零，Holm校正P<0.001）。独立60人同范式评估保留正的物理算子对比（+4.029点；+2.338~+5.773），动态-静态差异不显著；该结果属预注册的部分验证而非无限制外部验证。SSM贡献+1.059点（+0.013~+1.892）。

Conclusion: 将导联与参考变化显式建模为观测算子可显著改善目标统计盲的跨数据集EEG迁移，且增益可归因于物理算子而非坐标基或谱匹配等替代解释；但结论仍限于五个二分类运动想象域及所测试的算子，适用边界需谨慎界定。

Abstract: Cross-dataset EEG decoding often treats montage, reference, and active-sensor changes as representation shift, although they also alter the observation operator. We study target-statistics-blind transfer: fitting receives no target signal, label, or empirical statistic, and electrode coordinates only instantiate a fixed prediction operator. method removes the active-set common-reference gauge and transports each native mask through a template motor leadfield. A source-fitted spectral classifier anchors the decision; a bounded state-space-model (SSM) residual is secondary. We establish restricted identifiability and an operator-perturbation bound. Across five leave-one-dataset-out targets (253 participants), dynamic method achieved 60.865% equal-target balanced accuracy versus 52.684% for strict-clean 5/5 BIOT (hierarchical difference +8.182 points; 95% CI +6.382 to +10.196; positive in every target). On four EEGPT-clean targets, the descriptive means were 61.520% and 59.057%. In four post-hoc exact same-parent tests, FULL exceeded coordinate-basis, matched-spectrum, row-shuffled, and wrong-anatomy controls by 9.959, 9.698, 11.241, and 3.985 points, respectively (all 95% CIs excluded zero; Holm-adjusted P<0.001). A separate 60-participant same-paradigm evaluation retained a positive physical-operator contrast (+4.029 points; +2.338 to +5.773), whereas dynamic--static was inconclusive; its preregistered status is partial confirmation rather than unrestricted external validation. The SSM added +1.059 points (+0.013 to +1.892). Claims remain bounded to five binary motor-imagery domains and the tested operators.

</details>


### [210] [Deconstructing Multi-Task Active Learning: The Paradox of Gradient Conflict and Orthogonal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23623)
*Juho Jung,Seongjae Kang,Jinyoung Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文批评现有多任务主动学习（MTL-AL）依赖对不确定性、多样性、梯度冲突等异质目标的线性标量化，提出从"组合"转向"解构"，沿三个正交维度重构MTL-AL框架，在COCO、Cityscapes、NYUv2上取得一致的SOTA提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTL-AL方法主要用线性标量化把不确定度、多样性、梯度冲突等异质目标糅合为一个采集分数，作者认为这种纠缠式建模存在根本局限：线性组合无法刻画冲突目标下的Pareto最优权衡。因此需要从"目标组合"转向"结构解构"来重新设计采集机制。

Method: 提出沿三个正交维度重构MTL-AL：(1) Conflict Paradox（冲突悖论）——发现将梯度冲突作为候选排序惩罚项反而劣于完全不考虑冲突，遂解耦两件事：采集函数不评分冲突，训练阶段用梯度手术（gradient surgery）化解干扰；(2) Orthogonal Decomposition（正交分解）——将采集拆为信息量（Informativeness）与探索（Exploration）两阶段，先做信息量过滤再做覆盖最大化，避免"多样性稀释"；(3) Cross-Task Mutual Information（CTMI）——基于MC-dropout任务熵的协方差统计量，度量跨任务依赖关系。

Result: 在COCO、Cityscapes、NYUv2上进行大量实验，并与近期MTL-AL及数据选择基线对比，结果一致优于现有SOTA方法；表明对采集机制的结构性重构比增量式启发式设计带来更大收益。代码已开源。

Conclusion: MTL-AL的核心问题在于采集目标的纠缠式线性组合，而非单点启发式不足；通过解耦冲突处理、正交分解信息量与多样性、并以CTMI显式建模跨任务依赖，可获得稳定且显著的性能提升，说明"结构重思"优于"增量调优"。

Abstract: Existing multi-task active learning (MTL-AL) methods predominantly rely on linear scalarization of heterogeneous objectives such as uncertainty, diversity, and gradient conflict. We argue that this entangled formulation is fundamentally limited, as linear combination fails to capture Pareto-optimal trade-offs under conflicting objectives. We propose a shift from combination to deconstruction and introduce a principled framework that rethinks MTL-AL along three orthogonal dimensions. First, we identify the Conflict Paradox: although the optimization literature treats gradient conflict as interference to be suppressed, penalizing conflict when ranking candidates is measurably worse than not scoring it at all. We therefore decouple the two concerns: the acquisition function does not score conflict, and the resulting interference is resolved by gradient surgery during training. Second, we introduce Orthogonal Decomposition, separating acquisition into Informativeness and Exploration. This two-stage process, informativeness filtering followed by coverage maximization, prevents "diversity dilution" by excluding uninformative outliers before enforcing diversity. Third, we propose Cross-Task Mutual Information (CTMI), a covariance-based statistic of cross-task dependency computed from MC-dropout task entropies. Extensive experiments on COCO, Cityscapes, and NYUv2, including comparisons against recent MTL-AL and data-selection baselines, demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods, highlighting that structural rethinking of acquisition yields larger gains than incremental heuristic design. Code is available at https://github.com/Juho-Jung/multitask_active_learning_official.

</details>


### [211] [Deep LLM layers expose passage-level context in EEG during silent reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23638)
*Ingo Marquardt,Anthilia Alchanat,Priyanka Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过为 Llama-3.1-8B 全部 33 层分别训练 EEG 解码器，发现词级可解码性在浅层达到峰值并逐层下降，而上层语境追踪成分随层深上升；单个 0.5 秒 EEG 窗口即携带篇章级信息，目标层选择可作为 EEG 语言解码器的设计旋钮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作仅在 33 层中对比了无上下文的输入嵌入层与一个中层，无法说明 EEG 可解码结构在整个模型深度上的分布。本文旨在系统刻画词级可解码性与语境追踪成分随层深的变化规律，并厘清二者是否相互混淆。

Method: 对 Llama-3.1-8B 每一层分别训练解码器（在使用或不使用因果 Transformer 建模 EEG 窗口序列两种设置下），通过将 19 通道静默阅读 EEG 短窗口与对应隐藏状态嵌入对齐来评估检索性能；区分词级可解码性与语境追踪成分。设置两类新对照：(1) 目标几何对照——以真实目标加噪声（噪声按各解码器词级准确率标定）构建合成预测器；(2) 预测置换对照——在相同会话或跨会话的运行间置换预测，以排除 EEG 会话签名的影响。

Result: 词级可解码性在浅层达到峰值，向最后一层持续下降；语境追踪成分的检索增益随层深上升，且在有/无因果 Transformer 时均成立。意外的是，仅观察单个 0.5 s 窗口的解码器也能追踪文本段落。目标几何对照表明语境追踪并非词解码或目标几何的副产物；置换对照表明大部分语境追踪并非源于记录会话签名，而是主要跟随文本本身。

Conclusion: 词身份信息位于语言模型浅层，语篇/话语状态信息位于深层；单个 EEG 窗口即可承载可被上下文目标吸收的篇章级信息。目标层的选择应作为 EEG 语言解码器的显式设计变量。

Abstract: In a previous report we showed that a decoder can recover open-vocabulary word-level information from 19-channel EEG recorded during silent reading, by aligning short EEG windows with hidden-state embeddings of the presented word taken from a large language model (LLM). That analysis contrasted two of the model's 33 layers: the context-free input embedding and one mid-depth layer. Here we ask how EEG-decodable structure is distributed over the full depth of the language model. We trained one decoder per layer of Llama-3.1-8B, with and without a causal transformer over the sequence of EEG windows. We distinguish word-level decodability from a context-tracking component. Word-level decodability peaks in early layers and declines steadily towards the last layer. In contrast, the context-tracking component of the retrieval gain rises with depth, both with and without a causal transformer. Unexpectedly, a decoder that sees a single 0.5-s window at a time tracks the text passage too. Two new controls dissect this component. A target-geometry control, a synthetic predictor made of the true target plus noise calibrated to each decoder's word-level accuracy, shows that context tracking is not a by-product of word decoding and target geometry. A control that permutes predictions across runs from either the same or from different sessions shows that most of the observed context-tracking is not caused by an EEG recording session signature. Instead, context tracking mostly follows the text. A single EEG window therefore carries passage-level information that a contextual target can absorb. The results locate word identity in the early layers of the language model, discourse state in the deeper layers, and make the choice of target layer a design dial for EEG language decoders.

</details>


### [212] [No-Regret Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23641)
*Jiaxun Li,Vinod Raman,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"无遗憾生成"（no-regret generation）框架，引入基于树的错误强制维度来精确刻画有限时域极小极大复杂度，并建立无遗憾生成的充要条件，同时给出与在线预测复杂度的联系及最优遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成任务缺乏对"在学习者面对任意演示序列与兼容目标语言时，能否保持次线性错误数"这一目标的系统性理论刻画，作者希望建立类似在线学习遗憾框架的生成理论，理解生成速率的可能范围及其与在线预测复杂度的关系。

Method: 提出 no-regret generation 及其 agnostic 变体；针对两种设定引入 tree-based mistake-forcing dimensions（树状错误强制维度），用于精确刻画有限时域极小极大复杂度，并据此推导无遗憾生成的充要条件；进一步将生成复杂度与在线预测（Littlestone 维度）相联系。

Result: 证明在常数重缩放意义下，所有可容许的非降速率曲线在 realizable 与 agnostic 设定下均可实现，揭示生成速率的丰富谱系；对 Littlestone 维度为 d 的语言类，得到 realizable 错误界 O(d log(1+T/d)) 与 agnostic 遗憾界 O(√(dT log(1+T/d)))，且 realizable 界在常数意义下最优。

Conclusion: 基于树的错误强制维度为无遗憾生成提供了完整的极小极大刻画与充要条件；生成复杂度的谱系极为丰富，且当复杂度以有限 Littlestone 维度度量时，可与经典在线预测界对齐并获得最优 realizable 界。

Abstract: We introduce no-regret generation, which requires a generator to make a sublinear number of mistakes uniformly over all demonstration sequences and compatible target languages. We also formulate an agnostic variant that allows arbitrary demonstrations that may not be compatible with any languages. For both settings, we introduce tree-based mistake-forcing dimensions that exactly characterize finite-horizon minimax complexity and establish necessary and sufficient conditions for no-regret generation. Our characterizations reveal a rich spectrum of generation rates: up to constant rescaling, every admissible nondecreasing profile is attainable in both the realizable and agnostic settings. Finally, we connect generation complexity to online prediction: for language classes of finite Littlestone dimension \(d\), we obtain realizable mistake bounds of \(O(d\log(1+T/d))\) and agnostic regret bounds of \(O(\sqrt{dT\log(1+T/d)})\). The realizable bound is optimal up to universal constants.

</details>


### [213] [JAXtari: High-Throughput and Easy-to-Modify Arcade Learning Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23650)
*Quentin Delfosse,Raban Emunds,Paul Seitz,Sebastian Wette,Jannis Blüml,Daniel Kirn,Dominik Mandok,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JAXtari 是 Atari 2600 环境的高性能开源重实现，借助大规模 GPU 并行将 PPO 训练 100M 步压缩到单张 A100 上不足一小时，并提供对象中心观测与灵活的环境修改机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ALE 基准两大局限：一是缺乏原生的对象提取与环境修改支持，难以研究智能体是学习稳健因果模型还是依赖脆弱的视觉启发式；二是依赖 CPU 仿真，成为现代 GPU 加速训练流水线的瓶颈，限制实验规模与迭代频率。

Method: 基于 JAX 重实现 Atari 2600 环境，利用大规模 GPU 并行进行向量化仿真；在标准观测之外增加对象中心（object-centric）观测，并提供灵活的环境修改系统以支持神经符号方法与捷径（shortcut）发现实验。

Result: 在单张 A100 GPU 上，PPO 智能体完成 100M 环境步训练耗时不足一小时；借助快速训练与修改能力，研究者可在数分钟内完成智能体的训练与适配。

Conclusion: JAXtari 以开源形式同时解决了 ALE 的速度瓶颈与可操控性不足，为规模化实验、因果/捷径研究与神经符号强化学习提供了高效可改的平台。

Abstract: The Arcade Learning Environment (ALE) has been a cornerstone of Deep Reinforcement Learning research, providing a standardized benchmark for evaluating general agents. However, its lack of native support for object-extraction and environment modification restricts research into evaluating whether agents learn robust causal models or rely on brittle visual heuristics. Furthermore, the ALE's dependence on CPU-based emulation creates a significant bottleneck in modern GPU-accelerated training pipelines, limiting both experimental scale and iteration frequency. In this work, we introduce JAXtari, an open-source, high-performance reimplementation of Atari 2600 environments. By leveraging massive GPU parallelization, JAXtari allows PPO agents to finish training on 100M environment steps on a single A100-GPU in under an hour. Beyond speed, it provides object-centric observations and a flexible modification system, allowing researchers to, e.g. develop neuro-symbolic methods or discover novel shortcuts and subsequently train and adapt their agents rapidly, sometimes in only minutes.

</details>


### [214] [TriRouter: Sparse-to-Dense Electrode-Space Routing for Robust EEG Motor Imagery Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23665)
*Ying Zhou,Yaoxin,Mengting Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TriRouter电极空间路由框架，通过判别保持、掩码鲁棒与潜空间补偿三条路径提升稀疏/受损通道条件下运动想象EEG解码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动想象脑机接口依赖多通道EEG可靠解码，但实际采集常出现电极蒙太奇稀疏或通道损坏，标准模型在缺失通道下判别性能显著退化。

Method: 构建含三条路径的电极空间路由框架（判别保持、掩码鲁棒、潜空间补偿）；采用基于可观测通道可用性并经验证集校准的路由器分配各路径贡献，兼顾完整通道判别性与严重缺失下的表征恢复。

Result: 在BNCI2014-001上优于标准公开骨干网络与动态空间滤波的受损EEG基线；在名义25%、50%、75%缺失设置下分别提升3.68、4.02、6.46个百分点，并在结构化区域缺失与低通道数据集上保持有效性。

Conclusion: 通道可用性驱动的多路径路由可有效应对稀疏与损坏电极蒙太奇，为实际部署下的鲁棒运动想象解码提供可行方案。

Abstract: Motor imagery brain-computer interfaces require reliable decoding from multichannel EEG, but practical acquisition often yields sparse or corrupted electrode montages. We propose TriRouter, an electrode-space routing framework with discriminative-preservation, mask-robustness, and latent-compensation paths. A validation-calibrated router uses observable channel availability to allocate path contributions, preserving complete-channel discrimination while increasing representation recovery under severe missingness. In BNCI2014-001, TriRouter outperforms standard public backbones and a dynamic-spatial-filtering corrupted-EEG baseline. It improves accuracy over the strongest corrupted-EEG baseline by 3.68, 4.02, and 6.46 percentage points at nominal 25%, 50%, and 75% missing settings, and remains effective under structured regional missingness and additional low-channel dataset evaluation.

</details>


### [215] [Equivalence testing and run-count design for stochastic machine-learning experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23700)
*Dmytro Knopov,Nadiya Shvai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一套带功效理论的"实用等价性"认证协议：以两级(seed×item)方差模型分离运行间离散与item噪声，用TOST/置信区间在预设margin内检验等价，并给出由margin与目标功效反推每方法运行数的闭式公式及其t修正；仿真与两个真实案例（JEM四seed、千seed CIFAR-10 zoo与MultiBERTs）验证公式在设计相关范围内成立，说明少量运行难以认证等价，并建议两阶段设计用pilot方差的上置信界以避免样本量不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文指出，宣称两种训练方法"表现相同"通常仅依据每方法2–3次运行之间的不显著差异，这种推断缺乏功效保证，容易把"证据不足"误读为"等价"。因此需要一个可操作的、有统计功效理论支撑的等价性认证流程。

Method: 1) 两级(seed×item)方差模型，将运行间离散度与item级噪声分离，用于AUROC等per-run指标；2) 采用TOST（两个单侧检验），等价于要求margin内的置信区间，pilot方差用上置信界；3) 两级bootstrap处理复合统计量；4) 闭式样本量公式将margin与目标功效转换为每方法运行数，并给出shifted-t修正，经真实两级过程校准；5) 在两阶段设计（先pilot再套公式）中评估。

Result: 270格仿真网格及非高斯seed分布下，公式在设计相关范围内（自每方法5次运行起）得到验证。对JEM已发表的4次运行，未通过等价认证很大程度上由设计决定：按该方差校准，每方法2次运行的认证概率约3%；即便完全相等，以80%功效认证0.01 AUROC也需12次运行。千seed CIFAR-10 zoo与MultiBERTs案例中，公式与真实seed分布吻合（该范围内误差<0.05），并解决了2–3次运行无法判定的比较。两阶段设计实验显示pilot方差的上置信界显著缓解点估计导致的样本量不足。

Conclusion: per-run指标上的"实用等价"应当以预设margin下的TOST认证和功效驱动的运行数设计来替代"不显著即等价"的做法；闭式公式（含shifted-t修正）可直接用于规划运行数，两阶段设计中应用pilot方差的上置信界以控制样本量不足风险。

Abstract: Claims that two training methods perform equally well often rest on a non-significant difference between two or three runs per method. We develop a protocol, with its power theory, for certifying practical equivalence in per-run metrics such as the AUROC (area under the receiver operating characteristic curve). It rests on a two-level (seed x item) variance model separating between-run dispersion from item-level noise. Equivalence within a pre-specified margin is tested by two one-sided tests (TOST), equivalently by a confidence interval inside the margin; a two-level bootstrap covers composite statistics. A closed-form formula converts a margin and a target power into runs per method; its shifted-t refinement is calibrated against the realised two-level process. Simulations on a 270-cell grid and under non-Gaussian seed laws confirm the formula within its design-relevant range, from five runs per method. For four published runs of a joint energy-based model, the failure to certify equivalence was largely decided by design: with the variance calibrated to those runs, two per method certify with probability near three per cent, whereas certifying 0.01 AUROC at 80 per cent power needs twelve even under exact equality. A second case study on a thousand-seed CIFAR-10 zoo and the MultiBERTs checks the formula against real seed laws (within 0.05 in that range) and resolves comparisons that two or three runs leave undecided. In a two-stage design (pilot, then formula), our experiments suggest that an upper confidence bound on the pilot variance substantially reduces the undersizing seen with its point estimate.

</details>


### [216] [Blitz: Accelerating the Diffusion Modules of AlphaFold3 and Boltz-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23703)
*Vinay Saji Mathew,Soundar Kumara,Gretta Kellogg,William K. M. Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Blitz：基于几何噪声调度与多步一致性训练的少步蒸馏框架，将AlphaFold 3与Boltz-2的去噪步数从200降至8步，并在统一评测协议下保持教师级结构精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式蛋白质结构预测模型需约200步去噪，推理代价高；而现有少步采样结果各自采用不同基准与评分流程，缺乏可比性。

Method: 提出Blitz少步蒸馏框架：采用几何噪声调度使步数在噪声尺度上更均匀分布；通过多步一致性训练学习这些更新；推理阶段引入物理引导（physical steering）与无目标候选选择（target-free candidate selection）以减少残余几何缺陷。应用于AlphaFold 3与Boltz-2。

Result: 去噪步数由200降至8。在覆盖结构精度、界面质量、配体放置与立体化学有效性的统一评测协议下，Blitz保持教师级结构精度，AF3-Blitz优于所有已评测少步基线，并在多项精度指标上超过其教师模型。

Conclusion: 少步蒸馏可在显著降低采样成本的同时维持甚至局部超越教师模型精度，且需以统一评测协议进行公平比较。

Abstract: Diffusion-based protein structure prediction models produce all-atom structures in about 200 denoising steps, and that cost has driven a number of few-step samplers. We present Blitz, a few-step distillation framework that uses a geometric noise schedule to distribute steps more evenly across noise scales, then learns those updates through multistep consistency training. Physical steering and target-free candidate selection help reduce the remaining geometric defects at inference. We apply Blitz to AlphaFold 3 and Boltz-2, reducing denoising from 200 steps to eight. Because published few-step results use different benchmarks and scoring procedures, we compare both distilled models with their teachers and other few-step methods under a common evaluation protocol covering structural accuracy, interface quality, ligand placement, and stereochemical validity. Blitz retains teacher-level structural accuracy, with AF3-Blitz outperforming all evaluated few-step baselines and exceeding its teacher on several accuracy metrics.

</details>


### [217] [Neural Expansion: A Unified Mechanism for How Deep Neural Networks Generalize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23716)
*Chashi Mahiul Islam,Samuel Jacob Chacko,Mao Nishino,Canlin Zhang,Xiuwen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"神经扩张"(Neural Expansion)统一几何框架,通过输入空间中方向选择性射线覆盖的扩张来刻画泛化,并用泛化区间(GIs)量化方向特定的稳定区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计学习理论难以解释高度过参数化网络的优异泛化。现有margin界、PAC-Bayes、NTK等全局理论缺乏对泛化如何从训练动力学中涌现的机制性解释。

Method: 引入Generalization Intervals(GIs),量化训练样本周围预测保持稳定的方向特定区域;在不同架构与数据集上实证研究训练过程中射线覆盖的演化,并关联Jacobian奇异值。

Result: 训练导致射线覆盖渐进扩张,尤其在Jacobian奇异值低的方向上;该机制可解释相当比例的正确分类测试样本、对抗样本乃至误标注和OOD样本,体现为射线覆盖几何而非实例级记忆。

Conclusion: 将输入空间曲率与预测行为相联系,为深度学习提供补充现有全局理论的机制性基础。

Abstract: Deep neural networks demonstrate remarkable generalization despite being highly over-parameterized, challenging traditional statistical learning theories. While several theoretical paradigms such as margin-based bounds, PAC-Bayesian analyses, and neural tangent kernels provide global explanations, they often lack mechanistic insight into how generalization emerges from training dynamics. We introduce Neural Expansion, a unified geometric framework that characterizes generalization through the expansion of directionally-selective ray coverage in the input space. Using Generalization Intervals (GIs), we quantify direction-specific regions around training samples where the model's predictions remain stable. Our extensive empirical studies across diverse architectures and datasets reveal that training leads to progressive expansion of ray coverage, particularly along directions with low Jacobian singular values. This mechanism helps explain a large fraction of correctly classified test inputs, adversarial examples, and even mislabeled or out-of-distribution samples through the geometry of ray coverage rather than instance-level memorization. By connecting input-space curvature to predictive behavior, our framework provides a mechanistic foundation that complements existing global theories of deep learning.

</details>


### [218] [Escaping Parameter Space: Tight Generalization Bounds via Representation Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23729)
*Niclas Göring,Shuofeng Zhang,Branton DeMoss,Ard A. Louis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于最后一层隐表示、用无参数k-NN分类器评估表示质量的确定性神经网络泛化界，tight且非真空，并分解为三项可独立测量的经验量，可作为诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统泛化界依赖参数空间复杂度、到后验的KL散度、权重范数或压缩等度量，往往过于宽松（vacuous）且与网络实际泛化机制关联弱。作者希望构建一个基于表示质量、结构上tight且可解释的泛化证书。

Method: 以最后一层隐表示为核心，用参数无关的k-NN分类器衡量表示质量；据此推导确定性网络的泛化界，避免参数空间复杂度和权重范数等度量；并将界加性分解为局部类几何（k-NN解码误差）、解码器-网络一致性和训练稳定性三个经验可测量量。

Result: 从零训练的ResNet-18在CIFAR-10上界与测试误差仅差8.0个百分点，在CIFAR-100上给出首个非真空证书；冻结DINOv2表示时，CIFAR-10上证书为1.4%，1000类ImageNet上为23.1%。破坏局部类几何会消除泛化但保留记忆，说明类几何是因果相关机制；学习到的表示对权重噪声的敏感度显著低于线性读出层。

Conclusion: 该界tight、非真空、可分解，兼具诊断价值；局部类几何是泛化的因果相关机制；表示对参数噪声不敏感可解释为何参数空间证书常难以tight。为基于表示的泛化认证提供新范式。

Abstract: We derive a tight generalization bound for deterministic neural We derive a tight generalization bound for deterministic neural networks based on their last hidden layer representations. By evaluating representation quality with a parameter-free $k$-nearest-neighbor ($k$-NN) classifier, our certificate avoids traditional parameter-space complexity measures, KL divergences to a posterior, weight norms, and compression. The bound is tight: trained from scratch, it reaches an 8.0 percentage point gap to test error on CIFAR-10 (ResNet-18) and yields the first non-vacuous certificates we are aware of on CIFAR-100; with frozen DINOv2 representations, it certifies at 1.4\% on CIFAR-10 and 23.1\% on 1,000-class ImageNet. It decomposes additively into three empirical, independently measurable quantities: local class geometry ($k$-NN decoder error), decoder-network agreement, and training stability. This allows the bound to act as a diagnostic tool: we demonstrate that actively destroying local class geometry eliminates generalization while leaving dataset memorization intact, identifying class geometry as a causally relevant mechanism for generalization. Finally, we show that learned representations are significantly less sensitive to weight noise than linear readouts, suggesting why parameter-space certificates often struggle to remain tight.

</details>


### [219] [LagNet: Lag-Structured Operators for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23735)
*Zhenan Yu,Guangxin Jiang,Jiuyun Jiang,Jin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从"历史到未来的预测算子"本身出发，发现不同模型族的敏感度矩阵呈现与相对时间滞后对应的对角模式，据此提出基于滞后结构时间算子的直接多步预测框架LagLayer与LagNet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期时间序列预测研究多聚焦于输入表示与模型结构设计，而较少关注"历史窗口→未来视野"这一预测算子本身的结构形式；作者希望直接审视该算子以获得更本质、更简洁的建模方式。

Method: 观察到多个模型族的敏感度矩阵存在反复出现的对角结构，且对角线对应相对时间滞后。据此提出LagLayer：以同一相对滞后的历史—未来连接共享参数构造Toeplitz矩阵，并引入horizon-wise与history-wise缩放系数以放松严格共享；LagLayer可替换现有模型中的时序线性层，配合激活与多组特征映射堆叠形成LagNet。

Result: 在长期时间序列预测基准上，该方法与强基线相比具有竞争力；敏感度矩阵诊断与小样本实验表明LagLayer是线性层的有效且样本高效的替代方案。

Conclusion: 预测算子的滞后对角结构是跨模型族的共性规律，将其显式参数化为Toeplitz滞后算子可作为一种通用、样本高效的时序线性层替代，为长期预测提供了新的算子视角。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting maps a finite history window to an extended future horizon. Many existing models focus primarily on the design of input representations and model structures, while paying less attention to the structural form of the history-to-future forecasting operator itself. We examine this operator directly and find that, across model families, sensitivity matrices exhibit recurring diagonal patterns whose diagonals correspond to relative temporal lags. Motivated by this observation, we develop a framework for direct multi-step forecasting based on lag-structured temporal operators. Its basic building block, LagLayer, constructs a Toeplitz matrix by tying history-to-future connections with the same relative lag and incorporates horizon-wise and history-wise scaling coefficients to relax exact sharing. This design enables LagLayer to serve as an effective replacement for temporal linear layers in existing forecasting models. Stacking LagLayer modules with activations and multiple feature maps yields LagNet. Experiments on long-term time series forecasting benchmarks show that our method is competitive with strong baselines, while sensitivity matrix diagnostics and limited-data experiments demonstrate that LagLayer provides an effective and sample-efficient alternative to a linear layer.

</details>


### [220] [Universal Semantic Enhancement for Weakly Supervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23765)
*Siyu Zhou,Yuqin Xie,Hao Yuan,Yingjie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向表格数据的弱监督异常检测方法，通过融合预训练表格基础模型的通用语义知识与目标数据集上的领域特定知识，并用原型条件调制机制整合二者，在仅8个标注异常样本下优于竞争方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据广泛存在，实际场景中标签稀缺（仅少量标注异常+大量无标注样本），弱监督异常检测至关重要。现有方法主要学习刻画目标域内在分布模式的特征，但这类特征难以完整描述潜在的异常特性，且极易对极端稀缺的标签过拟合。

Method: 提出基于通用语义增强的弱监督异常检测方法：将预训练表格基础模型中的通用知识与目标数据集上学习的领域特定知识相融合，二者天然互补（通用语义刻画跨域共性模式，领域特征刻画目标数据集中正常/异常行为的内在分布）；并设计原型条件调制（prototype-conditioned modulation）机制有效整合两类特征，使其相互增强。

Result: 在公开数据集上的大量实验表明，在训练过程仅提供8个标注异常样本时，所提方法优于竞争方法。

Conclusion: 引入表格基础模型的通用语义可弥补稀缺标签下领域特征描述不完整与过拟合的问题，通用语义与领域知识的互补融合是提升弱监督表格异常检测性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Weakly supervised anomaly detection on tabular data is an essential task in many label-scarce applications, since tabular data is widely encountered and it is commonly observed in practice that only a limited number of labeled anomalies are available alongside abundant unlabeled samples. Most existing methods try to learn features that capture the inherent distribution patterns in the target domain. However, such features often struggle to provide a complete description of possible abnormal characteristics and are highly susceptible to overfitting the extremely scarce labels. To address this challenge, we propose a weakly supervised anomaly detection method based on universal semantic enhancement for tabular data, which fuses the universal knowledge from a pre-trained tabular foundation model with the domain-specific knowledge learned on the target dataset. The two types of knowledge are naturally complementary: universal semantics describe the common patterns of tabular data across domains, while the domain-specific features capture the inherent distributions of normal and anomalous behaviors in the target dataset. A prototype-conditioned modulation mechanism is then designed to effectively integrate the two types of features, allowing them to reinforce each other for improved anomaly detection. Extensive experiments on public datasets show that the proposed method outperforms competitive methods when only eight labeled anomalies are available in the training procedure.

</details>


### [221] [Logic-Guided Graph Denoising Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23766)
*Samuel Cognolato,Luciano Serafini,Alessandro Sperduti,Andrea Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用一阶逻辑(FOL)经分类器引导将领域知识注入图扩散模型，以模糊可微的逻辑约束目标在推理阶段引导去噪轨迹，显著提高生成图满足本体约束的比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当数据分布具有严格结构约束时，图扩散模型难以精确捕捉，会生成无效图。作者希望以表达力强且可解释的形式化手段（FOL）在生成过程中施加结构约束。

Method: 采用分类器引导(classifier guidance)：以外部变量对扩散模型分布进行条件化，在生成阶段将去噪轨迹导向一个目标函数，该目标为逻辑规则的模糊、可微松弛。方法只需指定FOL约束，在推理时鼓励其满足，无需重训练预训练生成模型。评估上对比知识图谱的无条件生成与引入领域本体作为结构约束的逻辑引导框架。

Result: 逻辑引导框架显著提升了生成图中满足本体约束的比例。

Conclusion: 将FOL约束通过分类器引导嵌入图扩散推理过程，是一种仅需约束规范、可即插即用地保证结构有效性的有效方案。

Abstract: Graph diffusion models are competitive approaches for generating complex graph-structured data. However, when such distributions have strict structural constraints, diffusion models may fail to capture them exactly, resulting in the generation of invalid graphs. In this paper, we propose a method for grounding graph diffusion models in domain-specific knowledge via First Order Logic (FOL), leveraging its expressive and interpretable formalism to impose structural constraints on the generative process. We incorporate domain knowledge using classifier guidance, a technique that conditions the diffusion model's distribution on external variables to exert control over the sampled data. During the generative process, classifier guidance steers the denoising trajectory to optimize an objective function, which we define as a fuzzy and differentiable relaxation of the logical rules. Our methodology requires only the specification of constraints in FOL, whose satisfaction is encouraged at inference time to guide a pre-trained generative model. We evaluate our approach by comparing the unconditional generation of knowledge graphs with our logic-guided framework, which incorporates domain ontologies as structural constraints during inference. This approach significantly increases the proportion of generated graphs that satisfy the ontological constraints.

</details>


### [222] [Explainable Machine Learning Analysis of Temperature Effects on Mechanical Strength of Fiber-Reinforced High-Performance Concrete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23811)
*Jagaran Chakma,Zhiguang Zhou,Badhan Chakma,Priyanta Chakma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 构建基于1204条实验记录的数据驱动框架预测高温暴露下纤维增强高性能混凝土抗压强度，梯度提升与XGBoost表现最优(R²=0.903)，SHAP与部分依赖分析显示温度为主导因素，并开发Python GUI工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤维增强高性能混凝土在温度暴露下的抗压强度预测困难，源于配合比组成、纤维特性、养护条件与温度之间复杂的相互依赖关系，需要精确且可解释的数据驱动方法。

Method: 采用1204条涵盖多样材料设计与暴露条件的实验记录构建数据集；评估八种机器学习模型；使用SHAP（Shapley加性解释）与部分依赖分析进行模型可解释性分析；并将最优模型部署于Python图形用户界面。

Result: 集成方法普遍优于单一学习器；梯度提升与XGBoost在未见数据上R²均为0.903；梯度提升RMSE最低(6.67 MPa)，XGBoost的MAE(3.97 MPa)与MAPE(10.60%)最低；温度是抗压强度最主要影响因素，其次为细骨料、减水剂、水泥含量、水胶比和含水量；强度随温度升高显著下降，且劣化程度取决于具体配合比设计。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了精确且可解释的温度相关抗压强度评估方法，并通过GUI实现快速预测，支持纤维增强高性能混凝土的实用化数据驱动设计。

Abstract: Predicting the compressive strength of fiber-reinforced high-performance concrete subjected to temperature exposure is challenging because to the intricate interdependencies of mixture composition, fiber properties, curing conditions, and temperature. A data-driven framework to predict compressive strength utilizing 1,204 experimental records containing a diverse array of material designs and exposure situations. This study evaluates eight machine-learning models and demonstrate that ensemble approaches routinely surpass individual learners. Both gradient boosting and extreme gradient boosting exhibit superior performance on unseen data, each attaining a R^2 of 0.903. Gradient boosting produces the minimal root mean square error (6.67 MPa), whereas extreme gradient boosting achieves the lowest mean absolute error (3.97 MPa) and mean absolute percentage error (10.60%). The models are further interpreted utilizing Shapley additive explanations and partial dependence analysis, which reveal temperature as the primary factor influencing compressive strength, succeeded by fine aggregate, superplasticizer, cement content, water-to-binder ratio, and water content. Strength diminishes significantly with rising temperature, albeit the extent of deterioration is contingent upon the specific combination design. The best performing models are subsequently implemented in a Python-based graphical user interface for expedited prediction. Our findings offer a precise and comprehensible methodology for evaluating temperature-dependent compressive strength and facilitate the practical, data-driven design of fiber-reinforced high-performance concrete.

</details>


### [223] [Learning Personalized Memory Gating from Delayed One-Sided Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23812)
*Kaixuan Ji,Zixiang Chen,Wenting Zhao,Cheng Qian,Heng Wang,Weizhi Zhang,Jielin Qiu,Liangwei Yang,Quanquan Gu,Silvio Savarese,Shelby Heinecke,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究事实级记忆门控：用检索式标注判定事实是否被复用，将在线门控建模为带延迟单边反馈的上下文苹果品尝问题，提出Warm-Start UCB 实现个性化记忆管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆使语言模型智能体可个性化交互，但有限记忆库无法保存全部抽取事实；现有门控策略跨用户共享，忽视了用户特有的未来复用模式。

Method: 提出基于检索的标注策略，按事实是否在后续被检索并写入回复打标签；将在线记忆门控形式化为上下文苹果品尝（contextual apple tasting）问题，反馈延迟且单边；提出 Warm-Start UCB，结合已有数据的离线学习与对当前用户的在线适应。

Result: 理论证明：延迟窗口不超过 O(√T) 时，遗憾上界以高概率为 Õ(Tε ∧ d√T)，T 为总轮数、ε 为初始化误差、d 为特征映射维度；实验表明在不同记忆预算与历史长度下均优于全部基线。

Conclusion: 事实级、用户自适应的记忆门控是可行且有效的个性化记忆管理策略，为有限记忆库下的长期记忆系统提供了理论与实证支持。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables language-model agents to personalize future interactions, but a finite memory bank cannot preserve every extracted fact. Existing memory gating policies are typically shared across users and therefore fail to account for user-specific patterns of future reuse. In this paper, we study memory gating at the fact level. We first propose a retrieval-based labeling strategy that labels each fact by whether it is later retrieved and incorporated into responses. We then formulate online memory gating as contextual apple tasting with delayed one-sided feedback, and propose Warm-Start UCB, which combines offline learning from existing data with online adaptation to the current user. Our theoretical analysis establishes a high-probability regret upper bound of $\tilde{O}(Tε\wedge d\sqrt{T})$ when the delay window is at most $O(\sqrt{T})$, where $T$ is the total number of rounds, $ε$ is the initialization error, $d$ is the dimension of the feature map, and $\tilde{O}(\cdot)$ hides constant and poly-logarithmic factors. The results of our experiment demonstrate that our method outperforms all baselines under different memory budgets and history lengths, establishing it as a promising personalized memory gating policy.

</details>


### [224] [CocoRNA: Collective RNA Design with Cooperative Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23820)
*Tianmeng Hu,Biao Luo,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CocoRNA——一种协作式多智能体强化学习框架，用于RNA二级结构逆设计，通过分解子问题、集中式critic引导与多智能体协同优化，在Rfam数据集上显著优于SOTA，并具跨任务泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计能可靠折叠为目标二级结构的RNA序列对理解其生物功能至关重要，但仍是高难度的计算问题；现有方法在复杂度与设计策略探索上存在不足。

Method: 将RNA逆设计任务分解为多个较小子问题，每个子问题由多个智能体协同求解；训练时采用集中式critic利用全局结构信息指导各智能体，联合优化设计策略，从而学到高质量设计策略。

Result: 在Rfam数据集上，成功率与设计速度均大幅超越现有最先进方法；在未见过结构上无需额外训练即可有效泛化；在其他生物序列设计任务上同样验证了有效性与广泛潜力。

Conclusion: CocoRNA通过多智能体协作与任务分解有效降低问题复杂度、提升策略探索，是一种高效且具通用潜力的复杂生物序列设计框架。

Abstract: Designing RNA sequences that reliably fold into specific secondary structures is essential for understanding their biological functions but remains a challenging computational problem. We propose CocoRNA, a cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for RNA inverse design. CocoRNA simplifies the design task by decomposing it into smaller sub-problems, each solved collaboratively by multiple agents. This approach reduces the complexity of the problem and improves the exploration of design policies. During training, a centralized critic uses global structural information to guide the agents, enabling them to jointly optimize their design strategies. As a result, CocoRNA learns high-quality RNA design policies that generalize effectively to unseen structures without additional training. Experiments on the Rfam dataset demonstrate that CocoRNA substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both success rate and design speed. Further experiments on other biological sequence design tasks highlight the effectiveness and broad potential of CocoRNA for complex design tasks.

</details>


### [225] [Waiting Buys Exactly Two Moments: Endogenous Identification in Prediction-Driven Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23830)
*Rama AlHamidi,Rasul Khanbayov,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究固定预算下"帮谁、何时帮"的时序分配问题：等待可揭示风险但高风险者先流失。作者刻画了噪声信号重复观测下的识别性与时机遗憾，指出更长观测每期仅新增两个矩、且无法识别分配福利与优先者流失，故在任意样本量下存在不可避免的时机遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规划者预算固定，需要在信息不足时决定干预对象与时机；等待虽能积累风险信息，却因风险依赖的流失（高风险个体先离开）而损失干预机会。核心问题是：额外观测究竟能带来多少可识别信息，以及这些信息是否足以支撑福利最优的时机选择。

Method: 在标准模型下，用长度为τ的观测前缀刻画潜在风险矩的可识别性；构造观测等价类以证明时机遗憾的不可避免性；给出精确判定证书，检验所有兼容总体是否共享唯一最优日；并用匹配实验分离估计、bootstrap平均与遗憾加权的各自效应。

Result: 长度τ的观测前缀恰好识别 m_j=E[p^j], j=0,…,2τ-1，每增加一期多识别两个矩；这些矩可识别幸存者的平均下期流失，但不能识别分配福利或优先受助者的流失。观测等价构造表明，在固定观测长度下任意样本量都存在时机遗憾；实验显示bootstrap平均与早停改善决策，而遗憾加权在合并均方遗憾上无增益。

Conclusion: 更好的统计拟合未必带来更好的分配福利：延长观测常能消解时机歧义，但最优时点可能已错过。实践上，bootstrap平均与早停是有效改进手段，遗憾加权无效用。

Abstract: A planner with a fixed budget of preventive help must decide whom to help and when. Waiting reveals risk but removes intervention opportunities as higher-risk individuals leave first. We characterize this tradeoff under repeated noisy signals and risk-dependent attrition: in the canonical model, a length-$τ$ observation prefix identifies exactly the latent risk moments $m_j=\mathbb E[p^j]$ for $j=0,\ldots,2τ-1$, buying two new moments per additional period. These identify average next-period attrition among survivors but need not identify allocation welfare or attrition among those prioritized for treatment. Our observationally equivalent constructions establish unavoidable timing regret at any sample size for a fixed observation length, while exact certificates determine whether all compatible populations share a unique optimal date. Matched experiments separate the effects of estimation, bootstrap averaging, and regret weighting. Longer observation often resolves timing ambiguity, but the optimal date may already have passed. In our experiments, bootstrap averaging and early stopping improve decisions, while regret weighting adds no improvement in pooled mean squared regret: better statistical fit need not yield better allocation welfare. The code and artifacts are available at https://github.com/KurbanIntelligenceLab/waiting-buys-two-moments.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [226] [Eliciting Specifications: When Does Interactive Clarification Help?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22382)
*Mowafak Allaham,Aditya Vempaty,Ashish Jagmohan,Prasenjit Dey,Ravi Kokku*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究让"elicitation agent(EA)"在形式化前向参考真值驱动的模拟开发者提问澄清，以改善LLM生成的Lean规格；实验表明其效果依赖模型配置，跨模型管线增益明显而同模型管线为负，且失败主要源于后置条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务描述常欠规范或有歧义，并遗漏与验证器相关的边界情况，导致LLM从描述生成的Lean形式规格不完美。作者希望验证"主动澄清"能否提升经verifier检验的Lean规格质量。

Method: 提出EA流程：分解任务→识别未决决策→在问题预算(k=3、k=10)内提出有界澄清问题→向基于参考真值的模拟开发者求答→写出精炼的自然语言规格→由LLM形式化器翻译为Lean。在189个Verina Lean 4规格生成任务上，对比单次生成与elicitation，覆盖同模型与跨模型配置，报告strict pass@1与compile-conditional估计，并按basic/advanced划分分析，另做失败分析。

Result: 同模型Claude Sonnet 4.6管线在k=3与k=10下strict pass@1估计均为负，说明elicitation并非普遍提效。Sonnet 4.6→GPT-5.6与Sonnet 4.6→Gemini 3.8 Flash管线strict估计为正；最大strict结果65.6%(+6.3点,+12任务)出现在Sonnet 4.6→GPT-5.6。8个条件中6个compile-conditional估计为正，编译输出间最大跨模型差异+4.9点。advanced划分最大变化为Sonnet 4.6→GPT-5.6、k=10时从42.0%升至50.6%。失败分析指出后置条件(postconditions)是最大退化来源。

Conclusion: 自动形式化系统应将elicited自然语言规格视为中间工件，并联合优化"编译输出中的语义正确性"与"澄清信息向Lean的可靠翻译"；elicitation的收益依赖形式化器配置，不能假定为普适增益。

Abstract: LLMs can generate Lean specifications from task descriptions, but those descriptions are often underspecified or ambiguous and may omit verifier-relevant edge cases, yielding imperfect formal specifications. We study whether an elicitation agent (EA) can improve verifier-checked Lean specifications by seeking clarification from a reference-grounded simulated developer before formalization. The agent decomposes the task, identifies unresolved decisions, asks bounded clarification questions, and uses the answers to write a refined natural-language specification that an LLM formalizer then translates into Lean. On 189 Verina Lean 4 specification-generation tasks, we compare single-shot generation with elicitation under question budgets of k=3 and k=10, allowing at most three or ten EA questions per task, across same-model and cross-model configurations. The same-model Claude Sonnet 4.6 pipeline has negative strict pass@1 estimates at both budgets, showing elicitation is not a uniform accuracy booster. In contrast, the Sonnet 4.6-to-GPT-5.6 and Sonnet 4.6-to-Gemini 3.8 Flash pipelines have positive strict estimates. The largest strict result reaches 65.6%, a +6.3-point change (+12 tasks), in the Sonnet 4.6-to-GPT-5.6 pipeline. Six of eight conditions have positive compile-conditional estimates, with a maximum cross-model difference of +4.9 points among compiling outputs. Treatment estimates also differ across the basic and advanced splits; the largest advanced-task change occurs in the Sonnet 4.6-to-GPT-5.6 configuration at k=10, from 42.0% to 50.6%. Failure analysis identifies postconditions as the largest source of degradation. These findings suggest future autoformalization systems should treat elicited natural-language specifications as intermediate artifacts and optimize jointly for semantic correctness among compiling outputs and reliable translation of clarified information into Lean.

</details>


### [227] [EnterpriseSynth: Zero-Execution SFT and Evaluation Data from OpenAPI Specs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22412)
*Rashmi Thimmaraju,Natan Vidra,Setty Spurthi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出零执行框架EnterpriseSynth，从OpenAPI规范生成SFT轨迹与评测数据，无需调用真实API，并揭示端点级指标高估端到端正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用型LLM智能体需要大量训练与评测数据，但从企业API采集执行轨迹受访问控制、合规约束、速率限制及生产数据修改风险影响，往往不安全、昂贵或不可行。

Method: 四阶段流水线：规范解析、意图生成、轨迹合成、静态校验；每条轨迹在入库前对照源规范检查端点、参数、类型与schema一致性；另采用对抗式精化提升校验器。在17个真实与合成API上评测，并用5个随机种子、留出公共及5个私有手写规范验证。

Result: 对抗式精化将植入schema违规的检出率从57–80%提升至98%（43/44）；紧凑开源模型微调后优于未微调模型与Self-Instruct基线；私有集工具选择准确率40.0%，公共留出集39.6%；但独立LLM评测显示指标由端点级48.9%降至含参数选择与取值的21.3%。

Conclusion: OpenAPI规范可支撑可扩展、免执行的数据生成，但评估工具智能体部署就绪度时必须采用参数级评测，端点级准确率会显著高估端到端正确性。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model agents require substantial training and evaluation data, yet collecting execution traces from enterprise APIs is often unsafe, expensive, or infeasible because of access controls, compliance constraints, rate limits, and the risk of modifying production data. We present EnterpriseSynth, a zero-execution framework that converts OpenAPI specifications into supervised fine-tuning trajectories and paired evaluation instances without invoking the underlying APIs. The framework uses a four-stage pipeline for specification parsing, intent generation, trajectory synthesis, and static validation. Each generated trajectory is checked against the source specification for endpoint, parameter, type, and schema consistency before being admitted to the dataset. We evaluate EnterpriseSynth on 17 real and synthetic APIs. Adversarial refinement increases the validator's detection rate for planted schema violations from 57-80% to 98% (43/44). Across five random seeds, a compact open-weight model fine-tuned on EnterpriseSynth data outperforms both an untuned model and a Self-Instruct baseline on held-out APIs. Performance remains consistent on five private, hand-authored API specifications that were not available during training, with tool-selection accuracy of 40.0% on the private set and 39.6% on the public held-out set. However, an independent LLM-based evaluation shows that endpoint-selection accuracy substantially overestimates end-to-end correctness: accuracy falls from 48.9% under the endpoint-level metric to 21.3% when argument selection and values are also evaluated. These results demonstrate that OpenAPI specifications can support scalable, execution-free data generation, while highlighting the need for argument-level evaluation when assessing the deployment readiness of tool-using agents.

</details>


### [228] [Understanding LLM Hallucinations Caused by Soft Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22520)
*Jinghao Wen,Dongning Ma,Ruixuan Wang,Shaohuang Wang,Xun Jiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 从硬件视角研究大模型幻觉：提出LLMEI在推理期注入软错误，评估不同架构与任务下的影响，归纳失败模式分类学并分析其随错误率的分布，强调硬件可靠性诊断的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM幻觉研究多集中于软件层面，硬件级因素（尤其是硬件软错误）对幻觉的诱发机制关注不足。

Method: 提出LLMEI，在LLM推理过程中注入软错误，覆盖多种LLM架构及数学推理、代码生成等任务；基于幻觉行为归纳失败模式分类学，并统计不同错误率下的分布。

Result: 软错误会在不同架构与任务中引发多样化的幻觉失败模式，其分布随错误率变化，呈现出硬件层面的幻觉特异性特征。

Conclusion: 硬件软错误是LLM幻觉的重要来源，需在硬件实现可靠性评估中引入面向幻觉的专门诊断。

Abstract: While hallucinations in Large Language Models (LLMs) have been extensively studied from a software perspective, limited attention has been paid to hardware-level factors. In this paper, we performed a case study to understand LLM hallucination caused by soft errors on hardware. We introduce LLMEI to inject soft errors into LLMs during inference, based on what we evaluate the impact of soft error across diverse LLM architectures and tasks, including mathematical reasoning and code generation. We summarize the taxonomy of different failure modes based on LLM hallucination behaviors and analyze their distributions under different error rates. Our find- ings underscore the hallucination-specific diagnostics within the reliability of LLM implementation on hardware.

</details>


### [229] [Code Skill Server: A Large-Scale Search Engine for Code Generation Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23186)
*Xiaoyong Li,Haolong Li,Dongliang Xu,Haoyu Li,Yifan Wang,Jingyuan Shi,Yanghua Wang,Yanbin Sun,Shidong Pan,Yue Yao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 Code Skill Server（CSS），一个面向代码生成技能的规模化搜索引擎，可从多源异构渠道增量收集并索引公开技能，通过检索最相关技能注入智能体以提升代码生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agent skill 使用方式依赖人工从异构来源发现与整理技能，而现有技能检索方法仅面向已建成的技能库做筛选；随着可复用技能数量与种类快速增长，人工跨源检索已不可行。

Method: 构建 CSS：一个技能服务器，增量采集多个公开来源的代码生成技能并建立索引，本文语料含 993 个技能（CSS-993）；在不同检索方法与多种代码生成基准上进行系统实验，并开展跨模型评估。

Result: 最优 CSS 配置使 Pass@1 最高提升 13.91%；跨模型评估中，所有被评测模型均获得至少 4.21% 的稳定提升。

Conclusion: CSS 能有效检索并提供相关技能，提升代码生成质量并增强模型的代码生成能力；代码与 CSS-993 语料已开源。

Abstract: Agent skills have proven to be an effective technique for improving agent ability on specific tasks, including software engineering, natural science, and mathematics. During such a process, using agent skills often involves manually discovering and organizing skills from heterogeneous sources, while existing skill retrieval methods mainly focus on selecting relevant skills from established skill libraries. However, with the rapidly increasing number and variety of reusable agent skills, manually searching for relevant skills across heterogeneous sources is becoming impractical at scale. In this paper, we introduce the Code Skill Server (CSS), a large-scale search engine for finding the most useful code generation skills for a target task. CSS consists of a skill server that incrementally collects and indexes publicly available code generation skills from multiple sources, with 993 skills included in the corpus used in this work. Extensive experiments across different retrieval methods and code generation benchmarks show that the best CSS configuration improves Pass@1 by up to 13.91% and consistently improves all evaluated models by at least 4.21% in the cross-model evaluation. These results demonstrate that CSS can effectively search for and serve relevant skills, improving code generation quality and enhancing models' code generation capabilities. Code and the CSS-993 corpus are available at {https://github.com/haoyuli-sdu/CodeSkillServer}.

</details>


### [230] [ForTress: Bringing Assurance and Determinism for LLM-Driven Fortran-to-Python Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22528)
*Qinrun Dai,Ruitong Liu,Chien-Wei Huang,Guanhong Tao,Guannan Wei,Yueqi Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ForTress 将 LLM 的重复翻译决策固化为受保护的可执行规则，并在 Lean 中对规则做形式化语义审计，实现 Fortran→Python 的确定性、可复用且带语义保证的翻译，同时降低 44.2% 的 LLM 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成熟科学软件从 Fortran 迁移到 Python 可提升可访问性与工具集成度，但人工重写成本高。LLM 翻译虽可行，却存在两个问题：生成的代码可能通过回归测试却违反源语言语义；上下文式修正无法在后续翻译中持续生效。

Method: 提出 ForTress：把反复出现的 LLM 翻译决策固化为带守卫的可执行规则，以便确定性复用；对其中非平凡子集提供语义保证。其 LLM 辅助审计流程在 Lean 中基于机械化 Fortran 与 Python 语义表达全称量化的保持性义务，由人类专家审查语义保真度及与生产规则的一致性，再由 Lean 内核在显式假设下检验证明与反例。

Result: 在来自 15 个科学软件的 17 个基准上评估，覆盖多个领域；所有测试在记录的执行与初始化配置下与原生 Fortran 逐位一致。累计 193 条可执行规则、188 条审计记录，其中 121 条具有已检验的可靠性证明，含 31 条经反例引导精化后确立；15 个软件中 14 个的跨软件复用率超过 90%；即便增加保证与确定性，LLM 使用成本仍比直接 LLM 翻译低 44.2%。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 翻译决策规则化并在 Lean 中形式化审计，可在不牺牲确定性、复用性与成本的前提下为 Fortran→Python 翻译提供可检验的语义保证。审计记录显式暴露支持正确性的条件或指出失败情形，但可靠性保证仅覆盖规则的非平凡子集，且依赖显式记录的假设。

Abstract: Modernizing mature scientific software from Fortran to Python can improve accessibility and integration with modern computing tools, but manual rewriting is expensive. Large language models (LLMs) make translation more practical, yet generated code can pass regression tests while violating source-language semantics, and contextual corrections may not persist across subsequent translations. We present ForTress, which solidifies recurring LLM translation decisions into guarded executable rules for deterministic reuse and provides semantic assurance for a non-trivial subset of these rules. Its LLM-assisted auditing process expresses universally quantified preservation obligations over mechanized Fortran and Python semantics in Lean. Human experts review semantic fidelity and correspondence with production rules, and Lean's kernel checks proofs and refutations under explicit assumptions. These audit records expose the conditions either supporting correctness or identifying failure cases. We evaluate ForTress on 17 benchmarks from 15 scientific software spanning diverse domains. The translated programs achieve bitwise-identical with native Fortran on all evaluated tests under the recorded execution and initialization configurations. Against these software, ForTress accumulates 193 executable rules and 188 audit records, of which 121 have checked soundness proofs, including 31 established after counterexample-guided refinement. Cross-software reuse exceeds 90% for 14 of the 15 software. Even with additional assurance and determinism, the LLM usage cost is 44.2% lower than direct LLM translation.

</details>


### [231] [SIEVE: A Parameterized GitHub Corpus Builder for Software Engineering Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22544)
*Musfiqur Rahman,Mahi Begoug,Emad Shihab*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SIEVE 是一个开源、参数化的 GitHub 代码语料构建工具，可在函数与方法/类粒度上按研究者指定标准提取并自动标注代码记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件工程研究依赖大规模真实代码语料，但通常用临时脚本或静态预构建数据集组装，无法控制语料组成；且已有工具多停留在仓库层级，缺少可复用、可细粒度、可定制的语料构建工具。

Method: SIEVE（Software Ingestion & Extraction for Verifiable Evaluation）在代码粒度上定位并抽取单个函数、方法（method）与类，生成 CodeSearchNet 与 OpenClassGen 兼容记录；支持按主流语言（Python、Java、JavaScript、C++）、仓库质量与创建日期等条件筛选；每条记录标注 22 项结构指标与 AST 派生特征，可选附加基于 CodeBERT 的 P(AI-generated) 分数（F1=0.9478，AUROC=0.9902）以及仓库包清单中声明的第三方依赖。

Result: 对四种语言、两种粒度的 400 条记录进行人工验证：代码指标与导入检测准确率 100%，源码完整性 99.0%，元数据准确率 99.5%。

Conclusion: SIEVE 支持结构分析、代码依赖研究、AI 生成代码评估以及（含时间约束下的）LLM 评测基准构建；工具已开源并提供在线演示（https://mrahman2025-sieve.hf.space）。

Abstract: Software engineering research increasingly relies on large, richly annotated corpora of real-world code. Still, researchers typically assemble these with ad-hoc scripts or static, pre-built datasets that offer no control over corpus composition. This leaves a gap for a reusable tool that goes below the repository level and builds annotated, function- and class-level code corpora under researcher-specified criteria. We present SIEVE (Software Ingestion & Extraction for Verifiable Evaluation), an open-source, parameterized GitHub corpus builder that, unlike repository-level mining tools, works at code granularity: it locates individual functions, methods, and classes in repositories and extracts them as records, giving direct control over corpus construction based on several criteria, including widely used programming languages (Python, Java, JavaScript, C++), repository quality, and creation date. To support software engineering research and LLM evaluation on real-world code, SIEVE extracts CodeSearchNet- and OpenClassGen-compatible records, each annotated with 22 structural metrics and AST-derived features, and optionally with a CodeBERT-based P(AI-generated) score (F1=0.9478, AUROC=0.9902), alongside the third-party dependencies declared in each repository's package manifest. These annotations enable researchers to conduct structural analyses, investigate code dependencies, assess AI-generated code, and construct customized benchmarks for evaluating LLMs, including under temporal constraints. A manual validation of 400 records in four languages and two granularities shows 100% accuracy in code metrics and import detection, 99.0% in source completeness, and 99.5% in metadata accuracy. SIEVE is publicly available with a live demo at https://mrahman2025-sieve.hf.space.

</details>


### [232] [Enjoying the Product but Not Joining the Builders: Investigating Why Students Don't Contribute to OSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22664)
*Italo Santos,Briana Lee,Derek Garcia,Yick Ching Wong,Anthony Peruma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一项针对夏威夷大学马诺阿分校计算专业学生的探索性调查研究（N=122）显示，学生对开源软件（OSS）熟悉度中等、使用频繁，但仅12.3%有主动贡献经历，主要障碍为技能差距、时间约束与代码库复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OSS参与能为学生提供真实的协作式软件开发体验，但学生的实际主动贡献仍然有限。作者希望刻画学生在认知、知识与经验层面的现状及其障碍，为课程与制度干预提供依据。

Method: 在单一院校情境下开展问卷调查，收集122份有效回答，采用定量与定性相结合的混合方法进行分析。

Result: 学生OSS熟悉度中等，在课程作业和个人项目中频繁使用OSS，但仅12.3%主动贡献，与OSS社区互动有限。非贡献者障碍：技能差距72.89%、时间约束59.81%；贡献者障碍：代码库复杂性66.67%；冒名顶替感36.45%。学生认为更多时间、导师指导、适合新手的issue、更好的入门引导与文档、课程激励（如学分）最能提升参与度；58.20%表示其课程未要求或激励OSS贡献。

Conclusion: 课程结构、教师鼓励与学业竞争性需求共同塑造了学生的OSS参与；研究为支持学生入门与参与OSS社区的有针对性课程与制度干预设计提供了洞见。研究基于单一机构情境，结论具有情境性。

Abstract: Open-source software (OSS) participation provides students with authentic exposure to collaborative, real-world software development, yet active contributions remain limited. This short paper presents an exploratory study of computing students' perceptions, knowledge, and experiences with OSS at the University of Hawai'i at Manoa. We administered a survey and analyzed 122 responses using both quantitative and qualitative methods. Students reported moderate OSS familiarity and frequent OSS use in coursework and personal projects, yet only 12.3% actively contributed and engagement with OSS communities was limited. Key barriers included skill gaps (72.89% of non-contributors), time constraints (59.81%), and codebase complexity (66.67% of contributors), often alongside imposter syndrome (36.45%). Students indicated that more time, mentorship, beginner-friendly issues, better onboarding/documentation, and curricular incentives (e.g., course credit) would most increase participation, while most reported their courses do not require or incentivize OSS contributions (58.20%). Grounded in a single institutional context, this study provides a nuanced understanding of how curriculum structure, faculty encouragement, and competing academic demands shape OSS participation. These insights inform the design of targeted curricular and institutional interventions aimed at supporting student onboarding and participation in OSS communities.

</details>


### [233] [No One-Size-Fits-All: Adaptive Code Editing with Feature-Based Strategy Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22881)
*Jun Wan,Zhongxin Liu,Dajun Chen,Wei Jiang,Yong Li,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文指出不存在通用的代码编辑（CE）策略，基于实测发现的四类任务特征与策略的关系，提出自适应代码编辑框架 Ace，动态选择最优策略，在 CEBench 上将 Pass@1 提升 21.7%、Token 消耗降低 27.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代码编辑系统普遍对所有场景固定使用单一 CE 策略（one-step、search-replace、plan-apply），而实际 CE 任务在代码上下文、编辑局部性与语义复杂度上差异显著，单一策略难以稳定最优；同时缺乏对“策略—任务”关系的系统性研究，制约了策略优化的进一步推进。

Method: 构建 CEBench（从 SWE-bench 中筛选 500 个真实 CE 任务），对比三种代表性 CE 策略，归纳策略有效性与四类核心任务特征（代码总行数、修改类型、修改范围、任务类型）的关系；据此提出 Ace：一个经微调的 LLM 作为策略选择器，从经验数据中学习特征—策略映射，并给出可解释的推荐理由（区别于分类器方法或指令提示方法）。

Result: 在 CEBench-verified 上，Ace 相较最优基线 AutoCodeRover 将 Pass@1 提升 21.7%、Token 消耗降低 27.3%；策略选择器达到 72.6% 的选择准确率与 69.9% 的理由准确率（Cohen's Kappa = 0.807）。

Conclusion: CE 策略不存在普适最优性，其效果高度依赖任务特征；通过数据驱动的自适应策略选择可同时提升编辑效果与效率，并为开发者提供透明、可信的决策依据。

Abstract: Large language models have shown strong potential in automating code editing (CE) tasks, yet most existing systems rely on a fixed CE strategy (i.e., one-step, search-replace, and plan-apply) across diverse scenarios. In practice, CE tasks vary widely in code context, edit locality, and semantic complexity, causing no single strategy to perform consistently well. Moreover, systematic studies on the relationship between strategies and CE tasks remain scarce, limiting further optimization of LLM-based code editing strategies. To address this gap, we construct CEBench, comprising 500 real-world CE tasks curated from SWE-bench. Then we compare three representative CE strategies (one-step, search-replace, plan-apply). Our study reveals two key findings: (1) each strategy exhibits distinct advantages for specific CE tasks, with no universal optimality; (2) strategy effectiveness is significantly influenced by four core task features: total lines of code, modification type, modification scope, and task type. Motivated by these findings, we propose Ace, an adaptive code editing framework that dynamically selects optimal CE strategies based on task features. Unlike classifier-based or instruction-prompting approaches, Ace employs a fine-tuned LLM that learns feature-strategy mappings from empirical data to recommend strategies with interpretable rationales. Extensive experiments on our CEBench-verified show that Ace outperforms state-of-the-art baselines: it boosts Pass@1 by 21.7% while reducing token consumption by 27.3% compared to the best baseline (AutoCodeRover). Our strategy selector achieves 72.6% selection accuracy with 69.9% rationale accuracy (Cohen's Kappa = 0.807), providing transparent and trustworthy decision-making for developers.

</details>


### [234] [MergeShuffle: Reconstruct Shared Libraries for Improved Performance and Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22952)
*Huaifeng Zhang,Ahmed Ali-Eldin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出共享库重构工具，通过合并与重排保留区间实现去膨胀，文件尺寸最多降60%、内存降16%、运行时降32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共享库去膨胀工具依赖静态/动态分析定位被使用部分，但采用"删除未用部分（需重建）"或"覆盖未用函数"两条路线；近期一项广泛研究（Brown et al.）表明部分工具反而增大静态二进制体积，且对复杂负载效果较差。作者认为在识别出所用函数后，对共享库进行恰当的重构可以提升去膨胀效果。

Method: 提出 \myapp：无需源码，输入一个共享库和需保留的文件区间集合，进行两阶段重构——(1) 合并相邻区间以降低加载开销；(2) 在保持页对齐内存映射的前提下重排区间以最小化文件体积，将重排问题建模为旅行商问题（TSP）变体并用启发式算法求解；利用程序头将保留代码重映射到原始内存地址，从而保持用户指定功能不变。

Result: 文件尺寸最多减少60%，内存占用减少16%，运行时减少32%，同时保持用户指定功能并提升安全性。

Conclusion: 基于区间合并与TSP式重排的共享库重构是提升去膨胀有效性的可行路径，可在无源码条件下同时获得体积、内存与性能收益及安全改进。

Abstract: Debloating shared libraries has been an active research topic for years. Tools for shared library debloating rely on either static or dynamic analysis techniques to discover which parts of the shared library is used. Once these parts are found, these tools take one of two routes, either removing parts of the library that are not used, requiring reconstruction of the library; or they simply overwriting the unused functions. However, recently a broad study~\cite{brown2024broad} on debloating tools has shown that some of these tools either increase the static binary size, or are generally less effective for complex workloads.
  In this paper, we argue that shared-library debloaters' performance can be improved by proper reconstruction of the shared-libraries once the used functions in the library are identifies. We propose \myapp, a novel tool for reconstructing shared libraries from a given set of file ranges to eliminate unused code based on user-specified requirements, without requiring access to source code. \myapp takes as input a shared library and a set of file ranges to retain, and performs a two-phase reconstruction: merging nearby ranges to reduce loading overhead, and shuffling them to minimize file size while preserving page-aligned memory mappings. The shuffling problem is formulated as a variant of the Traveling Salesman Problem and solved using a heuristic algorithm. \myapp utilizes program headers to remap retained code to its original memory addresses, preserving user-specified functionality. Our results show that \myapp achieves up to 60\% reduction in file size, 16\% reduction in memory usage, and 32\% reduction in runtime, while maintaining user-specified functionality and improving security.

</details>


### [235] [VulnSAGE: Adjudicating SAST Reports by Connecting LLM Reasoning to Program Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23035)
*Jinbao Chen,Zixiang Zhou,Yu Zhang,Yihong Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VulnSAGE 是一个将 LLM 推理与程序结构双向连接起来的 SAST 报告裁决系统：通过以报告为中心的程序切片获取决定性代码，并用可被程序分析校验的中间表示 CLAIM 记录推理依据，从而实现高精度的误报过滤并支撑 PoC 生成与运行时复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAST 报告标记的操作其安全性依赖项目特定上下文，维护者需人工判断哪些是真实缺陷；LLM 虽可理解上下文，但裁决所需的关键代码往往远离报告行，且缺乏可被程序分析验证的判定依据。

Method: 提出 VulnSAGE：(1) 以报告为中心的切片器（report-centered slicer）恢复被报告操作的依赖及解释这些依赖的程序结构（如 helper 作用的调用者对象）；(2) 设计中间表示 CLAIM，将模型解读记录为引用其所依赖程序关系的语义事实；(3) 程序分析校验这些前提，仅基于前提成立的语义事实得出结论；(4) 两者共享同一程序图，使模型能引用所读代码、检查器能查询这些引用；(5) 静态支持的真阳性进一步引导 PoC 生成与运行时复现。

Result: 在覆盖五类漏洞、四个语料的 187 条标注报告上，VulnSAGE 在 97 个误报中排除 89 个，在 90 个真实缺陷中保留 85 个；在统一 PoC 流程下，其正判定有 25 个可在运行时复现，而 LLM 基线裁决器 LLM4SA 仅 3 个。应用于 GitHub 星标最高的 200 个 C/C++ 项目中五个 SAST 工具产生的 45,440 条告警，运行时复现 104 个缺陷；维护者已回复的 26 个缺陷中确认 24 个，含 10 个此前未知缺陷，其中 9 个已修复。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 裁决与可校验的程序分析前提相结合，可在保持高召回的同时显著降低误报，并使判定结论可追溯、可复现，在真实开源项目中具备实际缺陷发现与修复价值。

Abstract: Static Application Security Testing (SAST) reports flag operations whose safety depends on project-specific context, and maintainers must decide which reports describe real defects. Large language models (LLMs) can interpret this context, but adjudicating a report with them requires the decisive code, which often lies far from the reported line, and a decision basis that program analysis can check. We present VulnSAGE, an adjudication system for SAST reports that connects LLM reasoning to program structure in both directions. Its report-centered slicer recovers the dependencies of the reported operation together with the program structure that explains them, such as the caller object on which a helper acts. CLAIM, an intermediate representation for report judgments, records each model interpretation as a semantic fact that cites the program relations it assumes. Program analysis checks these premises, and the verdict follows from the facts whose premises hold. Both mechanisms share a program graph, which lets the model cite the code it reads and lets the checker query those citations. Statically supported true positives then guide proof-of-concept generation and runtime reproduction. Across 187 labeled reports from four corpora covering five vulnerability families, VulnSAGE rejects 89 of 97 false alarms and retains 85 of 90 real defects. Under a common PoC procedure, 25 of its positive verdicts reproduce at runtime, compared with three for the LLM-based adjudicator LLM4SA. Applied to 45,440 alerts raised by five SAST tools on the 200 most-starred C/C++ projects on GitHub, it reproduces 104 defects at runtime. Maintainers have confirmed 24 of the 26 defects they have responded to, including ten previously unknown defects, nine of which are already fixed.

</details>


### [236] [Efficient Program Reduction for Library Fuzzing Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23054)
*Haiying Li,Yunlong Lyu,Peng Chen,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 HarnessMinimizer，一种高效最小化 C/C++ 库模糊测试 harness 的系统：通过复用头部分析结果与预构建的库/运行时、记录并重放输入值、以及避免重复栈回溯符号化的快速崩溃等价性检查，将崩溃 harness 从约 1352 token 降至 143–263 token，平均总墙钟时间较未优化流水线加速 3.46–4.51 倍，并使大模型崩溃分类中对 harness bug 的识别准确率从 28% 提升至 44–51%（库 bug 保持 100%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 库 fuzzing harness 将生成输入翻译为 API 调用；当复杂 harness 触发崩溃时，开发者需判断成因是库缺陷还是 harness 使用不当。更小的复现用例能简化该排查，但获取它需反复构建、执行修改后的 harness 并分析崩溃，人工甚至自动最小化都耗时；此外，数据依赖语句阻碍冗余代码删除，库中繁重的栈回溯符号化也会拖慢候选执行。因此需要一个高效的 harness 最小化系统。

Method: HarnessMinimizer 采用优化流水线：(1) 复用头部分析结果与已准备好的库和运行时，减少缩减过程中每次构建与执行简化 harness 的工作量；(2) 记录并重放输入值，使冗余的输入操作可被删除而不改变保留调用的取值，从而绕过数据依赖语句的阻碍；(3) 采用快速方法校验每个简化 harness 是否仍保留原始崩溃，避免重复进行栈回溯符号化。系统与四种缩减引擎结合评估。

Result: 在来自 21 个开源库的 200 个崩溃用例上，与未优化流水线相比，平均总墙钟时间加速 3.46–4.51 倍；平均 harness 规模从原始 1352 token 降至最小化后的 143–263 token；在大语言模型辅助的崩溃分诊中，识别 harness bug 的准确率从原始 harness 的 28% 提升至 44–51%，库 bug 识别准确率保持 100%。

Conclusion: HarnessMinimizer 通过构建复用、输入记录重放和免符号化崩溃等价性检查，显著提升 C/C++ 库 fuzzing harness 的最小化效率与缩减质量，并实证改善下游基于 LLM 的崩溃归因准确性，为崩溃分诊提供实用支撑。

Abstract: Library fuzzing harnesses translate generated inputs into API calls. When a complex harness triggers a crash, developers must determine whether the cause is a library defect or invalid harness usage. A smaller reproducer can simplify this investigation, but obtaining one requires repeatedly building and executing modified harnesses and analyzing their crashes, making manual or even automatic minimization time-consuming. Moreover, data-dependent statements prevent the reducer to remove unnecessary code and the heavy stack trace symbolization in libraries also slows down the candidate execution. To address these challenges, we present HarnessMinimizer, a system for efficient minimization of C/C++ library fuzzing harnesses. Its optimized pipeline reuses header analysis and prepared libraries and runtimes to reduce the work required to build and execute each simplified harness during the reduction process. It records and replays input values so that unnecessary input operations can be removed without changing the values supplied to surviving calls. It also uses a fast method to check whether each simplified harness preserves the original crash, avoiding repeated stack trace symbolization. We evaluate HarnessMinimizer with four reduction engines on 200 crash cases from 21 open source libraries. Compared with the unoptimized pipeline, HarnessMinimizer achieves speedups of 3.46-4.51 times in average total wall time. Average harness size decreases from 1,352 original tokens to 143-263 remaining tokens after minimization. In crash triage using a large language model, accuracy in identifying harness bugs increases from 28% with the original harnesses to 44-51% with the minimized harnesses, while accuracy for library bugs remains at 100%.

</details>


### [237] [Towards Agentic Proof Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23241)
*Rohith Kumar Shanmuganathan,Cedric Richter,Heike Wehrheim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"代理式证明桩（proof-harness）工程师"，自动从软件项目与指定验证目标构造可供验证器使用的证明桩，并结合形式化可靠性与精度护栏校验和精化生成的抽象，在 curl 与 libuv 的探索性案例中成功让 CBMC 检出三处历史断言违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实项目的形式化验证需要大量人工投入：既包括描述正确性属性，也包括搭建检查所需的基础设施。其中"证明桩工程"（proof-harness engineering）既基础又适合自动化，但当前仍主要由验证工程师手工完成，因此作者希望探索编码代理在此任务上的潜力。

Method: 提出一个代理式证明桩工程师：输入软件项目与指定验证目标，自动生成可供验证器使用的证明桩（提供符号输入、初始化代码与依赖模型）；并将代理与形式化的可靠性（soundness）和精度（precision）护栏结合，用以验证所生成抽象的合理性并指导其迭代精化。评估采用探索性案例研究，选取 curl 与 libuv，并以源码中已嵌入的三处历史断言违规作为验证属性。

Result: 代理成功构造出证明桩，使 CBMC 检出全部三处违规；此外还演示了所生成证明桩可复用于其他验证工具的潜力。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，编码代理可在护栏约束下自动完成证明桩工程，降低真实项目形式化验证的基础设施门槛，并具备跨验证工具复用的前景；但结论受限于小规模探索性案例，尚需更广泛评估。

Abstract: Formal verification of real-world projects requires substantial manual effort, from specifying correctness properties to constructing the infrastructure needed to check them. We envision verification agents that assist developers throughout this process. Among the many tasks involved, one stands out as both foundational and amenable to automation: proof-harness engineering. A proof harness defines an isolated verification environment for a program unit, such as a function or module, by providing symbolic inputs, initialization code, and models of its dependencies. Today, proof-harness engineering remains largely a manual task performed by verification engineers. To explore the potential of coding agents for this task, we propose an agentic proof-harness engineer that automatically constructs verifier-ready harnesses from a software project and a designated verification target. We combine the agent with formal soundness and precision guards to validate generated abstractions and guide their refinement. We evaluate the feasibility of our approach in an exploratory case study on curl and libuv, using three historical assertion violations already embedded in the source code as verification properties. Our results show that the agent successfully constructs proof harnesses that enable CBMC to detect all three violations. We further demonstrate the potential for reusing these harnesses with other verification tools.

</details>


### [238] [MLOps-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents on Production ML Engineering in Real Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23506)
*Quoc Co Tran,Hitesh Laxmichand Patel,Diwakar Mahajan,Kshitij Bakliwal,Avi Sil,Katrin Kirchhoff*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 MLOps-Bench，一个面向生产级 ML 工程（MLOps）的仓库级基准，包含 212 个来自 31 个公开 ML 仓库的已验证任务；评测 6 个智能体，最佳系统仅解决 31.2% 任务、通过 68.7% 测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么只评测竞赛式 ML 实验，要么只评测通用仓库 issue 修复，无法衡量 AI 智能体在生产 ML 仓库中执行可运行的生命周期级修改与维护的能力。

Method: 构建仓库实证驱动的基准：任务覆盖数据管道、特征工程、模型训练、模型服务、监控、CI/CD、治理及端到端集成；每个任务包含自然语言需求、oracle patch、合理的 buggy baseline、fail-to-pass 测试、pass-to-pass 测试及溯源元数据；共 212 个已验证任务、636 个 F2P 测试与 856 个 P2P 测试，并评测 6 个智能体系统。

Result: 最佳智能体解决 31.2% 的任务，测试通过率 68.7%；说明智能体常能取得部分进展，但在生产相关行为要求上仍普遍失败。

Conclusion: MLOps-Bench 将智能体评测从"模型构建"和"issue 修复"推进到仓库原生的 MLOps 工程，其正确性依赖生命周期推理、行为鲁棒性以及对既有系统行为的保持。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly expected to modify and maintain machine-learning systems in production, yet existing benchmarks primarily evaluate either competition-style ML experimentation or generic repository issue resolution. We introduce MLOps-Bench, a repository-grounded benchmark for production ML engineering. Unlike prior ML-agent benchmarks, MLOps-Bench evaluates executable lifecycle changes inside real ML repositories, spanning data pipelines, feature engineering, model training, model serving, monitoring, CI/CD and governance, and end-to-end integration. Each task is grounded in repository evidence and includes a natural-language requirement, oracle patch, plausible buggy baseline, fail-to-pass tests, pass-to-pass tests, and provenance metadata. The benchmark contains 212 validated tasks from 31 public ML repositories, with 636 fail-to-pass tests and 856 pass-to-pass tests. We evaluate six agents and find that the best system solves 31.2% of tasks while passing 68.7% of tests, showing that current agents often make partial progress but still fail production-relevant behavioral requirements. MLOps-Bench shifts agent evaluation beyond model building and issue fixing toward repository-native MLOps work where correctness depends on lifecycle reasoning, behavioral robustness, and preservation of existing system behavior.

</details>


### [239] [Recall-Oriented Precise Binary-to-Source Function Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23546)
*Junlin Li,Jianjun Huang,Wei You,Bin Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BScout通过位置信息扩展候选集并用组合感知训练与LLM筛选，提升二进制-源码函数匹配的精度与召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于嵌入的二进制-源码函数匹配方法将后续精化限制在初始检索的候选集内，若正确源码函数不在候选集中则无法恢复；同时内联(inlining)将多个源码函数合并为一个二进制函数，加剧检索难度。

Method: 提出BScout：利用已有匹配的位置信息为未解析的二进制函数补充候选，突破初始候选集；采用组合感知(composition-aware)训练处理内联组合匹配；使用大语言模型对匹配进行选择与复核，并借助位置信息过滤不一致结果。

Result: BScout在整体精度、召回率和F1上均优于BinaryAI和SBridge，分析开销可接受；在大型剥离(stripped)二进制上精度提升32%，召回约为对比方法的2.9倍。

Conclusion: BScout有效改进二进制-源码函数匹配的精度与召回，能够发现初始候选集之外的额外匹配，并可用于软件成分分析中的第三方代码复用识别。

Abstract: Binary-to-source function matching supports software composition analysis by identifying reused third-party code, but existing embedding-based approaches restrict subsequent refinement to initially retrieved candidates. When the correct source function is absent from this set, selecting or filtering the available candidates cannot recover the match. Inlining further complicates the retrieval by combining multiple source functions into one binary function. In this paper, we propose BScout to improve matching precision and uncover additional matches. BScout uses the positional information of existing matches to supplement candidates for unresolved binary functions, extending the search beyond the initial candidate set, and adopts composition-aware training to address the inlined composition matching. Large language models are adopted to select and review matches, while positional information also helps filter inconsistent results. Experiments show that BScout is effective in binary-to-source function matching. BScout achieves overall higher precision, recall and F1 scores than BinaryAI and SBridge with acceptable analysis costs. Particularly, on large stripped binaries, BScout outperforms the competitors with a precision increment of 32% and approximately 2.9 times the recall.

</details>


### [240] [An Interactive GraphRAG Workspace for Reifying, Auditing, and Steering Conversational AI Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23559)
*Munshi Md Arafat Hussain,Md. Abdur Rahman,Md Saeed Siddik*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出交互式工具 CognitiveCanvas，将对话式 LLM 的记忆由临时文本日志转化为可持久化、可操作、可审计的知识对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多会话软件开发中技术知识被割裂在孤立的线性聊天历史里，跨会话复用上下文面临三大问题：扁平向量检索无法遍历远距离架构决策间的多跳关系依赖；软件包与 API 的时序漂移导致复用旧日志时出现隐性上下文污染；原始对话拼接造成提示膨胀与注意力衰减。

Method: 集成三大组件：(1) 层次化语义事实图 HSFG，将对话蒸馏为 Goal、Fact、Code 三类工件，支持多跳 GraphRAG 遍历；(2) 持续时序漂移审计器，结合 60+ 正则启发式与基于实时日历锚定的 LLM 校验，实现一键自纠正；(3) 空间-符号提示编译器 SSPC，将二维直接操作手势（Voronoi 聚类、节点缩放、综合边）直接翻译为结构化注意力指令。

Result: HSFG 实现 92.2% 的 token 缩减，同时支持多跳 GraphRAG 遍历；审计器支持时效性校验与一键修正；SSPC 将可视化操作映射为注意力指令。

Conclusion: CognitiveCanvas 将对话记忆升级为持久、可操作、可审计的知识对象，为跨会话软件工程上下文复用提供一体化方案。

Abstract: Conversational Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely employed in software engineering for code generation, debugging, and architectural design. However, as developers engage in multi-session development tasks, technical knowledge becomes siloed in isolated, linear chat histories. Reusing context across sessions leads to three critical challenges: (1) flat-vector retrieval fails to traverse multi-hop relational dependencies between distant architectural decisions; (2) temporal drift in software packages and APIs results in silent context poisoning when reusing aged dialogue logs; and (3) raw conversation concatenation induces prompt bloat and attention degradation.
